Posterior Augmented Flow Matching
Authors: George Stoica, Sayak Paul, Matthew Wallingford, Vivek Ramanujan, Abhay Nori, Winson Han, Ali Farhadi, Ranjay Krishna, Judy Hoffman
First: 2026-05-01T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Flow matching (FM) trains a time-dependent vector field that transports samples from a simple prior to a complex data distribution. However, for high-dimensional images, each training sample supervises only a single trajectory and intermediate point, yielding an extremely sparse and high-variance training signal. This under-constrained supervision can cause flow collapse, where the learned dynamics memorize specific source-target pairings, mapping diverse inputs to overly similar outputs, failing to generalize. We introduce Posterior-Augmented Flow Matching (PAFM), a theoretically grounded generalization of FM that replaces single-target supervision with an expectation over an approximate posterior of valid target completions for a given intermediate state and condition. PAFM factorizes this intractable posterior into (i) the likelihood of the intermediate under a hypothesized endpoint and (ii) the prior probability of that endpoint under the condition, and uses an importance sampling scheme to construct a mixture over multiple candidate targets. We prove that PAFM yields an unbiased estimator of the original FM objective while substantially reducing gradient variance during training by aggregating information from many plausible continuation trajectories per intermediate. Finally, we show that PAFM improves over FM by up to 3.4 FID50K across different model scales (SiT-B/2 and SiT-XL/2), different architectures (SiT and MMDiT), and in both class and text conditioned benchmarks (ImageNet and CC12M), with a negligible increase in the compute overhead. Code: https://github.com/gstoica27/PAFM.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后验增强流匹配
流匹配（FM）训练一个随时间变化的向量场，将简单先验分布中的样本传输到复杂的数据分布。然而，对于高维图像，每个训练样本仅监督一条单一轨迹和中间点，导致训练信号极度稀疏且方差高。这种欠约束的监督可能导致流坍缩，即学习到的动态会记住特定的源-目标配对，将多样化的输入映射到过于相似的输出，无法泛化。我们提出了后验增强流匹配（PAFM），它是FM的理论基础泛化方法，用给定中间状态和条件下的近似后验目标完成的期望替代单目标监督。PAFM将该不可行后验分解为（i）在假设终点下中间状态的似然，以及（ii）该终点在条件下的先验概率，并采用重要性采样方案构建多个候选目标的混合。我们证明PAFM在保持原始FM目标无偏估计的同时，通过为每个中间状态聚合多个合理延续轨迹的信息，显著降低了训练过程中的梯度方差。最后，我们在不同模型规模（SiT-B/2和SiT-XL/2）、不同架构（SiT和MMDiT）以及类条件和文本条件基准（ImageNet和CC12M）上展示了PAFM相比FM最高提升3.4 FID50K，且计算开销增加可忽略不计。代码：https://github.com/gstoica27/PAFM.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sparse and high-variance training signals in standard flow matching for high-dimensional images, which lead to flow collapse and poor generalization, the paper proposes Posterior-Augmented Flow Matching (PAFM). PAFM replaces single-target supervision with an expectation over an approximate posterior of plausible target completions for each intermediate state, factorizing this posterior into likelihood and prior components and using importance sampling to construct a mixture of candidate trajectories. Theoretically, PAFM provides an unbiased estimator while reducing gradient variance by aggregating information across multiple continuations. Experimentally, PAFM improves upon standard flow matching by up to 3.4 FID50K across multiple model scales and architectures, with minimal additional computational cost.
为解决高维图像中标准流匹配因训练信号稀疏和高方差导致的流坍缩与泛化能力差问题，本文提出后验增强流匹配（PAFM）。PAFM将单目标监督替换为对给定中间状态条件下多个合理目标完成的后验期望，通过将后验分解为终点似然与条件先验，并采用重要性采样构建多个候选轨迹的混合。理论上，PAFM保持无偏估计同时显著降低梯度方差，通过聚合多条可能轨迹信息实现。实验表明，PAFM在不同模型规模、架构及条件类型下较标准流匹配提升最高达3.4 FID50K，且计算开销增加极小。
Sentra-Guard: A Real-Time Multilingual Defense Against Adversarial LLM Prompts
Authors: Md. Mehedi Hasan, Sk Tanzir Mehedi, Ziaur Rahman, Rafid Mostafiz, Md. Abir Hossain
First: 2025-10-26T11:19:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:57:58+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 figures. Preprint version under review in the area of Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a real-time modular defense system named Sentra-Guard. The system detects and mitigates jailbreak and prompt injection attacks targeting large language models (LLMs). The framework uses a hybrid architecture with FAISS-indexed SBERT embedding representations that capture the semantic meaning of prompts, combined with fine-tuned transformer classifiers, which are machine learning models specialized for distinguishing between benign and adversarial language inputs. It identifies adversarial prompts in both direct and obfuscated attack vectors. A core innovation is the classifier-retriever fusion module, which dynamically computes context-aware risk scores that estimate how likely a prompt is to be adversarial based on its content and context. The framework ensures multilingual resilience with a language-agnostic preprocessing layer. This component automatically translates non-English prompts into English for semantic evaluation, enabling consistent detection across over 100 languages. The system includes a HITL feedback loop, where decisions made by the automated system are reviewed by human experts for continual learning and rapid adaptation under adversarial pressure. Sentra-Guard maintains an evolving dual-labeled knowledge base of benign and malicious prompts, enhancing detection reliability and reducing false positives. Evaluation results show a 99.96% detection rate (AUC = 1.00, F1 = 1.00) and an attack success rate (ASR) of only 0.004%. This outperforms leading baselines such as LlamaGuard-2 (1.3%) and OpenAI Moderation (3.7%). Unlike black-box approaches, Sentra-Guard is transparent, fine-tunable, and compatible with diverse LLM backends. Its modular design supports scalable deployment in both commercial and open-source environments. The system establishes a new state-of-the-art in adversarial LLM defense.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sentra-Guard：一种实时多语言对抗大型语言模型提示的防御系统
本文提出了一种名为Sentra-Guard的实时模块化防御系统，用于检测和缓解针对大型语言模型（LLM）的越狱和提示注入攻击。该框架采用混合架构，结合FAISS索引的SBERT嵌入表示（捕捉提示语义）与微调的Transformer分类器（专门用于区分良性与对抗性语言输入）。系统可识别直接和混淆攻击向量中的对抗性提示。核心创新是分类器-检索器融合模块，动态计算上下文感知的风险评分，评估提示语句的对抗可能性。框架通过语言无关预处理层实现多语言鲁棒性，自动将非英语提示翻译为英语进行语义评估，支持100多种语言的统一检测。系统包含人机协同反馈循环，由人类专家审核自动化决策，实现持续学习与对抗压力下的快速适应。系统维护一个不断演化的良性与恶意提示双标签知识库，提升检测可靠性并降低误报率。评估结果显示检测准确率达99.96%（AUC=1.00，F1=1.00），攻击成功率（ASR）仅为0.004%，显著优于LlamaGuard-2（1.3%）和OpenAI Moderation（3.7%）。与黑盒方法不同，Sentra-Guard透明、可微调，兼容多种LLM后端。其模块化设计支持在商业与开源环境中可扩展部署，确立了对抗LLM防御的新基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing threat of adversarial attacks on large language models, this paper introduces Sentra-Guard, a real-time, multilingual defense system that combines FAISS-indexed SBERT embeddings with fine-tuned transformer classifiers to detect jailbreak and prompt injection attacks. Its core innovation is a classifier-retriever fusion module that computes context-aware risk scores to assess adversarial likelihood, while a language-agnostic preprocessing layer translates non-English prompts into English for consistent semantic evaluation across 100+ languages. Evaluated against leading baselines, Sentra-Guard achieves a 99.96% detection rate (AUC = 1.00, F1 = 1.00) and an attack success rate of just 0.004%, outperforming LlamaGuard-2 (1.3%) and OpenAI Moderation (3.7%). The system also features a human-in-the-loop feedback loop and an evolving dual-labeled knowledge base to reduce false positives and adapt to new threats, making it transparent, fine-tunable, and compatible with diverse LLM backends.
为应对大型语言模型面临的日益严重的对抗攻击威胁，Sentra-Guard提出了一种实时模块化防御系统，结合FAISS索引的SBERT嵌入与微调的Transformer分类器，用于检测越狱和提示注入攻击。其核心创新是分类器-检索器融合模块，可计算上下文感知的风险评分以评估提示的对抗可能性，同时通过语言无关预处理层将非英语提示翻译为英语，实现对100多种语言的统一检测。实验结果显示，该系统在99.96%的检测率（AUC=1.00，F1=1.00）和仅0.004%的攻击成功率下，显著优于LlamaGuard-2和OpenAI Moderation等基线方法。系统还引入人机协同反馈机制与持续更新的双标签数据集，以降低误报并适应新威胁，具备透明、可微调及兼容多种LLM后端的特性。
HyCOP: Hybrid Composition Operators for Interpretable Learning of PDEs
Authors: Jinpai Zhao, Nishant Panda, Yen Ting Lin, Eirik Valseth, Diane Oyen, Clint Dawson
First: 2026-05-01T17:57:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:57:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce HyCOP, a modular framework that learns parametric PDE solution operators by composing simple modules (advection, diffusion, learned closures, boundary handling) in a query-conditioned way. Rather than learning a monolithic map, HyCOP learns a policy over short programs - which module to apply and for how long - conditioned on regime features and state statistics. Modules may be numerical sub-solvers or learned components, enabling hybrid surrogates evaluated at arbitrary query times without autoregressive rollout. Across diverse PDE benchmarks, HyCOP produces interpretable programs, delivers order-of-magnitude OOD improvements over monolithic neural operators, and supports modular transfer through dictionary updates (e.g., boundary swaps, residual enrichment). Our theory characterizes expressivity and gives an error decomposition that separates composition error from module error and doubles as a process-level diagnostic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HyCOP：用于可解释PDE学习的混合组合算子
我们提出了HyCOP，一个模块化框架，通过在查询条件下组合简单模块（对流、扩散、学习闭合项、边界处理）来学习参数化PDE解算子。与学习单一映射不同，HyCOP学习一个策略，根据环境特征和状态统计决定应用哪个模块以及持续多久。模块可以是数值子求解器或学习组件，从而支持在任意查询时间评估混合代理，无需自回归展开。在多种PDE基准测试中，HyCOP生成可解释的程序，相比单体神经算子在OOD场景下性能提升数量级，并支持通过字典更新实现模块化迁移（如边界交换、残差增强）。我们的理论刻画了表达能力，并给出误差分解，将组合误差与模块误差分离，同时作为过程级诊断工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and transferable PDE solvers, HyCOP introduces a modular framework that composes simple physical modules—such as advection, diffusion, and learned closures—in a query-conditioned manner to learn parametric PDE solution operators. Instead of learning a single monolithic neural operator, HyCOP learns a policy that selects which module to apply and for how long, based on regime features and state statistics. This enables hybrid surrogates that can be evaluated at arbitrary query times without autoregressive rollout. Experimental results across diverse PDE benchmarks show HyCOP generates interpretable programs, achieves order-of-magnitude improvements over monolithic neural operators on out-of-distribution data, and supports modular transfer via dictionary updates such as boundary swaps or residual enrichment.
为实现可解释且可迁移的偏微分方程求解器，HyCOP提出一种模块化框架，通过在查询条件下组合简单的物理模块（如对流、扩散、学习闭合项）来学习参数化PDE解算算子。与学习单个整体神经算子不同，HyCOP学习一个策略，根据环境特征和状态统计决定应用哪个模块以及持续多久。该方法支持在任意查询时间点评估混合代理，无需自回归展开。在多个PDE基准测试中，HyCOP生成可解释的程序，相比单体神经算子在分布外性能上提升数量级，并可通过字典更新（如边界交换或残差增强）实现模块化迁移。
ATLAS: Adaptive Trading with LLM AgentS Through Dynamic Prompt Optimization and Multi-Agent Coordination
Authors: Charidimos Papadakis, Angeliki Dimitriou, Giorgos Filandrianos, Maria Lymperaiou, Konstantinos Thomas, Giorgos Stamou
First: 2025-10-10T13:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:56:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models show promise for financial decision-making, yet deploying them as autonomous trading agents raises fundamental challenges: how to adapt instructions when rewards arrive late and obscured by market noise, how to synthesize heterogeneous information streams into coherent decisions, and how to bridge the gap between model outputs and executable market actions. We present ATLAS (Adaptive Trading with LLM AgentS), a unified multi-agent framework that integrates structured information from markets, news, and corporate fundamentals to support robust trading decisions. Within ATLAS, the central trading agent operates in an order-aware action space, ensuring that outputs correspond to executable market orders rather than abstract signals. The agent can incorporate feedback while trading using Adaptive-OPRO, a novel prompt-optimization technique that dynamically adapts the prompt by incorporating real-time, stochastic feedback, leading to increasing performance over time. Across regime-specific equity studies and multiple LLM families, Adaptive-OPRO consistently outperforms fixed prompts, while reflection-based feedback fails to provide systematic gains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATLAS：通过动态提示优化与多智能体协同实现的自适应交易
大型语言模型在金融决策中展现出潜力，但将其部署为自主交易代理面临根本性挑战：如何在奖励延迟且被市场噪音掩盖时调整指令，如何将异构信息流整合为连贯决策，以及如何弥合模型输出与可执行市场操作之间的鸿沟。我们提出ATLAS（自适应交易与LLM智能体），一个统一的多智能体框架，整合市场、新闻和公司基本面的结构化信息以支持稳健交易决策。在ATLAS中，中央交易智能体在订单感知的动作空间中运行，确保输出对应可执行的市场订单而非抽象信号。该智能体可借助Adaptive-OPRO（一种新颖的提示优化技术）在交易中融入反馈，该技术通过实时、随机反馈动态调整提示，从而随时间提升性能。在不同市场环境下的股票研究及多个LLM家族中，Adaptive-OPRO始终优于固定提示，而基于反思的反馈未能提供系统性增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy large language models as autonomous trading agents despite challenges like delayed rewards and noisy market signals, ATLAS introduces a multi-agent framework that integrates market, news, and fundamental data into structured trading decisions. The central agent operates in an order-aware space to ensure executable actions and employs Adaptive-OPRO, a prompt-optimization method that dynamically refines instructions using real-time stochastic feedback. Experimental results across multiple LLM families and equity regimes show that Adaptive-OPRO consistently outperforms static prompts, while reflection-based feedback does not yield consistent improvements.
为应对大语言模型在金融交易中部署时面临的延迟奖励与市场噪声干扰等挑战，ATLAS提出一个整合市场、新闻与基本面数据的多智能体框架，其核心交易智能体在订单感知动作空间中执行可执行交易指令。该框架采用Adaptive-OPRO动态提示优化技术，通过实时随机反馈持续调整提示，从而提升性能。在多个LLM家族和股票市场环境下，Adaptive-OPRO表现稳定优于固定提示，而基于反思的反馈机制未能带来系统性收益。
When LLMs Stop Following Steps: A Diagnostic Study of Procedural Execution in Language Models
Authors: Sailesh Panda, Pritam Kadasi, Abhishek Upperwal, Mayank Singh
First: 2026-05-01T17:55:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:55:47+00:00
Comments: 77 pages, 109 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong performance on reasoning benchmarks, but final-answer accuracy alone does not show whether they faithfully execute the procedure specified in a prompt. We study this question through a controlled diagnostic benchmark for procedural execution, where models are given a step-wise arithmetic algorithm and two numeric inputs, and must return the final computed value. The benchmark uses simple arithmetic operations but increases complexity through algorithm length and look-back dependencies over intermediate variables. Across 14 models and 55 datasets, average first-answer accuracy drops from 61% on 5-step procedures to 20% on 95-step procedures. Generation-level analysis shows that failures often involve missing answers, premature answers, self-correction after an initial error, under-executed traces, and hallucinated extra steps. These findings suggest that apparent reasoning ability can mask substantial weaknesses in faithful instruction execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当大语言模型停止遵循步骤：语言模型程序执行的诊断研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）通常在推理基准测试中表现优异，但仅凭最终答案的准确性无法判断它们是否忠实执行了提示中指定的程序。我们通过一个受控的程序执行诊断基准进行研究，模型被给予一个分步算术算法和两个数值输入，必须返回最终计算值。该基准使用简单算术运算，但通过算法长度和对中间变量的回溯依赖性增加复杂性。在14个模型和55个数据集上，平均首次答案准确率从5步程序的61%降至95步程序的20%。生成级分析显示，失败通常涉及遗漏答案、过早回答、初始错误后的自我修正、执行不完整和虚构额外步骤。这些发现表明，表面的推理能力可能掩盖了忠实指令执行方面的重大缺陷。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models faithfully execute multi-step procedures as instructed, motivated by the observation that high final-answer accuracy does not guarantee correct procedural adherence. The researchers designed a controlled diagnostic benchmark using step-wise arithmetic algorithms with increasing complexity, including variable dependencies and algorithm length, to evaluate model behavior. Across 14 models and 55 datasets, performance dropped sharply from 61% to 20% accuracy as procedure length increased from 5 to 95 steps. Analysis revealed common failure modes such as missing or premature answers, self-correction after errors, incomplete execution traces, and hallucinated steps, indicating that apparent reasoning ability may mask significant flaws in following instructions step-by-step.
该研究旨在探究大语言模型是否能忠实执行多步骤指令，动机在于发现高最终答案准确率并不意味着模型能正确遵循步骤。研究设计了一个控制性诊断基准，使用逐步算术算法，通过增加步骤长度和中间变量回溯依赖来提升复杂度。在14个模型和55个数据集上，当步骤从5步增至95步时，平均首次答案准确率从61%骤降至20%。分析发现常见失败模式包括漏答、过早输出、错误后自我修正、执行不完整和虚构步骤，表明模型看似具备推理能力，实则在忠实执行指令方面存在严重缺陷。
Persistent Visual Memory: Sustaining Perception for Deep Generation in LVLMs
Authors: Siyuan Huang, Xiaoye Qu, Yafu Li, Tong Zhu, Zefeng He, Muxin Fu, Daizong Liu, Wei-Long Zheng, Yu Cheng
First: 2026-05-01T17:54:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:54:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While autoregressive Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate remarkable proficiency in multimodal tasks, they face a "Visual Signal Dilution" phenomenon, where the accumulation of textual history expands the attention partition function, causing visual attention to decay inversely with generated sequence length. To counteract this, we propose Persistent Visual Memory (PVM), a lightweight learnable module designed to ensure sustained, on-demand visual perception. Integrated as a parallel branch alongside the Feed-Forward Network (FFN) in LVLMs, PVM establishes a distance-agnostic retrieval pathway that directly provides visual embeddings for precise visual perception, thereby structurally mitigating the signal suppression inherent to deep generation. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL models demonstrate that PVM brings notable improvements with negligible parameter overhead, delivering consistent average accuracy gains across both 4B and 8B scales, particularly in complex reasoning tasks that demand persistent visual perception. Furthermore, in-depth analysis reveals that PVM can resist length-induced signal decay and accelerate internal prediction convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：持久视觉记忆：为LVLMs深度生成提供持续感知
尽管自回归大视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态任务中表现出色，但其面临“视觉信号稀释”现象：文本历史的累积扩大了注意力分配函数，导致视觉注意力随生成序列长度增加而衰减。为此，我们提出持久视觉记忆（PVM），一种轻量级可学习模块，旨在确保按需持续的视觉感知。PVM作为并行分支集成于LVLMs的前馈网络（FFN）中，建立无距离依赖的检索路径，直接提供视觉嵌入以实现精准视觉感知，从而结构上缓解深度生成固有的信号抑制问题。在Qwen3-VL模型上的大量实验表明，PVM带来显著性能提升且参数开销极小，在4B和8B规模下均实现稳定平均准确率提升，尤其在需要持续视觉感知的复杂推理任务中表现突出。深入分析还表明，PVM可抵抗长度诱导的信号衰减并加速内部预测收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the visual signal dilution issue in autoregressive LVLMs, where visual attention decays with longer text generation, the paper introduces Persistent Visual Memory (PVM), a lightweight learnable module integrated in parallel with the FFN. PVM provides on-demand visual embeddings via a distance-agnostic retrieval mechanism, bypassing attention decay and enabling sustained perception. Experiments on Qwen3-VL models show PVM delivers consistent accuracy gains across 4B and 8B scales, especially in complex reasoning tasks, with minimal parameter overhead and improved convergence.
为解决自回归LVLM中因文本历史累积导致的视觉信号衰减问题，作者提出轻量级可学习模块Persistent Visual Memory（PVM），并将其与FFN并行集成。PVM通过距离无关的检索机制直接提供视觉嵌入，绕过注意力衰减，实现持续视觉感知。在Qwen3-VL模型上的实验表明，PVM在4B和8B规模下均带来显著准确率提升，尤其在复杂推理任务中表现突出，且参数开销极小，同时加速内部预测收敛。
Let ViT Speak: Generative Language-Image Pre-training
Authors: Yan Fang, Mengcheng Lan, Zilong Huang, Weixian Lei, Yunqing Zhao, Yujie Zhong, Yingchen Yu, Qi She, Yao Zhao, Yunchao Wei
First: 2026-05-01T17:51:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:51:38+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we present \textbf{Gen}erative \textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{I}mage \textbf{P}re-training (GenLIP), a minimalist generative pretraining framework for Vision Transformers (ViTs) designed for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To better align vision encoders with the autoregressive nature of LLMs, GenLIP trains a ViT to predict language tokens directly from visual tokens using a standard language modeling objective, without contrastive batch construction or an additional text decoder. This design offers three key advantages: (1) \textbf{Simplicity}: a single transformer jointly models visual and textual tokens; (2) \textbf{Scalability}: it scales effectively with both data and model size; and (3) \textbf{Performance}: it achieves competitive or superior results across diverse multimodal benchmarks. Trained on 8B samples from Recap-DataComp-1B, GenLIP matches or surpasses strong baselines despite using substantially less pretraining data. After continued pretraining on multi-resolution images at native aspect ratios, GenLIP further improves on detail-sensitive tasks such as OCR and chart understanding, making it a strong foundation for vision encoders in MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：让ViT开口说话：生成式语言-图像预训练
本文提出生成式语言-图像预训练（GenLIP），这是一种为视觉Transformer（ViT）设计的极简生成式预训练框架，旨在服务于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）。为更好地使视觉编码器与大语言模型的自回归特性对齐，GenLIP训练ViT直接从视觉token预测语言token，采用标准语言建模目标，无需对比批次构建或额外文本解码器。该设计具有三大优势：（1）简洁性：单个Transformer联合建模视觉与文本token；（2）可扩展性：在数据和模型规模上均能有效扩展；（3）性能：在多种多模态基准测试中达到竞争性或更优结果。在Recap-DataComp-1B数据集的80亿样本上训练后，GenLIP即使使用远少于基线的预训练数据，仍可达到或超越强基线性能。在继续使用原纵横比多分辨率图像进行预训练后，GenLIP在对细节敏感的任务（如OCR和图表理解）上进一步提升，使其成为MLLMs中视觉编码器的强大基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and scalable vision-language pretraining, GenLIP introduces a minimalist generative framework that trains Vision Transformers to predict language tokens directly from visual tokens using a standard language modeling objective, without contrastive learning or extra decoders. This unified transformer architecture enables simplicity, scalability, and strong performance across multimodal benchmarks. Evaluated on diverse tasks, GenLIP achieves competitive or superior results even with significantly less pretraining data than prior methods, and further improves on detail-sensitive tasks like OCR and chart understanding after multi-resolution pretraining.
为提升多模态大语言模型中视觉语言预训练的效率与可扩展性，GenLIP提出一种极简生成式框架，通过标准语言建模目标直接训练视觉Transformer预测语言标记，无需对比学习或额外文本解码器，实现视觉与文本标记的统一建模。实验表明，即使预训练数据量远少于现有方法，GenLIP在多个多模态基准上表现竞争力或更优，并在多分辨率图像预训练后，于OCR和图表理解等细节敏感任务上进一步提升性能。
Comparing Exploration-Exploitation Strategies of LLMs and Humans: Insights from Standard Multi-armed Bandit Experiments
Authors: Ziyuan Zhang, Darcy Wang, Ningyuan Chen, Rodrigo Mansur, Vahid Sarhangian
First: 2025-05-15T02:09:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:47:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate or automate human behavior in complex sequential decision-making settings. A natural question is then whether LLMs exhibit similar decision-making behavior to humans, and can achieve comparable (or superior) performance. In this work, we focus on the exploration-exploitation (E&E) tradeoff, a fundamental aspect of dynamic decision-making under uncertainty. We employ canonical multi-armed bandit (MAB) experiments introduced in the cognitive science and psychiatry literature to conduct a comparative study of the E&E strategies of LLMs, humans, and MAB algorithms. We use interpretable choice models to capture the E&E strategies of the agents and investigate how enabling thinking traces, through both prompting strategies and thinking models, shapes LLM decision-making. We find that enabling thinking in LLMs shifts their behavior toward more human-like behavior, characterized by a mix of random and directed exploration. In a simple stationary setting, thinking-enabled LLMs exhibit similar levels of random and directed exploration compared to humans. However, in more complex, non-stationary environments, LLMs struggle to match human adaptability, particularly in effective directed exploration, despite achieving similar regret in certain scenarios. Our findings highlight both the promise and limits of LLMs as simulators of human behavior and tools for automated decision-making and point to potential areas for improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型与人类在探索-利用策略上的比较：来自标准多臂赌博机实验的启示
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于模拟或自动化复杂序贯决策环境中的人类行为。一个自然的问题是，LLMs是否表现出与人类相似的决策行为，并能实现可比（或更优）的表现。本研究聚焦于探索-利用（E&E）权衡，这是不确定性下动态决策的基本方面。我们采用认知科学和精神病学文献中引入的经典多臂赌博机（MAB）实验，对LLMs、人类和MAB算法的E&E策略进行比较研究。我们使用可解释的选择模型来捕捉代理人的E&E策略，并探讨通过提示策略和思维模型启用思维痕迹如何塑造LLMs的决策行为。我们发现，启用思维功能可使LLMs的行为更趋近人类，表现为随机探索与定向探索的混合。在简单静态环境中，启用思维的LLMs在随机探索与定向探索水平上与人类相似。但在更复杂的非静态环境中，LLMs难以匹配人类的适应能力，特别是在定向探索的有效性方面，尽管在某些场景中其遗憾值与人类相当。我们的研究结果既突出了LLMs作为人类行为模拟器和自动化决策工具的潜力，也指出了其局限性，并指明了潜在的改进方向。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) mimic human decision-making in exploration-exploitation tradeoffs using standard multi-armed bandit experiments. By applying interpretable choice models and prompting strategies that enable 'thinking traces', researchers compare LLMs, humans, and classical MAB algorithms. Results show that thinking-enabled LLMs adopt more human-like behavior in stationary environments, exhibiting balanced random and directed exploration. However, in non-stationary settings, LLMs fail to match human adaptability in directed exploration, despite achieving comparable regret in some cases, revealing both potential and limitations in using LLMs as human behavior simulators or automated decision tools.
本研究通过标准多臂赌博机实验，比较大型语言模型（LLM）、人类与经典MAB算法在探索-利用权衡中的决策行为。作者采用可解释的选择模型量化各主体的策略，并测试提示与思维模型对LLM决策的影响。结果表明，启用思维机制后，LLM的决策更趋近人类模式，在静态环境中其随机与定向探索水平与人类相当；但在非静态环境中，LLM在定向探索的适应性上仍不及人类，尽管在部分场景下其遗憾值与人类相近，揭示了LLM作为人类行为模拟器和自动化决策工具的潜力与局限。
Discrete Cosine Transform Based Decorrelated Attention for Vision Transformers
Authors: Hongyi Pan, Emadeldeen Hamdan, Xin Zhu, Ahmet Enis Cetin, Ulas Bagci
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2024-05-22T18:15:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:47:13+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted to IJCAI-ECAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-attention is central to the success of Transformer architectures; however, learning the query, key, and value projections from random initialization remains challenging and computationally expensive. In this paper, we propose two complementary methods that leverage the Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) to enhance the efficiency and performance of Vision Transformers. First, we address the initialization problem by introducing a simple yet effective DCT-based initialization strategy for self-attention, where projection weights are initialized using DCT coefficients. This structure-preserving approach consistently improves classification accuracy on the CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-1K benchmarks. Second, we propose a DCT-based attention compression technique that exploits the decorrelation properties of the frequency domain. By observing that high-frequency DCT coefficients typically correspond to noise, we truncate high-frequency components of the input patches, thereby reducing the dimensionality of the query, key, and value projections without sacrificing accuracy. Experiments on Swin Transformer models demonstrate that the proposed compression method achieves a substantial reduction in computational overhead while maintaining comparable performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于离散余弦变换的去相关注意力机制用于视觉Transformer
自注意力机制是Transformer架构成功的核心；然而，从随机初始化学习查询、键和值投影仍具挑战性且计算成本高昂。本文提出两种互补方法，利用离散余弦变换（DCT）提升视觉Transformer的效率与性能。首先，我们通过引入基于DCT的初始化策略解决初始化问题，即使用DCT系数初始化投影权重，该结构保持方法在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-1K基准上持续提升分类准确率。其次，我们提出一种基于DCT的注意力压缩技术，利用频域的去相关特性。通过观察高频DCT系数通常对应噪声，我们截断输入块的高频成分，从而在不牺牲精度的前提下降低查询、键和值投影的维度。在Swin Transformer模型上的实验表明，所提压缩方法显著降低计算开销，同时保持相近性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational burden and initialization challenges in Vision Transformers, this paper introduces two DCT-based methods: a decorrelation-aware attention mechanism and an initialization strategy using DCT coefficients. The first method initializes query, key, and value projections via DCT, improving classification accuracy on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-1K. The second method compresses attention by truncating high-frequency DCT components, which correspond to noise, thereby reducing computational load without degrading performance. Experiments on Swin Transformer models show significant efficiency gains while preserving accuracy.
为解决视觉Transformer中自注意力机制的初始化困难与计算开销问题，本文提出两种基于离散余弦变换（DCT）的方法：一种是利用DCT系数初始化投影权重的结构保持初始化策略，另一种是通过截断输入块的高频DCT分量压缩注意力维度。实验表明，两种方法在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-1K上均提升分类准确率，同时大幅降低计算开销，压缩方法在Swin Transformer模型中尤其显著地提升了效率而不牺牲性能。
Prop-Chromeleon: Adaptive Haptic Props in Mixed Reality through Generative Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Haoyu Wang, Fengyuan Zhu, Bingjian Huang, Zhecheng Wang, Ludwig Sidenmark
First: 2026-05-01T17:45:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:45:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACM DIS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixed Reality (MR) aims to blend digital and physical worlds, but the absence of haptic feedback often breaks visual-tactile consistency. We introduce Prop-Chromeleon, a MR system based on generative artificial intelligence (AI) that dynamically transforms everyday objects into adaptive passive haptic props through user-provided text prompts. Our AI pipeline performs generation and anchoring of virtual assets that align with the shape of physical props, allowing us to study how virtual content generation behaves under geometric and prompt-based constraints. We evaluate Prop-Chromeleon's effectiveness through a generation study using varied object shapes and user prompts, combining quantitative shape similarity metrics with qualitative prompt fidelity analysis. Our user study further showcases Prop-Chromeleon's improvements in perceived realism, immersion, and enjoyment compared to static baselines. These results show that shape-aware generation can support both believable haptic interaction and creative engagement in MR.
中文标题/摘要

混合现实（MR）旨在融合数字与物理世界，但缺乏触觉反馈常破坏视觉与触觉的一致性。我们提出Prop-Chromeleon，一种基于生成式人工智能（AI）的MR系统，通过用户提供的文本提示，将日常物品动态转化为自适应被动触觉道具。我们的AI流程生成并锚定与物理道具形状对齐的虚拟资产，使我们得以研究虚拟内容生成在几何与提示约束下的行为。我们通过使用不同物体形状和用户提示的生成研究评估Prop-Chromeleon的有效性，结合定量形状相似性度量与定性提示保真度分析。用户研究进一步展示了Prop-Chromeleon相比静态基线在感知真实感、沉浸感和愉悦度方面的改进。结果表明，形状感知生成可支持MR中的可信触觉交互与创意参与。
