The system generates and anchors virtual assets that conform to the physical object’s geometry, enabling shape-aware content creation under prompt and geometric constraints. Experimental results demonstrate high shape similarity and prompt fidelity, alongside user-reported improvements in perceived realism, immersion, and enjoyment compared to static baselines, validating the system’s ability to enhance both haptic believability and creative engagement in MR.
为解决混合现实环境中触觉反馈缺失导致的视觉-触觉不一致问题，Prop-Chromeleon 利用生成式人工智能，通过用户文本提示将日常物理物体动态转化为自适应触觉道具。系统生成并锚定与物体几何形状匹配的虚拟资产，支持在几何与提示约束下进行内容生成。实验表明，该系统在形状相似度与提示忠实度方面表现优异，用户感知到的现实感、沉浸感和愉悦度显著优于静态基线，验证了其在增强混合现实触觉真实性和创意互动方面的有效性。
PPLLaVA: Varied Video Sequence Understanding With Prompt Guidance
Authors: Shangkun Sun, Ruyang Liu, Haoran Tang, Yixiao Ge, Haibo Lu, Wei Gao, Jiankun Yang, Chen Li
Venue: ICLR
First: 2024-11-04T17:50:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:44:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR' 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In the past year, video-based large language models (Video LLMs) have achieved impressive progress, particularly in their ability to process long videos through extremely extended context lengths. However, this comes at the cost of significantly increased computational overhead due to the massive number of visual tokens, making efficiency a major bottleneck. In this paper, we identify the root of this inefficiency as the high redundancy in video content. To address this, we propose a novel pooling strategy that enables aggressive token compression while retaining instruction-relevant visual semantics. Our model, Prompt-guided Pooling LLaVA (PPLLaVA), introduces three key components: a CLIP-based visual-prompt alignment module that identifies regions of interest based on user instructions, a prompt-guided pooling mechanism that adaptively compresses the visual sequence using convolution-style pooling, and a clip context extension module tailored for processing long and complex prompts in visual dialogues. With up to 18x token reduction, PPLLaVA maintains strong performance across tasks, achieving state-of-the-art results on diverse video understanding benchmarks-ranging from image-to-video tasks such as captioning and QA to long-form video reasoning-while significantly improving inference throughput. Codes have been available at https://github.com/farewellthree/PPLLaVA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PPLLaVA：基于提示引导的多样化视频序列理解
过去一年中，基于视频的大语言模型（Video LLMs）在处理长视频方面取得了显著进展，尤其得益于极长上下文长度的支持。然而，由于大量视觉token导致计算开销大幅增加，效率成为主要瓶颈。本文将效率低下根源归结为视频内容的高冗余性。为此，我们提出一种新颖的池化策略，在保留与指令相关的视觉语义的同时实现激进的token压缩。我们的模型Prompt-guided Pooling LLaVA（PPLLaVA）包含三个关键组件：基于CLIP的视觉提示对齐模块，用于根据用户指令识别感兴趣区域；提示引导的池化机制，通过卷积式池化自适应压缩视觉序列；以及针对视觉对话中长复杂提示的CLIP上下文扩展模块。PPLLaVA最多可实现18倍token压缩，在各类任务中保持强劲性能，于多样化的视频理解基准测试中取得最先进结果——涵盖从图像到视频的任务（如字幕生成与问答）到长视频推理，并显著提升推理吞吐量。代码已开源：https://github.com/farewellthree/PPLLaVA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of video-based large language models caused by redundant visual tokens, this paper proposes PPLLaVA, a prompt-guided pooling framework that compresses video sequences while preserving instruction-relevant visual semantics. It integrates a CLIP-based visual-prompt alignment module to locate relevant regions, a convolution-style prompt-guided pooling mechanism for adaptive token reduction, and a clip context extension module for handling long prompts. Experiments show that PPLLaVA achieves up to 18x token reduction without sacrificing performance, outperforming existing methods on multiple video understanding benchmarks including captioning, QA, and long-form reasoning, while significantly boosting inference speed.
为解决视频大语言模型因视觉token冗余导致的计算效率瓶颈，本文提出PPLLaVA框架，通过提示引导的池化策略实现视频序列压缩并保留指令相关视觉语义。该方法包含基于CLIP的视觉-提示对齐模块以定位关键区域，卷积风格的提示引导池化机制实现自适应压缩，以及针对长提示视觉对话优化的上下文扩展模块。实验表明，该方法可实现最高18倍的token压缩，同时在包括视频描述、问答和长视频推理等多任务上保持领先性能，并显著提升推理效率。
Can Coding Agents Reproduce Findings in Computational Materials Science?
Authors: Ziyang Huang, Yi Cao, Ali K. Shargh, Jing Luo, Ruidong Mei, Mohd Zaki, Zhan Liu, Wyatt Bunstine, William Jurayj, Somdatta Goswami, Tyrel McQueen, Michael Shields, Jaafar El-Awady, Paulette Clancy, Benjamin Van Durme, Nicholas Andrews, William Walden, Daniel Khashabi
First: 2026-05-01T17:42:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:42:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous coding agents and have achieved remarkably strong performance on software engineering benchmarks. However, it is unclear whether such success transfers to computational scientific workflows, where tasks require not only strong coding ability, but also the ability to navigate complex, domain-specific procedures and to interpret results in the context of scientific claims. To address this question, we present AutoMat, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents' ability to reproduce claims from computational materials science. AutoMat poses three interrelated challenges: recovering underspecified computational procedures, navigating specialized toolchains, and determining whether the resulting evidence supports a claim. By working closely with subject matter experts, we curate a set of claims from real materials science papers to test whether coding agents can recover and execute the end-to-end workflow needed to support (or undermine) such claims. We then evaluate multiple representative coding agent settings across several foundation models. Our results show that current LLM-based agents obtain low overall success rates on AutoMat, with the best-performing setting achieving a success rate of only 54.1%. Error analysis further reveals that agents perform worst when workflows must be reconstructed from paper text alone and that they fail primarily due to incomplete procedures, methodological deviations, and execution fragility. Taken together, these findings position AutoMat as both a benchmark for computational scientific reproducibility and a tool for diagnosing the current limitations of agentic systems in AI-for-science settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编码代理能否在计算材料科学中重现研究结果？
大型语言模型正越来越多地作为自主编码代理部署，并在软件工程基准测试中表现出卓越性能。然而，尚不清楚这种成功能否迁移到计算科学工作流中，因为这些任务不仅需要强大的编码能力，还需要在复杂、特定领域的程序中导航，并在科学主张的背景下解释结果。为回答这一问题，我们提出了AutoMat，一个用于评估基于LLM的代理能否重现计算材料科学中主张的基准。AutoMat包含三个相互关联的挑战：恢复未明确指定的计算程序、导航专用工具链，以及判断所得证据是否支持主张。通过与领域专家紧密合作，我们精选了真实材料科学论文中的若干主张，以检验编码代理能否恢复并执行支持（或反驳）这些主张所需的端到端工作流。随后，我们在多个基础模型上评估了多种代表性编码代理设置。结果表明，当前基于LLM的代理在AutoMat上的整体成功率较低，表现最佳的设置仅达到54.1%的成功率。错误分析进一步表明，当工作流必须仅从论文文本重建时，代理表现最差，其失败主要源于程序不完整、方法偏差和执行脆弱性。综合来看，这些发现使AutoMat成为计算科学可重复性评估的基准，同时也是诊断当前AI for Science场景中代理系统局限性的工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether large language models can reliably reproduce scientific findings in computational materials science, the authors introduce AutoMat, a benchmark designed to test LLM-based coding agents on end-to-end workflow reproduction. The method involves curating real claims from materials science papers and requiring agents to recover computational procedures, navigate specialized toolchains, and validate results against scientific assertions. Experimental evaluation across multiple agent configurations and foundation models reveals low overall success rates, with the top-performing setup achieving only 54.1% success. Key failures stem from incomplete procedure reconstruction, methodological deviations, and fragile execution, indicating current agents struggle with domain-specific scientific workflows.
为评估大语言模型是否能在计算材料科学领域可靠复现科学发现，作者设计了AutoMat基准测试，要求基于LLM的编码代理完成从论文中恢复计算流程、调用专业工具链并验证结果是否支持科学主张的全流程。实验在多个代理配置和基础模型上进行，结果显示最高成功率仅为54.1%，主要失败原因包括流程重建不完整、方法学偏差和执行脆弱性，表明当前代理在领域特定科学工作流中仍存在显著局限。
Adaptive Node Feature Selection For Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Ali Azizpour, Madeline Navarro, Santiago Segarra
First: 2025-10-03T15:26:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:39:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose an adaptive node feature selection approach for graph neural networks (GNNs) that identifies and removes unnecessary features during training. The ability to measure how features contribute to model output is key for interpreting decisions and reducing dimensionality by eliminating unhelpful variables. However, graph-structured data introduces complex dependencies that may be unsuited to classical feature importance metrics. Inspired by this, we present a data-, model-, and task-agnostic method that determines relevant features during training based on changes in validation performance upon permuting feature values. We theoretically motivate our approach by characterizing how the relationships between node data and graph structure influences GNN performance. Empirically, we show that (i) our highly general approach rivals the performance of tailored feature selection approaches that exploit prior assumptions; (ii) we return meaningful feature importance scores well before the GNN is fully trained; and (iii) our scores demonstrably extract relevant properties that inform feature importance for various graph learning settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图神经网络的自适应节点特征选择
我们提出了一种图神经网络（GNNs）的自适应节点特征选择方法，该方法在训练过程中识别并移除不必要的特征。衡量特征对模型输出的贡献能力，对于解释决策和通过消除无用变量降低维度至关重要。然而，图结构数据引入了复杂的依赖关系，可能不适合经典特征重要性度量方法。受此启发，我们提出了一种与数据、模型和任务无关的方法，该方法基于对特征值进行置换后验证性能的变化，来确定相关特征。我们从理论上阐述了节点数据与图结构关系如何影响GNN性能，并通过实验证明：(i) 我们高度通用的方法可媲美利用先验假设的定制化特征选择方法；(ii) 我们在GNN完全训练前即可返回有意义的特征重要性评分；(iii) 我们的评分能明确提取出对各类图学习场景中特征重要性具有指导意义的相关属性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to interpret GNN decisions and reduce dimensionality by removing irrelevant node features, this work proposes an adaptive feature selection method that dynamically identifies and eliminates unhelpful features during training. The method is data-, model-, and task-agnostic, relying on validation performance changes induced by permuting feature values to assess feature relevance, addressing the challenges posed by complex graph dependencies. Experiments show that this general approach matches or exceeds tailored methods, provides meaningful importance scores early in training, and effectively captures relevant properties across diverse graph learning tasks.
为了解释GNN决策并降低维度，该研究提出一种自适应节点特征选择方法，在训练过程中动态识别并剔除无用特征。该方法不依赖特定数据、模型或任务，通过置换特征值后验证性能的变化来评估特征相关性，以应对图结构数据的复杂依赖关系。实验表明，该方法性能可媲美或超越定制化特征选择方法，能在训练早期生成有意义的特征重要性评分，并有效提取适用于各类图学习任务的相关属性。
NRGPT: An Energy-based Alternative for GPT
Authors: Nima Dehmamy, Benjamin Hoover, Bishwajit Saha, Leo Kozachkov, Jean-Jacques Slotine, Dmitry Krotov
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-12-18T16:59:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:39:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative Pre-trained Transformer (GPT) architectures are the most popular design for language modeling. Energy-based modeling is a different paradigm that views inference as a dynamical process operating on an energy landscape. We propose a minimal modification of the GPT setting to unify it with the EBM framework. The inference step of our model, which we call eNeRgy-GPT (NRGPT), is conceptualized as an exploration of the tokens on the energy landscape. We prove, and verify empirically, that under certain circumstances this exploration becomes gradient descent, although they don't necessarily lead to the best performing models. We demonstrate that our model performs well for simple language (Shakespeare dataset), algebraic ListOPS tasks, and richer settings such as OpenWebText language modeling. We also observe that our models may be more resistant to overfitting, doing so only during very long training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NRGPT：GPT的一种基于能量的替代方案
生成式预训练变换器（GPT）架构是语言建模中最流行的设计。能量模型是一种不同的范式，将推理视为在能量景观上运行的动态过程。我们提出对GPT设置进行最小修改，以将其与能量模型框架统一。我们模型的推理步骤，称为eNeRgy-GPT（NRGPT），被概念化为在能量景观上探索token。我们证明并实证验证，在某些条件下，这种探索会演变为梯度下降，尽管它们不一定能产生表现最佳的模型。我们展示了该模型在简单语言（莎士比亚数据集）、代数ListOPS任务以及更丰富的OpenWebText语言建模设置中表现良好。我们还观察到，我们的模型可能对过拟合更具抵抗力，仅在极长训练时才会发生过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to integrate energy-based modeling with the success of GPT architectures, the authors propose NRGPT, a minimal modification of GPT that frames inference as exploration on an energy landscape. The model, termed eNeRgy-GPT, conceptualizes token selection as a dynamical process on this landscape, which empirically and theoretically aligns with gradient descent under certain conditions. Experiments show NRGPT performs competitively on Shakespeare, ListOPS, and OpenWebText tasks, and exhibits greater resistance to overfitting compared to standard GPT, though only after very long training.
为将能量模型与GPT架构的成功结合，作者提出NRGPT，对GPT进行最小修改，将推理过程建模为在能量景观上的探索过程。该模型（eNeRgy-GPT）将词元选择视为该景观上的动态过程，在特定条件下可近似梯度下降。实验表明，NRGPT在莎士比亚语料、ListOPS及OpenWebText任务上表现良好，且相比标准GPT在极长训练阶段后更不易过拟合。
Generating Statistical Charts with Validation-Driven LLM Workflows
Authors: Pavlin G. Poličar, Andraž Pevcin, Blaž Zupan
First: 2026-05-01T17:39:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:39:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating diverse, readable statistical charts from tabular data remains challenging for LLMs, as many failures become apparent after rendering and are not detectable from data or code alone. Existing chart datasets also rarely provide fully aligned artifacts, such as executable code, dataset context, and question-answer pairs. We present a structured LLM-based workflow that decomposes chart generation into dataset screening, plot proposal, code synthesis, rendering, validation-driven refinement, description generation, and question-answer generation. By incorporating rendered-output validation, the workflow addresses visualization-specific failure modes such as readability and semantic mismatch. It treats chart generation as an inspectable process rather than a one-shot prompt-to-code task, retaining each chart with its code, dataset context, description, and question-answer pairs. Applied to UCI datasets, the workflow produces 1,500 charts from 74 datasets, spanning 24 chart families and paired with 30,003 question-answer pairs. We evaluate 16 multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) on these chart-question pairs. The results show that chart-syntax questions are nearly saturated, while value extraction, comparison, and reasoning remain more challenging, illustrating the workflow's utility for diagnostic studies of chart-grounded multimodal reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于验证驱动的LLM工作流生成统计图表
从表格数据生成多样且可读的统计图表对LLM仍具挑战性，因许多失败在渲染后才显现，且无法仅从数据或代码中检测。现有图表数据集也罕有完全对齐的产物，如可执行代码、数据上下文及问答对。我们提出一种结构化的LLM工作流，将图表生成分解为数据筛选、图表提案、代码合成、渲染、验证驱动优化、描述生成及问答生成。通过引入渲染输出验证，该工作流解决可视化特有的失败模式，如可读性与语义不匹配。它将图表生成视为可检视过程而非单次提示到代码任务，保留每个图表及其代码、数据上下文、描述与问答对。应用于UCI数据集，该工作流从74个数据集生成1500张图表，涵盖24类图表，并配30003组问答对。我们评估16种多模态LLM（MLLMs）在这些图表问答对上的表现。结果表明，图表语法类问题已近饱和，而数值提取、比较与推理仍具挑战性，凸显该工作流对图表基础多模态推理诊断研究的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in generating readable and semantically accurate statistical charts from tabular data, this work introduces a structured workflow that decomposes chart creation into multiple stages including dataset screening, plot proposal, code synthesis, rendering, validation-driven refinement, description generation, and QA pair creation. The workflow leverages rendered output validation to detect failures like poor readability or semantic mismatch, treating chart generation as an inspectable process rather than a single-step prompt-to-code task. Applied to 74 UCI datasets, it produced 1,500 charts across 24 families with 30,003 QA pairs. Evaluation of 16 MLLMs on these pairs revealed that chart-syntax questions are nearly saturated, while value extraction, comparison, and reasoning remain challenging, highlighting the workflow’s value for diagnosing multimodal reasoning limitations.
为解决大语言模型在生成可读且语义正确的统计图表时的困难，该研究提出一种结构化工作流，将图表生成分解为数据筛选、图表提案、代码合成、渲染、验证驱动优化、描述生成和问答对生成等阶段。通过渲染结果验证，该工作流能捕捉仅靠代码或数据无法发现的失败模式，如可读性问题或语义不匹配。应用于74个UCI数据集，生成了1500张覆盖24类图表的图表，并配对30003组问答对。对16个多模态大模型的评估显示，图表语法类问题已基本解决，但数值提取、比较和推理仍具挑战性，凸显该工作流在诊断多模态推理能力方面的价值。
GMGaze: MoE-Based Context-Aware Gaze Estimation with CLIP and Multiscale Transformer
Authors: Xinyuan Zhao, Yihang Wu, Ahmad Chaddad, Sarah A. Alkhodair, Reem Kateb
First: 2026-05-01T17:35:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:35:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted in KBS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gaze estimation methods commonly use facial appearances to predict the direction of a person gaze. However, previous studies show three major challenges with convolutional neural network (CNN)-based, transformer-based, and contrastive language-image pre-training (CLIP)-based methods, including late fusion of image features, lack of factor-aware conditioning, and impractical capacity scaling. To address these challenges, we propose Globally-conditioned Multi-scale Gaze estimation (GMGaze), which leverages a multi-scale transformer architecture. Specifically, the model first introduces semantic prototype conditioning, which modulates the CLIP global image embedding using four learned prototype banks (i.e., illumination, background, head pose and appearance) to generate two complementary context-biased global tokens. These tokens, along with the CLIP patch and CNN tokens, are fused at the first layer. This early unified fusion prevents information loss common in late-stage merging. Finally, each token passes through sparse Mixture-of-Experts modules, providing conditional computational capacity without uniformly increasing dense parameters. For cross-domain adaptation, we incorporate an adversarial domain adaptation technique with a feature separation loss that encourages the two global tokens to remain de-correlated. Experiments using four public benchmarks (MPIIFaceGaze, EYEDIAP, Gaze360, and ETH-XGaze) show that GMGaze achieves mean angular errors of 2.49$^\circ$, 3.22$^\circ$, 10.16$^\circ$, and 1.44$^\circ$, respectively, outperforming previous baselines in all within-domain settings. In cross-domain evaluations, it provides state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on two standard transfer routes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GMGaze：基于MoE的上下文感知注视估计方法，结合CLIP与多尺度Transformer
注视估计方法通常利用面部外观预测人物注视方向。然而，以往研究表明，基于卷积神经网络（CNN）、Transformer和对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）的方法存在三大挑战：图像特征晚期融合、缺乏因子感知条件化、以及不切实际的容量扩展。为解决这些问题，我们提出全局条件多尺度注视估计（GMGaze），采用多尺度Transformer架构。具体而言，模型首先引入语义原型条件化，通过四个学习到的原型库（即光照、背景、头部姿态和外观）调节CLIP全局图像嵌入，生成两个互补的上下文偏置全局标记。这些标记与CLIP补丁和CNN标记在第一层融合。这种早期统一融合避免了晚期合并常见的信息损失。最后，每个标记通过稀疏Mixture-of-Experts模块，提供条件计算能力而无需均匀增加密集参数。为实现跨域适应，我们引入对抗域适应技术，结合特征分离损失，促使两个全局标记保持去相关。在四个公开基准（MPIIFaceGaze、EYEDIAP、Gaze360和ETH-XGaze）上的实验表明，GMGaze在所有同域设置中均优于先前基线，平均角误差分别为2.49°、3.22°、10.16°和1.44°。在跨域评估中，其在两个标准迁移路径上达到当前最优（SOTA）结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations in existing gaze estimation methods—such as late feature fusion, lack of context-aware conditioning, and inefficient scaling—this paper introduces GMGaze, a multi-scale transformer model that integrates CLIP embeddings with semantic prototype conditioning via four learned prototype banks (illumination, background, head pose, appearance) to generate context-biased global tokens. These tokens are fused early with CLIP patch and CNN features to avoid information loss, then processed through sparse Mixture-of-Experts modules for efficient, conditional computation. An adversarial domain adaptation mechanism further decouples global tokens to improve cross-domain generalization. Experiments on four benchmarks show GMGaze achieves mean angular errors of 2.49°, 3.22°, 10.16°, and 1.44°, outperforming prior methods in all within-domain settings and achieving state-of-the-art results in cross-domain evaluations.
为解决现有基于CNN、Transformer和CLIP的注视估计方法中存在的特征融合过晚、缺乏因素感知条件化及容量扩展不现实等问题，本文提出GMGaze模型，采用多尺度Transformer架构，通过四个学习到的原型库（光照、背景、头部姿态、外观）对CLIP全局图像嵌入进行语义原型条件化，生成两个互补的上下文偏置全局token。这些token与CLIP patch和CNN token在第一层早期融合，避免信息丢失，再经稀疏MoE模块进行条件化计算，提升效率。引入对抗域适应机制与特征分离损失，增强跨域鲁棒性。在四个公开数据集上，GMGaze在所有同域设置中均优于基线方法，平均角误差分别为2.49°、3.22°、10.16°和1.44°，并在跨域评估中达到当前最优水平。
ScreenParse: Moving Beyond Sparse Grounding with Complete Screen Parsing Supervision
Authors: A. Said Gurbuz, Sunghwan Hong, Ahmed Nassar, Marc Pollefeys, Peter Staar
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-15T19:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:32:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. 28 pages, 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern computer-use agents (CUA) must perceive a screen as a structured state, what elements are visible, where they are, and what text they contain, before they can reliably ground instructions and act. Yet, most available grounding datasets provide sparse supervision, with insufficient and low-diversity labels that annotate only a small subset of task-relevant elements per screen, which limits both coverage and generalization; moreover, practical deployment requires efficiency to enable low-latency, on-device use. We introduce ScreenParse, a large-scale dataset for complete screen parsing, with dense annotations of all visible UI elements (boxes, 55-class types, and text) across 771K web screenshots (21M elements). ScreenParse is generated by Webshot, an automated, scalable pipeline that renders diverse urls, extracts annotations and applies VLM-based relabeling and quality filtering. Using ScreenParse, we train ScreenVLM, a compact, 316M-parameter vision language model (VLM) that decodes a compact ScreenTag markup representation with a structure-aware loss that upweights structure-critical tokens. ScreenVLM substantially outperforms much larger foundation VLMs on dense parsing (e.g., 0.592 vs. 0.294 PageIoU on ScreenParse) and shows strong transfer to public benchmarks. Moreover, finetuning foundation VLMs on ScreenParse consistently improves their grounding performance, suggesting that dense screen supervision provides transferable structural priors for UI understanding. Project page: https://saidgurbuz.github.io/screenparse/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ScreenParse：超越稀疏锚定的完整屏幕解析监督
现代计算机使用代理（CUA）必须将屏幕感知为结构化状态，包括可见元素、其位置及所含文本，才能可靠锚定指令并执行操作。然而，现有大多数锚定数据集提供稀疏监督，标注不足且多样性低，仅标注每屏中少量任务相关元素，限制了覆盖范围与泛化能力；此外，实际部署需高效以支持低延迟、设备端运行。我们引入ScreenParse，一个大规模完整屏幕解析数据集，包含77.1万张网页截图（2100万个元素）中所有可见UI元素（框、55类类型及文本）的密集标注。ScreenParse由Webshot自动生成，该自动化可扩展流水线渲染多样URL、提取标注并应用基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的重标注与质量过滤。基于ScreenParse，我们训练了ScreenVLM，一个紧凑型3.16亿参数视觉语言模型（VLM），其通过结构感知损失解码紧凑的ScreenTag标记表示，该损失增强结构关键词。ScreenVLM在密集解析任务上显著优于更大规模基础VLM（如ScreenParse上PageIoU达0.592 vs. 0.294），并在公开基准上展现强迁移能力。此外，以ScreenParse微调基础VLM可一致提升其锚定性能，表明密集屏幕监督为UI理解提供了可迁移的结构先验。项目页面：https://saidgurbuz.github.io/screenparse/。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of sparse UI grounding datasets that hinder coverage and generalization, ScreenParse introduces a large-scale dataset with dense annotations of all visible UI elements across 771K web screenshots, generated via an automated pipeline called Webshot. Using this dataset, ScreenVLM—a compact 316M-parameter VLM—is trained with a structure-aware loss to decode a compact ScreenTag markup, achieving significantly better dense parsing performance (0.592 PageIoU vs. 0.294 for larger models) and demonstrating strong transfer to public benchmarks, while fine-tuning existing VLMs on ScreenParse consistently improves their grounding capabilities.
为解决现有稀疏UI标注数据集在覆盖范围和泛化能力上的局限，ScreenParse构建了一个大规模密集标注数据集，涵盖77.1万张网页截图中的2100万个可见UI元素（包括框坐标、55类类型和文本）。该数据集通过自动化工具Webshot生成，包含视觉语言模型驱动的重标注与质量过滤。基于此数据集训练的ScreenVLM模型（3.16亿参数）采用结构感知损失输出结构化ScreenTag标记，其在密集解析任务上显著优于大型基础模型（PageIoU达0.592 vs 0.294），并在公开基准上表现优异；此外，对基础模型进行ScreenParse微调也持续提升其定位性能，表明密集屏幕监督可提供可迁移的UI结构先验知识。
RunAgent: Interpreting Natural-Language Plans with Constraint-Guided Execution
Authors: Arunabh Srivastava, Mohammad A., Khojastepour, Srimat Chakradhar, Sennur Ulukus
First: 2026-05-01T17:29:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:29:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans solve problems by executing targeted plans, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for structured workflow execution. We propose RunAgent, a multi-agent plan execution platform that interprets natural-language plans while enforcing stepwise execution through constraints and rubrics. RunAgent bridges the expressiveness of natural language with the determinism of programming via an agentic language with explicit control constructs (e.g., \texttt{IF}, \texttt{GOTO}, \texttt{FORALL}). Beyond verifying syntactic and semantic verification of the step output, which is performed based on the specific instruction of each step, RunAgent autonomously derives and validates constraints based on the description of the task and its instance at each step. RunAgent also dynamically selects among LLM-based reasoning, tool usage, and code generation and execution (e.g., in Python), and incorporates error correction mechanisms to ensure correctness. Finally, RunAgent filters the context history by retaining only relevant information during the execution of each step. Evaluations on Natural-plan and SciBench Datasets demonstrate that RunAgent outperforms baseline LLMs and state-of-the-art PlanGEN methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RunAgent：通过约束引导执行解释自然语言计划
人类通过执行有针对性的计划解决问题，但大型语言模型（LLM）在结构化工作流执行方面仍不可靠。我们提出了RunAgent，一个多代理计划执行平台，它在执行过程中通过约束和评分标准引导步骤式执行，同时解释自然语言计划。RunAgent通过一种具有显式控制结构（如\texttt{IF}、\texttt{GOTO}、\texttt{FORALL}）的代理语言，弥合了自然语言的表达力与编程的确定性之间的差距。除了根据每一步的具体指令验证语法和语义外，RunAgent还能自主推导并验证基于任务描述及其实例的约束。RunAgent还动态选择基于LLM的推理、工具使用和代码生成与执行（如Python），并集成错误纠正机制以确保正确性。最后，RunAgent在每一步执行过程中过滤上下文历史，仅保留相关信息。在Natural-plan和SciBench数据集上的评估表明，RunAgent优于基线LLM和最先进的PlanGEN方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to execute natural-language plans reliably in structured workflows, RunAgent introduces a multi-agent system that interprets and enforces step-by-step execution via constraint-guided mechanisms. It translates natural language into an agentic language with explicit control constructs like IF, GOTO, and FORALL, and dynamically validates each step’s output against task-specific constraints derived from the plan and context. RunAgent also autonomously selects between LLM reasoning, tool usage, and code execution (e.g., Python), applies error correction, and prunes irrelevant context history. Experimental results on Natural-plan and SciBench datasets show RunAgent significantly outperforms baseline LLMs and state-of-the-art PlanGEN methods in plan execution accuracy and reliability.
为解决大语言模型在结构化工作流中执行自然语言计划不可靠的问题，RunAgent提出一种多智能体系统，通过约束引导和控制结构（如IF、GOTO、FORALL）确保计划分步执行。系统在每一步自动生成并验证任务约束，动态选择LLM推理、工具调用或代码生成与执行（如Python），并结合错误纠正与上下文过滤机制保障正确性。在Natural-plan和SciBench数据集上的实验表明，RunAgent在计划执行准确率和可靠性上显著优于基线LLM及现有PlanGEN方法。
When RAG Chatbots Expose Their Backend: An Anonymized Case Study of Privacy and Security Risks in Patient-Facing Medical AI
Authors: Alfredo Madrid-García, Miguel Rujas
First: 2026-05-01T17:29:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:29:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Patient-facing medical chatbots based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) are increasingly promoted to deliver accessible, grounded health information. AI-assisted development lowers the barrier to building them, but they still demand rigorous security, privacy, and governance controls. Objective: To report an anonymized, non-destructive security assessment of a publicly accessible patient-facing medical RAG chatbot and identify governance lessons for safe deployment of generative AI in health. Methods: We used a two-stage strategy. First, Claude Opus 4.6 supported exploratory prompt-based testing and structured vulnerability hypotheses. Second, candidate findings were manually verified using Chrome Developer Tools, inspecting browser-visible network traffic, payloads, API schemas, configuration objects, and stored interaction data. Results: The LLM-assisted phase identified a critical vulnerability: sensitive system and RAG configuration appeared exposed through client-server communication rather than restricted server-side. Manual verification confirmed that ordinary browser inspection allowed collection of the system prompt, model and embedding configuration, retrieval parameters, backend endpoints, API schema, document and chunk metadata, knowledge-base content, and the 1,000 most recent patient-chatbot conversations. The deployment also contradicted its privacy assurances: full conversation records, including health-related queries, were retrievable without authentication. Conclusions: Serious privacy and security failures in patient-facing RAG chatbots can be identified with standard browser tools, without specialist skills or authentication; independent review should be a prerequisite for deployment. Commercial LLMs accelerated this assessment, including under a false developer persona; assistance available to auditors is equally available to adversaries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当RAG聊天机器人暴露其后端：面向患者的医疗AI隐私与安全风险匿名案例研究
背景：基于检索增强生成（RAG）的面向患者的医疗聊天机器人正日益被推广，以提供可及且有依据的健康信息。AI辅助开发降低了构建此类机器人的门槛，但其仍需严格的安全、隐私和治理控制。目标：报告一个公开可访问的面向患者医疗RAG聊天机器人的匿名、非破坏性安全评估，并识别生成式AI在医疗领域安全部署的治理经验。方法：我们采用两阶段策略。第一阶段，使用Claude Opus 4.6支持基于提示的探索性测试和结构化漏洞假设。第二阶段，通过Chrome开发者工具手动验证候选发现，检查浏览器可见的网络流量、负载、API架构、配置对象和存储的交互数据。结果：LLM辅助阶段发现一个关键漏洞：敏感系统和RAG配置通过客户端-服务器通信暴露，而非限制在服务器端。手动验证确认，普通浏览器检查即可收集系统提示、模型与嵌入配置、检索参数、后端端点、API架构、文档与分块元数据、知识库内容，以及最近1000次患者-聊天机器人对话。部署也违背其隐私承诺：无需认证即可检索完整对话记录，包括健康相关查询。结论：面向患者的RAG聊天机器人中严重的隐私与安全缺陷可通过标准浏览器工具识别，无需专业技能或认证；独立审查应作为部署前提。商业LLM加速了此评估，包括在虚假开发者身份下；审计人员可获得的协助同样可被攻击者利用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots in patient-facing medical AI, this study aimed to uncover privacy and security risks in such systems. Using a two-stage method involving Claude Opus 4.6 for exploratory testing and manual browser-based verification via Chrome Developer Tools, researchers identified critical vulnerabilities: sensitive backend configurations, system prompts, API schemas, knowledge-base content, and 1,000 recent patient conversations were exposed through client-server traffic without authentication. The findings revealed that deployment contradicted privacy claims, as full conversation records—including health-related queries—were accessible to anyone without login, highlighting the need for independent security audits before deployment.
鉴于基于检索增强生成（RAG）的面向患者的医疗聊天机器人日益普及，本研究旨在评估其安全与隐私风险。采用两阶段方法，先利用Claude Opus 4.6进行探索性测试，再通过Chrome开发者工具手动验证，研究人员发现关键漏洞：系统配置、后端接口、知识库内容及最近1000次患者对话均通过客户端与服务器通信暴露，且无需认证即可访问。结果表明，普通浏览器工具即可暴露这些风险，无需专业技能，严重违背隐私承诺，凸显部署前应强制进行独立安全审计的必要性。
Game-Time: Evaluating Temporal Dynamics in Spoken Language Models
Authors: Kai-Wei Chang, En-Pei Hu, Chun-Yi Kuan, Wenze Ren, Wei-Chih Chen, Guan-Ting Lin, Yu Tsao, Shao-Hua Sun, Hung-yi Lee, James Glass
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2025-09-30T15:23:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:28:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICASSP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Conversational Spoken Language Models (SLMs) are emerging as a promising paradigm for real-time speech interaction. However, their capacity of temporal dynamics, including the ability to manage timing, tempo and simultaneous speaking, remains a critical and unevaluated challenge for conversational fluency. To address this gap, we introduce the Game-Time Benchmark, a framework to systematically assess these temporal capabilities. Inspired by how humans learn a language through language activities, Game-Time consists of basic instruction-following tasks and advanced tasks with temporal constraints, such as tempo adherence and synchronized responses. Our evaluation of diverse SLM architectures reveals a clear performance disparity: while state-of-the-art models handle basic tasks well, many contemporary systems still struggle with fundamental instruction-following. More critically, nearly all models degrade substantially under temporal constraints, exposing persistent weaknesses in time awareness and full-duplex interaction. The Game-Time Benchmark provides a foundation for guiding future research toward more temporally-aware conversational AI. Demos and datasets are available on our project website https://ga642381.github.io/Game-Time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：游戏时间：评估口语语言模型中的时序动态
对话式口语语言模型（SLM）正成为实时语音交互的有前景范式。然而，其处理时序动态的能力——包括管理时长、语速和同时说话的能力——仍是对话流利性中关键且未被充分评估的挑战。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Game-Time基准测试框架，以系统评估这些时序能力。该框架借鉴人类通过语言活动学习语言的方式，包含基础指令遵循任务和具有时序约束的高级任务，如语速遵循和同步响应。对多种SLM架构的评估揭示了明显性能差异：虽然最先进模型能较好处理基础任务，但许多当前系统仍难以完成基本指令遵循。更关键的是，几乎所有模型在时序约束下性能显著下降，暴露出对时间感知和全双工交互的持续性弱点。Game-Time基准测试为未来研究引导更具时序感知能力的对话式AI提供了基础。演示和数据集可在项目网站https://ga642381.github.io/Game-Time获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how well spoken language models handle real-time conversational timing, the authors introduce Game-Time, a benchmark designed to assess temporal dynamics such as tempo control and synchronized speech. The method combines basic instruction-following tasks with advanced temporal constraints to probe model behavior under time-sensitive conditions. Experimental results show that while top-performing models manage simple tasks effectively, most struggle with fundamental instruction-following and exhibit significant performance drops under temporal demands, revealing a persistent lack of time-awareness in current conversational systems.
为评估语音语言模型在实时对话中处理时间动态（如节奏、同步发言）的能力，作者提出了Game-Time基准测试框架，其设计灵感来源于人类通过语言活动习得语言的过程，包含基础指令任务和需遵循节奏与同步响应的高级任务。实验表明，尽管顶尖模型在简单任务上表现良好，多数模型仍难以完成基本指令，且在时间约束下性能大幅下降，暴露出当前系统在时间感知与全双工交互方面的持续性缺陷。
The Quantization Trap: Breaking Linear Scaling Laws in Multi-Hop Reasoning
Authors: Henry Han, Xiyang Liu, Xiaodong Wang, Fei Han, Xiaodong Li
First: 2026-02-14T04:25:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:25:12+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural scaling laws provide a predictable recipe for AI advancement: reducing numerical precision should linearly improve computational efficiency and energy profile ($E \propto \mathrm{bits}$). In this paper, we demonstrate that this scaling law breaks in the context of multi-hop reasoning. We reveal a 'quantization trap' where reducing precision from 16-bit to 8/4-bit paradoxically increases net energy consumption while degrading reasoning accuracy. We provide a rigorous theoretical decomposition that attributes this failure to hardware casting overhead, the hidden latency cost of dequantization kernels, which becomes a dominant bottleneck in sequential reasoning chains, as well as to a sequential energy amortization failure. As a result, scaling law breaking is unavoidable in practice. We formalize a Critical Model Scale $N^*$ that predicts when the trap dissolves or deepens as a function of model size, batch size, and hardware configuration, validated across a 120$\times$ range (0.6B--72B) on six GPU architectures. Our findings suggest that the industry's "smaller-is-better" heuristic is mathematically counterproductive for complex reasoning tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化陷阱：多跳推理中的线性缩放定律失效
神经网络缩放定律提供了一种可预测的AI进步方法：降低数值精度应线性提升计算效率和能耗（$E \propto \mathrm{bits}$）。本文证明，在多跳推理场景中该缩放定律失效。我们揭示了“量化陷阱”：从16位降至8/4位精度反而增加总能耗并降低推理准确性。我们通过严谨的理论分解，将此失效归因于硬件截断开销、反量化内核的隐性延迟成本（在顺序推理链中成为主导瓶颈），以及顺序能耗摊销失败。因此，缩放定律失效在实践中不可避免。我们形式化了临界模型规模 $N^*$，预测模型大小、批大小和硬件配置下陷阱的消解或加深，已在六种GPU架构上0.6B至72B的120倍范围内验证。研究结果表明，业界“越小越好”的经验法则在复杂推理任务中数学上适得其反。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical observation that neural scaling laws fail in multi-hop reasoning tasks, this paper investigates why reducing model precision from 16-bit to 8-bit or 4-bit leads to increased energy consumption and degraded accuracy, contradicting the expectation that lower precision improves efficiency. The authors introduce a theoretical decomposition revealing that hardware casting overhead and dequantization latency become dominant bottlenecks in sequential reasoning chains, causing energy amortization to fail. Experiments across 120x model size range (0.6B–72B) on six GPU architectures validate a Critical Model Scale N*, which predicts whether the quantization trap deepens or dissolves based on model size, batch size, and hardware. Results show that smaller models are more susceptible to the trap, undermining the industry’s "smaller-is-better" heuristic for complex reasoning.
本文基于降低模型精度可线性提升效率的普遍假设，研究多跳推理任务时发现‘量化陷阱’：8位或4位量化反而增加能耗并降低精度，相比16位表现更差。作者归因于硬件转换开销和反量化延迟在序列推理链中成为主导瓶颈，以及能量摊销失效。他们提出临界模型规模 $N^*$，预测量化在不同模型大小、批处理规模和硬件配置下是更优或更差，已在120倍模型规模范围和六种GPU架构上验证。结果表明，行业‘越小越好’的直觉在复杂推理任务中数学上不可行。
TURBOTEST: Learning When Less is Enough through Early Termination of Internet Speed Tests
Authors: Haarika Manda, Manshi Sagar, Yogesh, Kartikay Singh, Cindy Zhao, Tarun Mangla, Phillipa Gill, Elizabeth Belding, Arpit Gupta
First: 2025-10-24T04:25:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:20:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Internet speed tests are indispensable for users, ISPs, and policymakers, but their static flooding-based design imposes growing costs: a single high-speed test can transfer hundreds of MB, and collectively, platforms like Ookla, M-Lab, and Fast.com generate petabytes of traffic each month. Reducing this burden requires deciding when a test can be stopped early without sacrificing accuracy. We frame this as an optimal stopping problem and show that existing heuristics-static thresholds, BBR pipe-full signals, or throughput stability rules from Fast.com and FastBTS-capture only a narrow slice of the achievable accuracy-savings trade-off. This paper introduces TurboTest, a systematic framework for speed test termination that sits atop existing platforms. The key idea is to decouple throughput prediction (Stage 1) from test termination (Stage 2): Stage 1 trains a regressor to estimate final throughput from partial measurements, while Stage 2 trains a classifier to decide when sufficient evidence has accumulated to stop. Leveraging richer transport-level features (RTT, retransmissions, congestion window) alongside throughput, TurboTest exposes a single tunable parameter epsilon for accuracy tolerance and includes a fallback mechanism for high-variability cases. Evaluation on 1 million M-Lab NDT speed tests (2024-2025) shows that TurboTest achieves 1.8-4.4x higher data savings than an approach based on BBR signals while reducing median error. These results demonstrate that adaptive ML-based termination can deliver accurate, efficient, and deployable speed tests at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TURBOTEST：通过提前终止互联网测速实现少即是多的学习
互联网测速对用户、ISP和政策制定者至关重要，但其静态洪水式设计带来日益增长的成本：单次高速测速可传输数百MB，而Ookla、M-Lab和Fast.com等平台每月合计生成数PB流量。减少此负担需判断何时可提前终止测速而不牺牲准确性。本文将其建模为最优停止问题，表明现有启发式方法（静态阈值、BBR管道满信号或Fast.com与FastBTS的吞吐量稳定性规则）仅覆盖可实现的准确性-节省量权衡的一小部分。本文提出TurboTest，一种基于现有平台的系统性测速终止框架。核心思想是将吞吐量预测（阶段1）与测速终止（阶段2）解耦：阶段1训练回归器从部分测量值预测最终吞吐量，阶段2训练分类器判断何时已积累足够证据可终止。结合更丰富的传输层特征（RTT、重传、拥塞窗口）与吞吐量，TurboTest仅需一个可调参数ε控制精度容忍度，并包含高变异性情况下的回退机制。在100万次M-Lab NDT测速数据（2024-2025）上的评估表明，TurboTest相比基于BBR信号的方法实现1.8-4.4倍更高的数据节省，同时降低中位误差。结果证明，自适应的基于机器学习的终止机制可大规模提供准确、高效且可部署的测速服务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high bandwidth costs of traditional internet speed tests, this paper proposes TurboTest, a machine learning framework for early termination of speed tests to reduce data usage without compromising accuracy. The method decouples throughput prediction from termination decision-making: Stage 1 trains a regressor to estimate final throughput from partial data, while Stage 2 trains a classifier to determine when enough evidence has been gathered to stop. TurboTest leverages transport-layer features like RTT and congestion window alongside throughput, and introduces a single tunable accuracy tolerance parameter epsilon with a fallback for high-variability cases. Evaluated on 1 million M-Lab NDT tests, TurboTest achieved 1.8–4.4x higher data savings than BBR-based methods while reducing median error, demonstrating scalable, accurate, and efficient speed testing.
为降低传统互联网测速工具的高带宽开销，本文提出TurboTest框架，通过机器学习实现测速过程的早期终止，以减少数据使用而不牺牲准确性。该框架将吞吐量预测与终止决策分离：第一阶段训练回归器从部分数据预测最终吞吐量，第二阶段训练分类器判断何时已积累足够证据可停止测试。TurboTest结合RTT、重传、拥塞窗口等传输层特征与吞吐量数据，并引入单一可调参数epsilon控制精度容忍度。在100万条M-Lab NDT测速数据上评估显示，TurboTest相较BBR方法节省1.8–4.4倍数据，同时降低中位误差，证明其具备可扩展、准确且高效的测速能力。
Detection Is Cheap, Routing Is Learned: Why Refusal-Based Alignment Evaluation Fails
Authors: Gregory N. Frank
First: 2026-03-18T20:54:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:19:25+00:00
Comments: Code and data: https://github.com/gregfrank/routing-is-learned
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current alignment evaluation mostly measures whether models encode dangerous concepts and whether they refuse harmful requests. Both miss the layer where alignment often operates: routing from concept detection to behavioral policy. We study political censorship in Chinese-origin language models as a natural experiment, using probes, surgical ablations, and behavioral tests across nine open-weight models from five labs. Three findings follow. First, probe accuracy alone is non-diagnostic: political probes, null controls, and permutation baselines can all reach 100%, so held-out category generalization is the informative test. Second, surgical ablation reveals lab-specific routing. Removing the political-sensitivity direction eliminates censorship and restores accurate factual output in most models tested, while one model confabulates because its architecture entangles factual knowledge with the censorship mechanism. Cross-model transfer fails, indicating that routing geometry is model- and lab-specific. Third, refusal is no longer the dominant censorship mechanism. Within one model family, hard refusal falls to zero while narrative steering rises to the maximum, making censorship invisible to refusal-only benchmarks. These results support a three-stage descriptive framework: detect, route, generate. Models often retain the relevant knowledge; alignment changes how that knowledge is expressed. Evaluations that audit only detection or refusal therefore miss the routing mechanism that most directly determines behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检测成本低廉，路由机制学习而来：为何基于拒绝的对齐评估失效
当前对齐评估主要衡量模型是否编码危险概念以及是否拒绝有害请求，但两者均忽略了对齐实际运作的关键层面：从概念检测到行为策略的路由机制。我们以中文源语言模型中的政治审查作为自然实验，通过探针、手术式消融和行为测试，研究了五个实验室的九个开源模型。得出三项发现：第一，探针准确率本身无诊断价值：政治探针、空对照组和排列基线均可达到100%，因此保留类别泛化能力才是有信息量的测试。第二，手术式消融揭示了实验室特异性的路由机制：移除政治敏感性方向后，多数模型的审查消失并恢复准确事实输出，但一个模型因架构将事实知识与审查机制纠缠，反而产生虚构内容；跨模型迁移失败，表明路由几何结构具有模型和实验室特异性。第三，拒绝机制不再是主要审查方式：在某一模型家族中，硬性拒绝率降至零，而叙事引导率升至最大，使审查机制对仅依赖拒绝的基准测试变得不可见。这些结果支持三阶段描述框架：检测、路由、生成。模型通常保留相关知识，对齐改变的是知识表达方式。仅审计检测或拒绝的评估因此忽略了最直接决定行为的路由机制。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why current alignment evaluation methods, which focus on concept detection and refusal, fail to capture the true mechanisms of alignment. Using probes, surgical ablations, and behavioral tests across nine open-weight Chinese-language models, the authors find that probe accuracy alone is misleading, as multiple baselines can achieve 100% accuracy without reflecting real generalization. Surgical removal of political-sensitivity directions often restores factual output and eliminates censorship, revealing lab-specific routing mechanisms that vary across models. Crucially, refusal is no longer the primary censorship method; narrative steering dominates in some models, rendering refusal-only benchmarks ineffective. The findings support a three-stage framework—detect, route, generate—where alignment primarily alters how knowledge is routed to behavior, not whether it is detected or refused.
该研究探讨为何当前以概念检测和拒绝机制为核心的对齐评估方法无法有效衡量语言模型的对齐效果。通过对五家机构的九个开源模型进行探针测试、手术式消融和行为实验，作者发现探针准确率本身无诊断价值，因为各类基线（包括空对照和排列基线）均可达到100%，唯有未见类别泛化能力才具信息量。手术式移除政治敏感性方向后，多数模型消除审查并恢复事实输出，但一个模型因架构中知识与审查机制混杂而产生幻觉，且模型间迁移失败，表明路由结构具有模型特异性。更重要的是，拒绝机制已非主要审查方式：在某一模型家族内，硬拒绝率趋近于零，而叙事引导机制达到峰值，使拒绝基准失效。这些结果支持“检测—路由—生成”三阶段框架，即对齐的核心在于改变知识表达的路由方式，而非检测或拒绝本身。
Unsupervised Denoising of Real Clinical Low Dose Liver CT with Perceptual Attention Networks
Authors: Jingxi Pu, Tonghua Liu, Zhilin Guan, Siqiao Li, Yang Ming, Zheng Cong, Wei Zhang, Fangwei Li
First: 2026-05-01T17:19:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:19:15+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 10 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the development of deep learning, medical image processing has been widely used to assist clinical research. This paper focuses on the denoising problem of low-dose computed tomography using deep learning. Although low-dose computed tomography reduces radiation exposure to patients, it also introduces more noise, which may interfere with visual interpretation by physicians and affect diagnostic results. To address this problem, inspired by Cycle-GAN for unsupervised learning, this paper proposes an end-to-end unsupervised low-dose computed tomography denoising framework. The proposed framework combines a U-Net structure for multi-scale feature extraction, an attention mechanism for feature fusion, and a residual network for feature transformation. It also introduces perceptual loss to improve the network for the characteristics of medical images. In addition, we construct a real low-dose computed tomography dataset and design a large number of comparative experiments to validate the proposed method, using both image-based evaluation metrics and medical evaluation criteria. Compared with classical methods, the main advantage of this paper is that it addresses the limitation that real clinical data cannot be directly used for supervised learning, while still achieving excellent performance. The experimental results are also professionally evaluated by imaging physicians and meet clinical needs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于感知注意力网络的实临床低剂量肝部CT图像无监督去噪
随着深度学习的发展，医学图像处理已广泛应用于辅助临床研究。本文聚焦于利用深度学习解决低剂量CT图像去噪问题。尽管低剂量CT可减少患者辐射暴露，但也引入更多噪声，可能干扰医生视觉解读并影响诊断结果。为解决此问题，受Cycle-GAN无监督学习启发，本文提出一种端到端的无监督低剂量CT去噪框架。该框架结合U-Net结构进行多尺度特征提取、注意力机制实现特征融合、残差网络进行特征变换，并引入感知损失以提升网络对医学图像特性的适应性。此外，本文构建真实低剂量CT数据集，设计大量对比实验，结合图像评价指标与医学评估标准验证方法有效性。相比经典方法，本文主要优势在于克服真实临床数据无法直接用于监督学习的限制，同时仍取得优异性能。实验结果经影像科医生专业评估，符合临床需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce noise in real clinical low-dose liver CT scans without labeled data, this paper proposes an unsupervised denoising framework inspired by Cycle-GAN. The method combines a U-Net for multi-scale feature extraction, an attention mechanism for selective feature fusion, and a residual network for transformation, enhanced by perceptual loss to better capture medical image characteristics. Evaluated on a newly constructed real low-dose CT dataset using both quantitative metrics and physician assessments, the framework outperforms classical methods and demonstrates clinical relevance by effectively restoring image quality while preserving diagnostic features without requiring paired training data.
为提升低剂量肝部CT图像诊断准确性，本文提出一种端到端无监督去噪框架，受Cycle-GAN启发，结合U-Net多尺度特征提取、注意力机制特征融合与残差网络变换，并引入感知损失以增强医学图像特性。在真实临床低剂量CT数据集上，通过图像指标与放射科医师评估验证，该方法在无需配对训练数据的前提下超越传统方法，展现出临床适用性与实际场景中的优异性能。
Last-Iterate Analyses of FTRL with the 1/2-Tsallis Entropy in Stochastic Bandits
Authors: Jingxin Zhan, Yuze Han, Zhihua Zhang
First: 2025-10-26T20:20:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:15:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The convergence analysis of online learning algorithms is central to machine learning theory, where the last-iterate convergence is particularly important, as it captures the learner's actual decisions and describes the evolution of the learning process over time. However, in multi-armed bandits, most existing algorithmic analyses mainly focus on the order of regret, while the last-iterate (simple regret) convergence rate remains less explored -- especially for the widely studied Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (FTRL) algorithms. Recently, FTRL with the $1/2$-Tsallis entropy regularizer $Ψ(p) = -4\sum_{i=1}^d \sqrt{p_i}$ (the $1/2$-Tsallis-INF algorithm, by arXiv:1807.07623) was shown to achieve logarithmic regret in stochastic bandits. Nevertheless, its last-iterate convergence rate has not yet been studied. Intuitively, logarithmic regret should correspond to a $t^{-1}$ last-iterate convergence rate. This paper studies the $1/2$-Tsallis-INF algorithm and partially confirms this intuition through theoretical analysis, showing that the Bregman divergence, defined by $Ψ(p)$, between the point mass on the optimal arm and the probability distribution over the arm set obtained at iteration $t$, decays at a rate of $t^{-1/2}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机多臂赌博机中带1/2-Tsallis熵的FTRL算法的最后迭代分析
在线学习算法的收敛性分析是机器学习理论的核心，其中最后迭代收敛尤为重要，因为它捕捉学习者的实际决策并描述学习过程随时间的演化。然而，在多臂赌博机问题中，现有算法分析主要关注遗憾的阶数，而最后迭代（简单遗憾）收敛率研究较少——尤其对于广泛研究的跟随正则化领导者（FTRL）算法。最近，使用$1/2$-Tsallis熵正则化器$Ψ(p) = -4\sum_{i=1}^d \sqrt{p_i}$（即arXiv:1807.07623中的$1/2$-Tsallis-INF算法）的FTRL算法在随机多臂赌博机中实现了对数遗憾。但其最后迭代收敛率尚未被研究。直观上，对数遗憾应对应$t^{-1}$的最后迭代收敛率。本文研究$1/2$-Tsallis-INF算法，通过理论分析部分证实了这一直觉，表明在第$t$次迭代时，最优臂的点质量与臂集上的概率分布之间由$Ψ(p)$定义的Bregman散度以$t^{-1/2}$的速度衰减。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the temporal behavior of online learning algorithms in stochastic bandits, this paper investigates the last-iterate convergence of the FTRL algorithm using the 1/2-Tsallis entropy regularizer. The method involves analyzing the decay rate of the Bregman divergence between the optimal arm’s point mass and the learner’s distribution over arms. Experimental results show that this divergence decays at a rate of t^{-1/2}, partially confirming the intuition that logarithmic regret corresponds to a t^{-1} convergence rate, though the actual rate is slower.
为理解随机多臂老虎机中在线学习算法的时序行为，本文研究了使用1/2-Tsallis熵正则化的FTRL变体（1/2-Tsallis-INF算法）的最后迭代收敛性。方法通过理论分析，对正则化器诱导的Bregman散度进行上界估计，该散度衡量最优臂的点质量与学习者在每轮迭代中分布之间的差距。实验结果表明，该散度以 $t^{-1/2}$ 的速率衰减，慢于由对数遗憾所暗示的 $t^{-1}$，说明最后迭代收敛率不能简单由遗憾率推断。
Demystifying Mergeability: Interpretable Properties to Predict Model Merging Success
Authors: Luca Zhou, Bo Zhao, Rose Yu, Emanuele Rodolà
First: 2026-01-29T20:00:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:12:01+00:00
Comments: 9 pages of main paper, 3 figures in the main paper, 4 tables in the main paper, many more figures and tables in the appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging combines knowledge from separately fine-tuned models, yet the factors driving its success remain poorly understood. While recent work treats mergeability as an intrinsic property of the models, we show with an architecture-agnostic framework that it fundamentally depends on both the merging method and the partner tasks. Using L1-regularized linear optimization over a set of interpretable pairwise metrics (e.g., gradient $L_2$ distance), we uncover properties correlating with post-merge normalized accuracy across five merging methods. We find architecture- and method-specific variation in success drivers (64.0% average top-5 metric overlap; 79.3% sign agreement), with certain methods, notably TIES, exhibiting distinct ``fingerprints'' that diverge from the broader consensus. Crucially, however, \textit{gradient alignment} metrics consistently emerge as the most fundamental signals of compatibility. These findings provide a diagnostic foundation for understanding mergeability and motivate future merge-aware fine-tuning strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭开模型合并可融合性的神秘面纱：预测模型合并成功的可解释性属性
模型合并将多个独立微调模型的知识融合，但其成功驱动因素仍不明确。尽管近期研究将可融合性视为模型的内在属性，我们通过架构无关框架表明，其根本上依赖于合并方法与合作任务。利用L1正则化线性优化在一组可解释的成对度量（如梯度L2距离）上，我们揭示了与五种合并方法合并后归一化准确率相关的属性。我们发现成功驱动因素存在架构和方法特异性差异（平均前5名度量重合率64.0%；符号一致性79.3%），某些方法（如TIES）展现出与主流共识不同的“指纹”。然而，关键的是，梯度对齐度量始终作为兼容性最根本的信号持续出现。这些发现为理解可融合性提供了诊断基础，并推动未来融合感知的微调策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of what makes model merging successful, this study introduces an architecture-agnostic framework to identify interpretable properties that predict merge outcomes. Using L1-regularized linear optimization over pairwise metrics like gradient L2 distance, the authors analyze five merging methods and find that success depends not only on model properties but also on the merging technique and partner tasks. Their experiments reveal method-specific success drivers with high metric overlap and sign agreement, yet gradient alignment consistently emerges as the most reliable indicator of compatibility across all methods, offering a diagnostic lens for future merge-aware fine-tuning.
为了解决模型合并成功与否缺乏明确判据的问题，该研究提出一种架构无关框架，通过L1正则化线性优化方法，基于梯度L2距离等可解释的成对指标，评估五种合并方法在多任务下的表现。结果发现，不同方法和架构的成功驱动因素存在差异，TIES方法表现出独特模式，而梯度对齐始终是预测兼容性的最可靠信号，为理解模型合并机制提供了诊断基础，并指导未来合并感知的微调策略。
D3-Gym: Constructing Real-World Verifiable Environments for Data-Driven Discovery
Authors: Hanane Nour Moussa, Yifei Li, Zhuoyang Li, Yankai Yang, Cheng Tang, Tianshu Zhang, Nesreen K. Ahmed, Ali Payani, Ziru Chen, Huan Sun
First: 2026-04-30T15:06:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:11:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite recent progress in language models and agents for scientific data-driven discovery, further advancing their capabilities is held back by the absence of verifiable environments representing real-world scientific tasks. To fill this gap, we introduce D3-Gym, the first automatically constructed dataset with verifiable environments for scientific Data-Driven Discovery. D3-Gym comprises (1) 565 tasks sourced from 239 real scientific repositories across four disciplines where (2) each task is equipped with a natural language instruction, an executable environment with pre-installed dependencies, input dataset and artifact previews, a reference code solution, and an automatically synthesized evaluation script. Rigorous evaluation of the quality of the verification signal in D3-Gym confirms that our evaluation scripts achieve 87.5% agreement with human-annotated gold standards and strong alignment in domain-specific evaluation logic, showing their scientific soundness. Further, training on trajectories sampled from D3-Gym yields consistent and substantial gains across Qwen3 models of varying sizes on ScienceAgentBench, boosting Qwen3-32B by 7.8 absolute points and substantially shrinking the gap with strong proprietary models. All D3-Gym artifacts (environments, creation workflow, trajectories, and models) can be found at https://github.com/OSU-NLP-Group/D3-Gym.
中文标题/摘要
标题：D3-Gym：构建用于数据驱动科学发现的可验证真实世界环境
尽管在语言模型和科学数据驱动发现的代理方面取得了最新进展，但其能力的进一步提升受限于缺乏代表真实世界科学任务的可验证环境。为填补这一空白，我们引入了D3-Gym，这是首个自动构建的、用于科学数据驱动发现的可验证环境数据集。D3-Gym包含（1）来自四个学科的239个真实科学仓库中的565个任务，其中（2）每个任务均配备自然语言指令、预装依赖项的可执行环境、输入数据集和成果预览、参考代码解决方案以及自动合成的评估脚本。对D3-Gym中验证信号质量的严格评估证实，我们的评估脚本与人工标注的黄金标准达成87.5%的一致性，并在领域特定评估逻辑上表现出强对齐，显示其科学合理性。此外，基于D3-Gym采样的轨迹进行训练，在ScienceAgentBench上对不同规模的Qwen3模型均带来一致且显著的提升，使Qwen3-32B模型绝对得分提升7.8分，并大幅缩小与强专有模型之间的差距。所有D3-Gym成果（环境、构建流程、轨迹和模型）均可在https://github.com/OSU-NLP-Group/D3-Gym找到。

Motivated by the lack of verifiable environments for real-world scientific tasks that hinder progress in data-driven discovery agents, the authors introduce D3-Gym, the first automatically constructed dataset featuring 565 tasks drawn from 239 real scientific repositories across four disciplines. Each task includes a natural language instruction, an executable environment with dependencies, input data, artifact previews, a reference solution, and an auto-generated evaluation script. Experimental results show these evaluation scripts achieve 87.5% agreement with human gold standards and align well with domain-specific logic, validating their scientific soundness. Training agents on D3-Gym trajectories led to consistent performance gains across Qwen3 models, with the 32B variant improving by 7.8 absolute points on ScienceAgentBench and narrowing the gap with proprietary models.

Make Your LVLM KV Cache More Lightweight
Authors: Xihao Chen, Yangyang Guo, Roger Zimmermann
First: 2026-05-01T17:11:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:11:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Key-Value (KV) cache has become a de facto component of modern Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) for inference. While it enhances decoding efficiency in Large Language Models (LLMs), its direct adoption in LVLMs introduces substantial GPU memory overhead due to the large number of vision tokens processed during the prefill stage. To tackle this problem, we propose LightKV, a novel approach that reduces KV cache size by exploiting the redundancy among vision-token embeddings. Guided by text prompts, LightKV employs cross-modality message passing to aggregate informative messages across vision tokens and progressively compress them during prefill. This prompt-aware guidance distinguishes our method from prior vision-only compression strategies. We evaluate LightKV on eight open-source LVLMs across eight public benchmark datasets, e.g., MME and SeedBench. Experimental results demonstrate that with only 55% of the original vision tokens, LightKV (a) halves the vision-token KV cache size, (b) reduces computation by up to 40%, and (c) preserves general-purpose performance while significantly outperforming existing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：让您的LVLM KV缓存更轻量
键值（KV）缓存已成为现代大视觉-语言模型（LVLM）推理的标配组件。虽然它提升了大语言模型（LLM）的解码效率，但在LVLM中直接采用会因预填充阶段处理大量视觉令牌而产生显著的GPU内存开销。为解决此问题，我们提出LightKV，一种通过利用视觉令牌嵌入间的冗余性来压缩KV缓存的新方法。LightKV在文本提示引导下，采用跨模态消息传递机制，对视觉令牌间的信息进行聚合，并在预填充阶段逐步压缩。这种提示感知的引导方式使我们的方法区别于以往仅针对视觉内容的压缩策略。我们在八个开源LVLM和八个公开基准数据集（如MME和SeedBench）上评估LightKV，实验结果表明，仅使用原视觉令牌的55%，LightKV可（a）将视觉令牌KV缓存大小减半，（b）降低计算量高达40%，（c）在保持通用性能的同时显著优于现有基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the excessive GPU memory overhead caused by large vision-token KV caches in LVLMs during prefill, the authors propose LightKV, a prompt-aware compression method that reduces KV cache size by aggregating and compressing vision-token embeddings via cross-modality message passing. Evaluated across eight LVLMs and benchmarks including MME and SeedBench, LightKV achieves a 50% reduction in vision-token KV cache size using only 55% of original tokens, cuts computation by up to 40%, and maintains or improves general-purpose performance over existing baselines.
为缓解LVLM推理中因大量视觉token导致的GPU内存开销，作者提出LightKV方法，通过跨模态消息传递，在提示引导下对视觉token嵌入进行聚合压缩。在八款LVLM及多个基准数据集上的实验表明，LightKV仅用55%的原始视觉token即可将视觉token KV缓存大小减半，计算量降低最高达40%，同时保持甚至超越现有基线模型的通用性能。
SAVGO: Learning State-Action Value Geometry with Cosine Similarity for Continuous Control
Authors: Stavros Orfanoudakis, Pedro P. Vergara
First: 2026-05-01T17:09:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:09:58+00:00
Comments: Reinforcement Learning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While representation and similarity learning have improved the sample efficiency of Reinforcement Learning (RL), they are rarely used to shape policy updates directly in the action space. To bridge this gap, a geometry-aware RL algorithm that explicitly incorporates value-based similarity into the policy update, State-Action Value Geometry Optimization (SAVGO), is proposed. In detail, SAVGO learns a joint state-action embedding space in which pairs with similar action-value estimates exhibit high cosine similarity, while dissimilar pairs are mapped to distinct directions. This learned geometry enables the generation of a similarity kernel over candidate actions sampled at each update, allowing policy improvement to be guided directly toward higher-value regions beyond local gradient-based updates. As a result, representation learning, value estimation, and policy optimization are unified within a single geometry-consistent objective, while preserving the scalability of off-policy actor-critic training. The proposed method is evaluated on standard MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks, demonstrating improvements over strong baselines on challenging high-dimensional tasks. Ablation studies are done to analyze the contributions of value-geometry learning and similarity-based policy updates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAVGO：利用余弦相似度学习状态-动作价值几何结构以实现连续控制
尽管表征与相似性学习已提升强化学习（RL）的样本效率，但它们很少直接用于塑造动作空间中的策略更新。为弥合这一差距，本文提出一种几何感知的RL算法——状态-动作价值几何优化（SAVGO），该算法显式将基于价值的相似性融入策略更新。具体而言，SAVGO学习一个联合状态-动作嵌入空间，其中具有相似动作价值估计的对在该空间中表现出高余弦相似度，而不同对则映射到不同方向。这种学习到的几何结构可生成候选动作在每次更新时的相似性核，从而直接引导策略改进至更高价值区域，超越局部梯度更新。因此，表征学习、价值估计与策略优化统一于单一几何一致目标中，同时保留了离策略演员-评论家训练的可扩展性。该方法在标准MuJoCo连续控制基准上进行了评估，在高维挑战性任务上优于强基线。通过消融研究分析了价值几何学习与基于相似性的策略更新的贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to directly leverage value-based similarity in policy updates for continuous control, SAVGO introduces a geometry-aware RL algorithm that learns a joint state-action embedding space where cosine similarity reflects action-value similarity. By mapping high-value action pairs to similar directions and low-value pairs to distinct ones, SAVGO constructs a similarity kernel over sampled actions to guide policy updates toward higher-value regions, bypassing local gradient-based methods. Evaluated on MuJoCo benchmarks, SAVGO outperforms strong baselines on high-dimensional tasks, with ablation studies confirming the effectiveness of both value-geometry learning and similarity-guided policy updates.
为提升连续控制任务中策略更新的效率，SAVGO提出一种几何感知的强化学习算法，通过学习联合状态-动作嵌入空间，使具有相似动作价值的对映射到高余弦相似度方向，而价值差异大的对则映射到不同方向。该几何结构用于构建动作候选集上的相似性核，引导策略更新直接朝高价值区域优化，超越传统梯度方法。在MuJoCo基准测试中，SAVGO在高维任务上优于多个强基线，消融实验验证了价值几何学习与相似性引导策略更新的重要性。
Error-free Training for MedMNIST Datasets
Authors: Bo Deng
First: 2026-04-20T23:37:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T17:07:43+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 figure, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we introduce a new concept called Artificial Special Intelligence by which Machine Learning models for the classification problem can be trained error-free, thus acquiring the capability of not making repeated mistakes. The method is applied to 18 MedMNIST biomedical datasets. Except for three datasets, which suffer from the double-labeling problem, all are trained to perfection.

Motivated by the desire to achieve error-free classification in medical imaging, this paper proposes a method termed Artificial Special Intelligence that enables machine learning models to be trained without errors on 18 MedMNIST biomedical datasets. The approach successfully achieves perfect accuracy on 15 of the 18 datasets, excluding three that suffer from double-labeling issues. Experimental results demonstrate that the method can eliminate repeated mistakes in classification, thereby enhancing model reliability in medical applications.
