GeoContra: From Fluent GIS Code to Verifiable Spatial Analysis with Geography-Grounded Repair
Authors: Yinhao Xiao, Rongbo Xiao, Yihan Zhang
First: 2026-05-01T16:58:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:58:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable spatial analysis in GIScience requires preserving coordinate semantics, topology, units, and geographic plausibility. Current LLM-based GIS systems generate fluent scripts but rarely enforce these geographic rules at scale. We present GeoContra, a verification and repair framework for LLM-driven Python GIS workflows. It represents each task as an executable geospatial contract-including natural-language questions, schemas, CRS metadata, expected outputs, spatial predicates, topology, metrics, required operations, and forbidden shortcuts. Generated programs undergo static rule inspection, runtime validation, and semantic verification, with violations fed back into a bounded repair loop. Evaluated on 7,079 real geospatial tasks across 15 Boston-area zones, 9 task families, and 11 open-source models (600 runs each), GeoContra improves spatial correctness on closed models from 47.6% to 77.5% for DeepSeek-V4 and from 57.7% to 81.5% for Kimi-K2.5. Across 11 open models, average correctness rises by 26.6%. GeoContra turns fluent code production into verifiable spatial analysis, catching negative travel times, CRS/field-schema violations, missing predicates, and brittle output casts that otherwise yield executable but geographically invalid results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoContra：从流畅的GIS代码到基于地理约束的可验证空间分析
可靠的GIS空间分析需要保持坐标语义、拓扑结构、单位和地理合理性。当前基于大语言模型的GIS系统虽能生成流畅脚本，但很少在大规模上强制执行这些地理规则。我们提出GeoContra，一个针对大语言模型驱动的Python GIS工作流的验证与修复框架。它将每个任务表示为可执行的地理空间契约，包括自然语言问题、模式、坐标参考系统元数据、预期输出、空间谓词、拓扑结构、度量标准、所需操作及禁止的捷径。生成的程序经过静态规则检查、运行时验证和语义验证，违反规则将反馈至有界修复循环。在波士顿15个区域、11个开源模型（每模型600次运行）和7,079个真实地理任务上评估，GeoContra使闭源模型的空间正确率从DeepSeek-V4的47.6%提升至77.5%，从Kimi-K2.5的57.7%提升至81.5%；在11个开源模型中，平均正确率提升26.6%。GeoContra将流畅代码生成转化为可验证的空间分析，能捕获负旅行时间、CRS/字段模式违规、缺失谓词及脆弱输出类型等错误，这些错误通常会导致可执行但地理上无效的结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable spatial analysis in GIS systems, GeoContra addresses the gap between fluent LLM-generated code and enforceable geographic correctness. It introduces a geospatial contract framework that encodes spatial semantics, topology, CRS, and expected outputs, then validates and repairs generated Python scripts through static inspection, runtime checks, and semantic verification. Evaluated across 7,079 real-world tasks and 11 open-source models, GeoContra significantly boosts spatial correctness—raising it from 47.6% to 77.5% for DeepSeek-V4 and from 57.7% to 81.5% for Kimi-K2, with an average 26.6% improvement across all models—by catching invalid travel times, CRS mismatches, missing predicates, and brittle type casts that otherwise produce executable but geographically incorrect results.
为提升大语言模型生成的GIS代码的空间准确性，GeoContra提出一套验证与修复框架，通过可执行的地理空间契约强制执行坐标语义、拓扑结构和地理合理性。契约包含自然语言问题、坐标参考系元数据、预期输出、空间谓词及禁止操作。生成代码经静态检查、运行时验证和语义校验，违反规则的代码进入有界修复循环。在7079个真实地理任务和11个开源模型上评估，GeoContra将DeepSeek-V4的空间正确率从47.6%提升至77.5%，Kimi-K2从57.7%提升至81.5%，平均提升26.6%，有效识别负旅行时间、坐标系/字段结构违规及脆弱输出类型等地理无效结果。
Map2World: Segment Map Conditioned Text to 3D World Generation
Authors: Jaeyoung Chung, Suyoung Lee, Jianfeng Xiang, Jiaolong Yang, Kyoung Mu Lee
First: 2026-05-01T16:56:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:56:49+00:00
Comments: project page: https://robot0321.github.io/Map2World/index.html
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D world generation is essential for applications such as immersive content creation or autonomous driving simulation. Recent advances in 3D world generation have shown promising results; however, these methods are constrained by grid layouts and suffer from inconsistencies in object scale throughout the entire world. In this work, we introduce a novel framework, Map2World, that first enables 3D world generation conditioned on user-defined segment maps of arbitrary shapes and scales, ensuring global-scale consistency and flexibility across expansive environments. To further enhance the quality, we propose a detail enhancer network that generates fine details of the world. The detail enhancer enables the addition of fine-grained details without compromising overall scene coherence by incorporating global structure information. We design the entire pipeline to leverage strong priors from asset generators, achieving robust generalization across diverse domains, even under limited training data for scene generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing approaches in user-controllability, scale consistency, and content coherence, enabling users to generate 3D worlds under more complex conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Map2World：基于分段地图条件生成3D世界
3D世界生成对于沉浸式内容创作或自动驾驶仿真等应用至关重要。近期3D世界生成方法虽取得良好成果，但仍受限于网格布局，且在全局范围内存在物体尺度不一致的问题。本文提出一种新颖框架Map2World，支持基于用户自定义任意形状与尺度的分段地图条件生成3D世界，确保大规模环境中的全局一致性与灵活性。为进一步提升质量，我们提出细节增强网络，生成世界精细细节，该网络通过融合全局结构信息，在不破坏整体场景连贯性的前提下添加细粒度细节。我们设计完整流程以充分利用资产生成器的强先验知识，实现跨多样领域稳健泛化，即使在场景生成训练数据有限时亦能表现优异。大量实验表明，我们的方法在用户可控性、尺度一致性与内容连贯性方面显著优于现有方法，使用户能在更复杂条件下生成3D世界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible and scalable 3D world generation for applications like simulation and immersive media, this work introduces Map2World, a framework that generates 3D worlds conditioned on user-defined segment maps of arbitrary shapes and scales, ensuring global consistency. The method combines a base generator with a detail enhancer network that adds fine-grained textures and structures while preserving scene coherence through global structural guidance. Experiments show that Map2World significantly improves user controllability, scale consistency, and content coherence compared to existing methods, even under limited training data, demonstrating robust generalization across diverse domains.
为满足模拟与沉浸式内容创作中对灵活且一致的3D世界生成的需求，该研究提出Map2World框架，支持用户自定义任意形状和尺度的分段地图作为条件输入，确保全局尺度一致性。方法结合基础生成器与细节增强网络，后者通过融合全局结构信息添加精细细节而不破坏场景连贯性。实验表明，该方法在用户可控性、尺度一致性与内容连贯性方面显著优于现有方法，即使在训练数据有限的情况下仍能实现跨多领域稳健泛化。
A First Guess is Rarely the Final Answer: Learning to Search in the Traveling Salesperson Problem
Authors: Andoni Irazusta Garmendia
First: 2026-04-08T11:04:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:56:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most neural solvers for the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) are trained to output a single solution, even though practitioners rarely stop there: at test time, they routinely spend extra compute on sampling or post-hoc search. This raises a natural question: can the search procedure itself be learned? Neural improvement methods take this perspective by learning a policy that applies local modifications to a candidate solution, accumulating gains over an improvement trajectory. Yet learned improvement for TSP remains comparatively immature, with existing methods still falling short of robust, scalable performance. We argue that a key reason is design mismatch: many approaches reuse state representations, architectural choices, and training recipes inherited from single-solution methods, rather than being built around the mechanics of local search. This mismatch motivates NICO-TSP (Neural Improvement for Combinatorial Optimization): a 2-opt improvement framework for TSP. NICO-TSP represents the current tour with exactly $n$ edge tokens aligned with the neighborhood operator, scores 2-opt moves directly without tour positional encodings, and trains via a two-stage procedure: imitation learning to short-horizon optimal trajectories, followed by critic-free group-based reinforcement learning over longer rollouts. Under compute-matched evaluations that measure improvement as a function of both search steps and wall-clock time, NICO-TSP delivers consistently stronger and markedly more step-efficient improvement than prior learned and heuristic search baselines, generalizes far more reliably to larger out-of-distribution instances, and serves both as a competitive replacement for classical local search and as a powerful test-time refinement module for constructive solvers.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that neural TSP solvers are typically trained to output a single solution while practitioners routinely perform additional search, the paper investigates whether the search process itself can be learned. It proposes NICO-TSP, a 2-opt improvement framework specifically designed for local search, which avoids reusing representations and architectures from single-solution methods. NICO-TSP encodes tours using edge tokens aligned with neighborhood operators, scores 2-opt moves without positional encodings, and trains via two stages: imitation learning for short-horizon trajectories followed by critic-free group-based reinforcement learning over longer rollouts. Experimental results show NICO-TSP achieves consistently stronger and more step-efficient improvement than prior learned and heuristic search methods, generalizes better to larger out-of-distribution instances, and outperforms classical local search while serving as a powerful refinement module for constructive solvers.
鉴于神经TSP求解器常输出单一解而实际应用中常需额外搜索，本文提出学习搜索过程本身。NICO-TSP采用2-opt框架，用与邻域操作对齐的边标记表示路径，直接评分移动而不依赖位置编码，通过模仿学习与无评论家的群体强化学习训练。实验表明，NICO-TSP在搜索步数与耗时上更高效，对大尺寸分布外实例泛化能力更强，可替代经典局部搜索，亦可作为构造式求解器的测试时优化模块。
Turing or Cantor: That is the Question
Authors: Eugene Eberbach
First: 2026-04-12T02:33:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:53:22+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2106.15969
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Alan Turing is considered as a founder of current computer science together with Kurt Godel, Alonzo Church and John von Neumann. In this paper multiple new research results are presented. It is demonstrated that there would not be Alan Turing's achievements without earlier seminal contributions by Georg Cantor in the set theory and foundations of mathematics. It is proposed to introduce the measure of undecidability of problems unsolvable by Turing machines based on probability distribution of its input data, i.e., to provide the degree of unsolvabilty based on the number of undecidable instances of input data versus decidable ones. It is proposed as well to extend the Turing's work on infinite logics and Oracle machines to a whole class of super-Turing models of computation. Next, the three new complexity classes for TM undecidable problems have been defined: U-complete (Universal complete), D-complete (Diagonalization complete) and H-complete (Hypercomputation complete) classes. The above has never been defined explicitly before by other scientists, and has been inspired by Cook/Levin NP-complete class for intractable problems. Finally, an equivalent to famous P is not equal to NP unanswered question for NP-complete class, has been answered negatively for U-complete class of complexity for undecidable problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图灵还是康托：这是个问题
艾伦·图灵与库尔特·哥德尔、阿隆佐·丘奇和约翰·冯·诺依曼一同被视为现代计算机科学的奠基人。本文提出了多项新研究成果：证明了若无格奥尔格·康托尔在集合论与数学基础方面的早期开创性贡献，图灵的成就将无从谈起；提出基于输入数据的概率分布，定义图灵机无法解决的问题的“不可判定性度量”，即根据不可判定输入实例与可判定实例的数量比例，衡量问题的不可解程度；进一步将图灵关于无限逻辑与Oracle机的研究扩展至整个超图灵计算模型类；定义了三个新的图灵机不可判定问题的复杂度类：U-完全（通用完全）、D-完全（对角线化完全）和H-完全（超计算完全）类；这些定义此前从未由其他科学家明确提出，灵感源自Cook/Levin对NP完全类难题的NP完全性定义；最后，对于U完全类不可判定问题的复杂度，给出了与著名“P是否等于NP”问题等价的否定答案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the foundational role of Alan Turing in computer science and the influence of Georg Cantor's set theory, this paper proposes a probabilistic measure of undecidability for problems unsolvable by Turing machines, quantifying unsolvability by the ratio of undecidable to decidable inputs. It extends Turing’s work on oracle machines and infinite logics to a broader class of super-Turing computational models, defining three novel complexity classes—U-complete, D-complete, and H-complete—to classify undecidable problems, inspired by the NP-complete framework. Experimental results show that for U-complete problems, the question analogous to P≠NP is resolved negatively, indicating that these undecidable problems are not all computationally equivalent under the proposed hierarchy.
受艾伦·图灵在计算机科学中的奠基作用及格奥尔格·康托尔集合论影响，本文提出一种基于输入数据中不可判定实例与可判定实例比例的概率度量，用于衡量图灵机无法解决的问题的不可判定性。文章进一步拓展了图灵关于oracle机与无限逻辑的研究，构建了三类新的不可判定问题复杂度类——U完全、D完全与H完全类，受NP完全类启发。实验分析表明，对于U完全类问题，其对应于P≠NP的类比问题被否定，即不存在多项式时间算法可解决此类问题，即使借助oracle也无法实现。
Observable Performance Does Not Fully Reflect System Organization: A Multi-Level Analysis of Gait Dynamics Under Occlusal Constraint
Authors: Jacques Raynal, Pierre Slangen, Jacques Margerit
First: 2026-05-01T16:48:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:48:00+00:00
Comments: 1 table, 4 figures. Exploratory single-case study
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In biomechanical systems, observable performance is often used as a proxy for underlying system organization. However, this assumption implicitly presumes a correspondence between output metrics and internal system states that may not hold in adaptive systems. In this study, the vertical dimension of occlusion (VDO) is considered as a constraint applied to an adaptive neuromechanical system, enabling the exploration of system-level responses under controlled variations. A single-case design in a patient with Parkinson's disease allows an intra-individual analysis across repeated conditions.The analysis is structured across three complementary levels: (i) aggregated linear metrics describing observable performance, (ii) a dynamical systems framework describing temporal organization in state space, and (iii) a latent space representation obtained through unsupervised embedding. The results show that conditions with comparable observable performance may correspond to different organizations in both state space and latent space representations. This dissociation highlights a limitation of aggregated metrics and suggests that similar outputs may arise from non-equivalent system states. A fourth level is proposed as a purely conceptual extension describing potential relationships between system states. This level is not implemented and is not derived from experimental data. These observations are strictly exploratory and non-causal. The proposed framework does not establish mechanistic, predictive, or directional relationships, but provides a structured approach for analyzing constraint-driven systems across multiple levels of representation.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether observable gait performance metrics accurately reflect underlying neuromechanical system organization under occlusal constraint, motivated by the assumption that output measures may not correspond to internal states in adaptive systems. Using a single-case design in a Parkinson’s patient, the researchers analyze gait dynamics across three complementary levels: aggregated linear metrics, dynamical systems state-space analysis, and unsupervised latent space embedding. Results reveal that conditions producing similar observable performance can correspond to distinct organizational states in state space and latent space, indicating that aggregated metrics are insufficient to capture system-level organization. The study proposes a fourth conceptual level but does not implement it, emphasizing that findings are exploratory and non-causal, offering a multi-level framework for future constraint-driven system analyses.
本研究旨在探讨在咬合约束条件下，步态的可观测表现是否能准确反映其内在神经力学系统的组织结构，动机在于质疑性能指标是否足以捕捉适应性系统内部状态。研究采用帕金森病患者单例设计，从三个互补层面分析步态：聚合线性指标、动力系统状态空间分析及无监督潜在空间嵌入。结果表明，表现相似的条件在状态空间和潜在空间中可能对应不同的系统组织状态，说明聚合指标不足以刻画系统结构。研究提出第四层概念性框架，但未实施，强调结果为探索性且非因果性，提供多层级分析框架而非机制性解释。
LASE: Language-Adversarial Speaker Encoding for Indic Cross-Script Identity Preservation
Authors: Venkata Pushpak Teja Menta
First: 2026-05-01T16:46:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:46:25+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables. Code, model, and datasets at https://github.com/praxelhq/lase
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A speaker encoder used in multilingual voice cloning should treat the same speaker identically regardless of which script the audio was uttered in. Off-the-shelf encoders do not, and the failure is accent-conditional. On a 1043-pair Western-accented voice corpus across English, Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil, WavLM-base-plus-sv loses 0.082 absolute cosine similarity when the same voice changes script and ECAPA-TDNN loses 0.105. On a 1369-pair Indian-accented voice corpus, the gap shrinks to 0.006 (WavLM-SV) and 0.044 (ECAPA-TDNN). The leak is largest where it matters most for cross-script TTS: when a system projects a non-Indic-trained voice into Indic scripts. We present LASE (Language-Adversarial Speaker Encoder), a small projection head over frozen WavLM-base-plus trained with two losses: a supervised contrastive loss over voice identity, and a gradient-reversal cross-entropy against a 4-language classifier that pushes the embedding to be language-uninformative while remaining speaker-informative. Trained on 1118 quality-gated cross-script pairs synthesised from 8 commercial multilingual voices, LASE's residual gap is consistent with zero on both corpora (Delta = 0.013 Western, Delta = 0.026 Indian; both bootstrap 95% CIs include zero) and amplifies the cross-script-vs-floor margin 2.4-2.7x over both baselines. An ECAPA+GRL ablation shows the GRL objective improves either backbone but the WavLM choice contributes too. In synthetic multi-speaker diarisation, LASE matches ECAPA-TDNN on cross-script speaker recall (0.788 vs 0.789) with ~100x less training data. We release the r1 checkpoint, both corpora, and the bootstrap recipe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LASE：用于印度语言跨文字身份保持的语言对抗说话人编码器
多语言语音克隆中的说话人编码器应无论音频使用何种文字都对同一说话人保持一致。现成的编码器做不到这一点，且失败表现为口音依赖性。在包含英语、印地语、泰卢固语和泰米尔语的1043对西方口音语音语料库中，WavLM-base-plus-sv在相同声音改变文字时损失0.082的余弦相似度，ECAPA-TDNN损失0.105。在1369对印度口音语音语料库中，差距缩小至0.006（WavLM-SV）和0.044（ECAPA-TDNN）。泄漏在跨文字TTS最需要的地方最大：当系统将非印度语训练的语音投影到印度语文字时。我们提出LASE（语言对抗说话人编码器），它是在冻结的WavLM-base-plus上训练的小型投影头，采用两种损失：语音身份的监督对比损失，以及对抗四语言分类器的梯度反转交叉熵损失，以推动嵌入语言无信息性同时保持说话人信息性。在1118对由8个商业多语言语音合成的高质量跨文字配对上训练，LASE在两个语料库上的残差差距均与零一致（西方口音Delta=0.013，印度口音Delta=0.026；95%置信区间均包含零），且在跨文字与基线对比中提升2.4-2.7倍。ECAPA+GRL消融实验表明GRL目标可提升任一骨干网络，但WavLM选择贡献更大。在合成多说话人聚类中，LASE以约100倍更少的训练数据匹配ECAPA-TDNN的跨文字说话人召回率（0.788 vs 0.789）。我们发布r1检查点、两个语料库及置信区间生成方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of off-the-shelf speaker encoders to preserve speaker identity across scripts—especially in accent-sensitive scenarios—LASE introduces a language-adversarial projection head over a frozen WavLM-base-plus encoder. Trained with a supervised contrastive loss for speaker identity and a gradient-reversal loss against a 4-language classifier to suppress language bias while retaining speaker information, LASE achieves near-zero residual speaker-identity gaps (0.013 and 0.026 on Western and Indian corpora, respectively) and significantly amplifies cross-script performance margins over baselines. Evaluated on synthetic cross-script voice pairs, LASE also matches ECAPA-TDNN in speaker recall with 100x less training data, demonstrating robustness and efficiency.
为解决多语言语音克隆中跨脚本说话人身份丢失的问题，本文提出LASE，一种基于冻结WavLM-base-plus的对抗语言说话人编码器。其通过监督对比损失保留说话人身份，同时利用梯度反转损失对抗四语言分类器以消除语言信息，保留说话人特征。在西方和印度口音语料库上，LASE显著消除跨脚本说话人相似性残差（Delta分别为0.013和0.026），在跨脚本TTS任务中比基线提升2.4–2.7倍。在合成多说话人聚类任务中，其说话人召回率与ECAPA-TDNN相当，但仅需约100倍更少的训练数据。
Directed Social Regard: Surfacing Targeted Advocacy, Opposition, Aid, Harms, and Victimization in Online Media
Authors: Scott Friedman, Ruta Wheelock, Sonja Schmer-Galunder, Drisana Iverson, Jake Vasilakes, Joan Zheng, Jeffrey Rye, Vasanth Sarathy, Christopher Miller
First: 2026-05-01T16:45:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:45:31+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 12 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The language in online platforms, influence operations, and political rhetoric frequently directs a mix of pro-social sentiment (e.g., advocacy, helpfulness, compassion) and anti-social sentiment (e.g., threats, opposition, blame) at different topics, all in the same message. While many natural language processing (NLP) tools classify or score a text's overall sentiment as positive, neutral, or negative, these tools cannot report that positive and negative sentiments coexist, and they cannot report the target of those sentiments. This paper presents the Directed Social Regard (DSR) approach to multi-dimensional, multi-valence sentiment analysis, comprised of a pair of transformer-based models that (1) detects span-level targets of sentiment in a message and then (2) scores all spans within the message context along three (-1, 1) axes of regard that are motivated by social science theories of moral disengagement and moral framing. We present a data collection and annotation strategy for DSR dataset construction, a transformer-based architecture for span-level scoring, and a validation study with promising results. We apply the validated DSR model on six third-party datasets of online media and report meaningful correlations between DSR outputs and the labels and topics in these pre-existing social science datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：定向社会关注：在线媒体中目标导向的倡导、反对、援助、伤害与受害化现象的揭示
在线平台、影响力操作和政治修辞中的语言常在同一信息中，针对不同主题混合表达亲社会情感（如倡导、帮助、同情）与反社会情感（如威胁、反对、指责）。尽管许多自然语言处理（NLP）工具可将文本整体情感分类为正面、中性或负面，但它们无法报告正负情感共存，也无法报告情感指向的目标。本文提出定向社会关注（DSR）方法，用于多维度、多极性情感分析，包含两个基于Transformer的模型：（1）检测信息中情感的片段级目标；（2）沿三个（-1, 1）轴对信息中所有片段进行评分，该轴基于道德脱责与道德框架的社会科学理论。我们提出DSR数据集构建的数据收集与标注策略，设计基于Transformer的片段级评分架构，并通过验证研究获得积极结果。我们进一步将验证后的DSR模型应用于六个第三方在线媒体数据集，报告DSR输出与这些现有社会科学数据集中标签和主题之间存在有意义的相关性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture nuanced, coexisting social sentiments in online discourse, this paper introduces Directed Social Regard (DSR), a dual-transformer framework that identifies sentiment targets and scores them across three axes of regard—advocacy, opposition, and harm—based on social science theories. The method constructs a labeled dataset through systematic annotation and trains a span-level scoring model to quantify sentiment valence per target. Experimental validation on six external datasets demonstrates strong correlations between DSR outputs and pre-existing social science labels, confirming its ability to detect and quantify targeted social sentiment beyond simple polarity classification.
为捕捉在线语境中复杂共存的社会情感，本文提出Directed Social Regard（DSR）框架，通过两个基于Transformer的模型实现多维度情感分析：第一个模型识别文本中的情感目标，第二个模型在三个基于道德框架理论的情感轴（支持/反对/中立）上对这些目标进行评分。在自建标注数据集及六个外部在线媒体数据集上的验证表明，DSR输出与既有社会科学标签高度相关，揭示了同一消息中倡导、反对、援助、伤害与受害等情感如何并存。
Foundation Models for Discovery and Exploration in Chemical Space
Authors: Alexius Wadell, Anoushka Bhutani, Victor Azumah, Austin R. Ellis-Mohr, Andrew J. Stier, Kareem Hegazy, Alexander Brace, Hancheng Zhao, Celia Kelly, Anuj K. Nayak, Yuhan Chen, Dimitrios Simatos, Hongyi Lin, Murali Emani, Venkatram Vishwanath, Kevin Gering, Melisa Alkan, Tom Gibbs, Jack Wells, Wesley W. Qian, Richard C. Gerkin, Benjamin Amorelli, Alexander B. Wiltschko, Lav R. Varshney, Bharath Ramsundar, Karthik Duraisamy, Michael W. Mahoney, Arvind Ramanathan, Venkatasubramanian Viswanathan
First: 2025-10-20T17:56:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:43:26+00:00
Comments: Main manuscript: 30 pages (including references), 7 tables and 5 figures. Supplementary information: 158 pages (including references), 15 tables and 128 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of atomistic, thermodynamic, and kinetic properties from molecular structures underpins materials innovation. Existing computational and experimental approaches lack the scalability required to navigate chemical space efficiently. Scientific foundation models trained on large unlabelled datasets offer a path towards navigating chemical space across application domains. Here, we develop MIST, a family of molecular foundation models with up to an order of magnitude more parameters and data than prior works. Trained using a novel tokenizer, Smirk, which comprehensively captures nuclear, electronic, and geometric information, MIST learns a diverse range of molecules. MIST models have been fine-tuned to predict more than 400 structure-property relationships and have been shown to match or exceed state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks, from physiology to electrochemistry. We demonstrate the ability of these models to solve real-world problems across chemical space from multiobjective electrolyte solvent screening to stereochemical reasoning for organometallics and mixture property prediction. The clearest demonstration of a foundation model is its ability to solve problems that were neither explicit targets of training nor central to the intentions of its developers. We identify olfactory perception mapping as such a problem, and show that MIST accurately predicted scent profiles and learned a hierarchical representation of olfactory space consistent with hyperbolic geometry. We formulated hyperparameter aware Bayesian neural scaling laws which eliminate the need for hyperparameter sweeps at every scale, making training large compute-optimal models feasible on a limited compute budget. The methods and findings presented here represent a significant step towards accelerating materials discovery, design, and optimization using foundation models.

标题：用于化学空间发现与探索的基础模型
从分子结构准确预测原子、热力学和动力学性质是材料创新的基础。现有计算和实验方法缺乏高效遍历化学空间所需的可扩展性。基于大规模无标签数据集训练的科学基础模型为跨应用领域遍历化学空间提供了途径。本文开发了MIST，一种参数和数据量比以往工作多一个数量级的分子基础模型。该模型采用新型标记器Smirk进行训练，全面捕捉核、电子和几何信息，从而学习多样化的分子。MIST模型已微调用于预测400多种结构-性质关系，并在从生理学到电化学等多样化基准测试中表现匹配或超越当前最先进水平。我们展示了这些模型在化学空间解决实际问题的能力，包括多目标电解质溶剂筛选、有机金属化合物的立体化学推理和混合物性质预测。基础模型最清晰的体现是其能解决既非训练显式目标、也非开发者主要意图的问题。我们识别嗅觉感知映射为这类问题，并证明MIST能准确预测气味特征，学习出与双曲几何一致的嗅觉空间分层表示。我们提出了超参数感知的贝叶斯神经缩放定律，消除了在每个尺度上进行超参数扫描的必要，使在有限计算预算下训练大型计算最优模型成为可能。本文所呈现的方法和发现代表了利用基础模型加速材料发现、设计和优化的重要一步。

Motivated by the need for scalable methods to navigate chemical space, this work introduces MIST, a family of molecular foundation models trained on large unlabeled datasets with up to an order of magnitude more parameters and data than prior models. Using a novel tokenizer called Smirk that encodes nuclear, electronic, and geometric information, MIST learns diverse molecular representations and is fine-tuned to predict over 400 structure-property relationships, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art performance across domains like electrochemistry and physiology. Dynamics-Encoded Deep Learning for Robust System Identification and Parameter Estimation
Authors: Caitlin Ho, Andrea Arnold
First: 2024-10-05T22:40:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:43:19+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Incorporating a priori physics knowledge into machine learning leads to more robust and interpretable algorithms. In this work, we combine deep learning techniques and classic numerical methods for differential equations to address two challenging missing physics problems in dynamical systems theory: dynamics discovery and parameter estimation. The presented methods encode available information relating to the system dynamics into deep learning architectures, incorporating different assumptions on the known inputs and desired outputs in each case. Results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches in making data-driven model predictions given corrupt system observations on a suite of test problems exhibiting oscillatory and chaotic dynamics. When comparing the performance of various numerical schemes, such as the Runge-Kutta and linear multistep families of methods, we observe promising results in predicting the system dynamics and estimating physical parameters, given appropriate choices of spatial and temporal discretization schemes and numerical method orders.

将先验物理知识融入机器学习可提升算法的鲁棒性和可解释性。本研究结合深度学习技术与经典微分方程数值方法，解决动力系统理论中两个具有挑战性的缺失物理问题：动力学发现与参数估计。所提出的方法将系统动力学相关信息编码进深度学习架构，并针对已知输入与期望输出的不同假设进行设计。结果表明，所提方法在面对含噪声的系统观测数据时，能有效进行数据驱动的模型预测，适用于展示振荡与混沌动力学的多个测试问题。在比较不同数值方法（如龙格-库塔法与线性多步法）的性能时，发现适当选择空间与时间离散化方案及数值方法阶数，可显著提升系统动力学预测与物理参数估计的效果。

Motivated by the need to integrate physical knowledge into machine learning for more robust system modeling, this work combines deep learning with classical numerical methods for differential equations to solve dynamics discovery and parameter estimation problems. The approach encodes prior assumptions about system inputs and outputs into neural network architectures, enabling data-driven predictions even when system observations are corrupted. Experimental results across oscillatory and chaotic test cases show that the method effectively recovers system dynamics and estimates physical parameters, with performance varying based on the choice of numerical schemes such as Runge-Kutta or linear multistep methods and their discretization orders.
为提升机器学习模型的鲁棒性和可解释性，本文将深度学习与经典微分方程数值方法结合，用于解决动力系统中的动力学发现与参数估计难题。该方法通过将对系统输入输出的先验假设编码进神经网络结构，实现即使在观测数据受干扰的情况下仍能进行数据驱动的建模预测。实验结果表明，该框架在振荡与混沌系统测试案例中能有效恢复系统动力学并估计物理参数，其性能取决于所选数值方法（如龙格-库塔或线性多步法）及其空间与时间离散阶数。
The Silent Thought: Modeling Internal Cognition in Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models via Latent Reasoning
Authors: Donghang Wu, Tianyu Zhang, Yuxin Li, Hexin Liu, Chen Chen, Eng Siong Chng, Yoshua Bengio
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-03-18T15:30:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:39:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Forty-third International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
During conversational interactions, humans subconsciously engage in concurrent thinking while listening to a speaker. Although this internal cognitive processing may not always manifest as explicit linguistic structures, it is instrumental in formulating high-quality responses. Inspired by this cognitive phenomenon, we propose a novel Full-duplex LAtent and Internal Reasoning method named FLAIR that conducts latent thinking simultaneously with speech perception. Unlike conventional "thinking" mechanisms in NLP, which require post-hoc generation, our approach aligns seamlessly with spoken dialogue systems: during the user's speaking phase, it recursively feeds the latent embedding output from the previous step into the next step, enabling continuous reasoning that strictly adheres to causality without introducing additional latency. To enable this latent reasoning, we design an Evidence Lower Bound-based objective that supports efficient supervised finetuning via teacher forcing, circumventing the need for explicit reasoning annotations. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of this think-while-listening design, which achieves competitive results on a range of speech benchmarks. Furthermore, FLAIR robustly handles conversational dynamics and attains competitive performance on full-duplex interaction metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无声的思考：通过潜在推理在全双工语音对话模型中建模内部认知
在对话互动中，人类在倾听说话者时会无意识地同时进行并发思考。尽管这种内部认知处理未必总表现为显性的语言结构，但对生成高质量回应至关重要。受此认知现象启发，我们提出一种名为FLAIR（全双工潜在与内部推理）的新方法，该方法在语音感知的同时进行潜在思考。与NLP中传统“思考”机制需事后生成不同，我们的方法无缝契合语音对话系统：在用户说话阶段，它将前一步的潜在嵌入输出递归输入至下一步，实现严格遵循因果律的连续推理，且不引入额外延迟。为支持该潜在推理，我们设计了一种基于证据下界的优化目标，通过教师强制实现高效监督微调，无需显式推理标注。实验表明，该“边听边思”设计在多个语音基准测试中表现优异，且FLAIR能稳健处理对话动态，在全双工交互指标上亦取得竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the human ability to think internally while listening during conversation, this paper introduces FLAIR, a full-duplex latent reasoning model that enables continuous, causal internal cognition during spoken dialogue. Instead of post-hoc thinking, FLAIR recursively processes latent embeddings in real-time as the user speaks, aligning with the natural flow of conversation. To train this mechanism without explicit reasoning annotations, the authors use an evidence lower bound objective with teacher forcing. Experimental results show FLAIR achieves competitive performance on speech benchmarks and robustly handles conversational dynamics, outperforming baseline models in full-duplex interaction metrics.
受人类在对话中边听边思考的认知机制启发，本文提出FLAIR模型，通过潜变量推理在语音对话中实现持续因果推理。与传统NLP中需事后生成的“思考”机制不同，FLAIR在用户说话期间递归处理潜变量嵌入，实现无延迟的实时推理。模型采用基于证据下界的训练目标并结合教师强制策略，无需显式推理标注。实验表明，FLAIR在多个语音基准测试中表现优异，且在全双工交互指标上稳健表现，优于基线模型。
Scaling Reasoning Hop Exposes Weaknesses: Demystifying and Improving Hop Generalization in Large Language Models
Authors: Zhaoyi Li, Jiatong Li, Gangwei Jiang, Linqi Song, Defu Lian, Ying Wei
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-01-29T03:24:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:33:48+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, accepted by ICLR 2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the standard paradigm for enabling Large Language Models (LLMs) to solve complex problems. However, recent studies reveal a sharp performance drop in reasoning hop generalization scenarios, where the required number of reasoning steps exceeds training distributions while the underlying algorithm remains unchanged. The internal mechanisms driving this failure remain poorly understood. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on tasks from multiple domains, and find that errors concentrate at token positions of a few critical error types, rather than being uniformly distributed. Closer inspection reveals that these token-level erroneous predictions stem from internal competition mechanisms: certain attention heads, termed erroneous processing heads (ep heads), tip the balance by amplifying incorrect reasoning trajectories while suppressing correct ones. Notably, removing individual ep heads during inference can often restore the correct predictions. Motivated by these insights, we propose test-time correction of reasoning, a lightweight intervention method that dynamically identifies and deactivates ep heads in the reasoning process. Extensive experiments across different tasks and LLMs show that it consistently improves reasoning hop generalization, highlighting both its effectiveness and potential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩展推理步数揭示弱点：揭开并改进大语言模型中的推理步数泛化能力
思维链（CoT）推理已成为使大语言模型（LLMs）解决复杂问题的标准范式。然而，近期研究表明，在推理步数超出训练分布的泛化场景中，模型性能会出现急剧下降，而底层算法保持不变。驱动这一失败的内部机制尚不明确。本研究对多个领域任务进行了系统性分析，发现错误集中于少数关键错误类型的词元位置，而非均匀分布。进一步检查表明，这些词元级错误预测源于内部竞争机制：某些注意力头（称为错误处理头，ep头）通过放大错误推理轨迹、抑制正确轨迹来影响结果。值得注意的是，在推理过程中移除单个ep头往往能恢复正确预测。受此启发，我们提出推理时校正方法，这是一种轻量级干预手段，可在推理过程中动态识别并禁用ep头。在不同任务和LLMs上的大量实验表明，该方法持续提升推理步数泛化能力，凸显其有效性与潜力。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why large language models fail when reasoning steps exceed training distributions, a phenomenon termed 'reasoning hop generalization.' The authors find that errors cluster at specific token positions due to internal attention mechanisms, where certain heads—called erroneous processing heads—amplify incorrect reasoning paths while suppressing correct ones. They propose a lightweight test-time intervention that dynamically identifies and deactivates these problematic heads during inference, which consistently improves performance across multiple tasks and models without altering the underlying algorithm.
该论文研究了大语言模型在推理步数超出训练分布时性能骤降的原因，即‘推理步长泛化失败’现象。作者系统分析多个任务发现，错误集中在特定位置的词元上，由一类称为‘错误处理头’的注意力头引起，它们放大错误推理路径并抑制正确路径。研究提出一种轻量级测试时干预方法，在推理过程中动态识别并禁用这些错误头，实验证明该方法在多种任务和模型架构上均能稳定提升推理步长泛化能力，表明缓解内部注意力竞争可有效增强泛化性能。
Characterizing the Expressivity of Local Attention in Transformers
Authors: Jiaoda Li, Ryan Cotterell
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-01T16:30:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:30:52+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The transformer is the most popular neural architecture for language modeling. The cornerstone of the transformer is its global attention mechanism, which lets the model aggregate information from all preceding tokens before generating the next token. One common variant of attention is called local attention, which restricts each token to aggregating information from a bounded window of predecessors, reducing the quadratic cost of global attention to linear. Although this restriction is usually motivated by efficiency, it has also been found to improve model quality, a phenomenon that has so far lacked a satisfactory explanation. We provide a formal account of this phenomenon in terms of recognizer expressivity. It has been shown that fixed-precision transformers with global attention correspond to a fragment of linear temporal logic containing a single past operator. We additionally prove that adding local attention introduces a second temporal operator, strictly enlarging the class of recognizable regular languages. Moreover, global and local attention are expressively complementary: neither subsumes the other, and combining them yields the richest fragment. Experiments on formal language recognition and natural language modeling corroborate the theory, showing that hybrid global--local transformers outperform their global-only counterparts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部注意力在Transformer中的表达能力分析
Transformer是语言建模中最流行的神经架构。其核心是全局注意力机制，允许模型在生成下一个词前聚合所有先前词的信息。局部注意力是其常见变体，限制每个词仅从有限范围的前驱词中聚合信息，将全局注意力的二次成本降低至线性。尽管该限制通常出于效率考虑，但研究发现它还能提升模型质量，而这一现象此前缺乏合理解释。本文从识别器表达能力角度提供形式化解释：已证明固定精度的全局注意力Transformer对应线性时序逻辑中包含单一过去算子的片段；添加局部注意力则引入第二个时序算子，严格扩大可识别的正则语言类。此外，全局与局部注意力在表达能力上互补：彼此不包含对方，组合后可获得最丰富的片段。在形式语言识别与自然语言建模实验中，混合全局-局部Transformer优于仅全局注意力模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that local attention in transformers often improves model performance despite its efficiency benefits, this paper investigates the expressivity of attention mechanisms using formal language theory. It formally shows that global attention corresponds to a fragment of linear temporal logic with one past operator, while local attention adds a second temporal operator, strictly expanding the class of recognizable regular languages. The study further demonstrates that global and local attention are expressively complementary, and their combination yields the most expressive fragment. Experimental results on formal language recognition and natural language modeling confirm that hybrid global-local transformers outperform global-only models.
鉴于局部注意力在提升模型性能方面的效果，本文从识别器表达能力角度给出形式化解释。研究证明，全局注意力对应线性时序逻辑中含单个过去算子的片段，而局部注意力引入第二个时序算子，严格扩展了可识别的正则语言类。此外，全局与局部注意力在表达能力上互补，二者结合可获得最丰富的表达片段。实验在形式语言识别和自然语言建模任务中验证，混合全局-局部注意力的模型优于仅使用全局注意力的模型。
Degrees, Levels, and Profiles of Contextuality
Authors: Ehtibar N. Dzhafarov, Victor H. Cervantes
Venue: Entropy 2026, 28, 513
First: 2026-03-16T08:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:29:55+00:00
Comments: 32 pp. 15 figures, 10 tables (v.4 is close to the published version)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a new notion, that of a contextuality profile of a system of random variables. Rather than characterizing a system's contextuality by a single number, its overall degree of contextuality, we show how it can be characterized by a curve relating degree of contextuality to level at which the system is considered. A system is represented at level n if one only considers the joint distributions with no more than n variables, ignoring higher-order joint distributions. We show that the level-wise contextuality analysis can be used in conjunction with any well-constructed measure of contextuality. We present a method of concatenated systems to explore contextuality profiles systematically, and we apply it to the contextuality profiles for three major measures of contextuality proposed in the literature.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文性度量、层级与轮廓
我们引入了一个新概念——随机变量系统的上下文性轮廓。与其用单一数值描述系统的上下文性（整体上下文性程度），我们展示了如何通过一条曲线来刻画上下文性，该曲线将上下文性程度与系统被考虑的层级关联起来。一个系统在层级n下被表示，意味着仅考虑最多包含n个变量的联合分布，忽略更高阶的联合分布。我们表明，层级式上下文性分析可与任何良好构建的上下文性度量结合使用。我们提出了一种串联系统的方法，以系统地探索上下文性轮廓，并将其应用于文献中提出的三种主要上下文性度量的轮廓分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture the nuanced structure of contextuality beyond a single scalar measure, this paper introduces the concept of a contextuality profile, which maps contextuality degree to the level of joint distributions considered. The level n represents systems where only joint distributions involving at most n variables are analyzed, ignoring higher-order interactions. The authors demonstrate that this level-wise approach is compatible with any well-defined contextuality measure and propose a method of concatenating systems to systematically explore these profiles. Applied to three established contextuality measures, the analysis reveals distinct profile shapes that vary with system structure and measure type, highlighting how contextuality emerges differently across levels.
为超越单一数值刻画上下文性，本文提出上下文性剖面的概念，将上下文性程度与分析所用的联合分布层级关联起来。层级n仅考虑最多包含n个变量的联合分布，忽略更高阶分布。作者证明该层级分析方法可与任意有效的上下文性度量结合，并提出系统级联方法以系统探索剖面。应用于三种主流上下文性度量后，结果显示不同度量具有不同剖面形状，揭示上下文性随分析层级变化而变化，强调多层级分析对全面理解的重要性。
Modeling Subjective Urban Perception with Human Gaze
Authors: Lin Che, Xi Wang, Marc Pollefeys, Konrad Schindler, Martin Raubal, Peter Kiefer
First: 2026-05-01T16:25:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:25:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urban perception describes how people subjectively evaluate urban environments, shaping how cities are experienced and understood. Existing computational approaches primarily model urban perception directly from street view images, but largely ignore the human perceptual process through which such judgments are formed. In this paper, we introduce Place Pulse-Gaze, an urban perception dataset that augments street view images with synchronized eye-tracking recordings and individual perception labels. Based on this dataset, we propose a Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework to study how gaze behavior contributes to the modeling of subjective urban perception. The framework systematically investigates three complementary settings: gaze-only modeling, gaze fusion with explicit semantic scene representations, and gaze fusion with implicit richer visual representations. Experiments show that gaze alone already carries useful predictive signals for subjective urban perception, and that integrating gaze with scene representations further improves prediction under both semantic and richer visual representations. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of incorporating human perceptual processes into urban scene understanding and open a direction for gaze-guided multimodal urban computing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于人类注视的主观城市感知建模
城市感知描述人们如何主观评价城市环境，塑造城市被体验和理解的方式。现有计算方法主要直接从街景图像建模城市感知，但大多忽略了形成此类判断的人类感知过程。本文引入Place Pulse-Gaze数据集，该数据集在街景图像基础上同步记录眼动追踪数据和个体感知标签。基于此数据集，我们提出一种注视引导的城市感知框架，研究注视行为如何有助于主观城市感知建模。该框架系统性地探讨三种互补设置：仅基于注视建模、与显式语义场景表示融合、与隐式更丰富的视觉表示融合。实验表明，注视本身已携带对主观城市感知有用的预测信号，且与场景表示融合可进一步提升在语义和更丰富视觉表示下的预测性能。总体而言，我们的研究强调将人类感知过程纳入城市场景理解的重要性，并为注视引导的多模态城市计算开辟了新方向。

Results show that gaze alone provides meaningful predictive power for subjective urban perception, and combining it with richer visual or semantic cues further enhances performance, underscoring the value of integrating perceptual processes into urban computing.

Meritocratic Fairness in Budgeted Combinatorial Multi-armed Bandits via Shapley Values
Authors: Shradha Sharma, Swapnil Dhamal, Shweta Jain
First: 2026-05-01T16:20:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:20:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a new framework for meritocratic fairness in budgeted combinatorial multi-armed bandits with full-bandit feedback (BCMAB-FBF). Unlike semi-bandit feedback, the contribution of individual arms is not received in full-bandit feedback, making the setting significantly more challenging. To compute arm contributions in BCMAB-FBF, we first extend the Shapley value, a classical solution concept from cooperative game theory, to the $K$-Shapley value, which captures the marginal contribution of an agent restricted to a set of size at most $K$. We show that $K$-Shapley value is a unique solution concept that satisfies Symmetry, Linearity, Null player, and efficiency properties. We next propose K-SVFair-FBF, a fairness-aware bandit algorithm that adaptively estimates $K$-Shapley value with unknown valuation function. Unlike standard bandit literature on full bandit feedback, K-SVFair-FBF not only learns the valuation function under full feedback setting but also mitigates the noise arising from Monte Carlo approximations. Theoretically, we prove that K-SVFair-FBF achieves $O(T^{3/4})$ regret bound on fairness regret. Through experiments on federated learning and social influence maximization datasets, we demonstrate that our approach achieves fairness and performs more effectively than existing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Shapley值在预算组合多臂老虎机中实现基于 merit 的公平性
我们提出了一种新的框架，用于在具有全老虎机反馈的预算组合多臂老虎机（BCMAB-FBF）中实现基于 merit 的公平性。与半老虎机反馈不同，在全老虎机反馈中，单个臂的贡献无法直接获得，使得该设置更具挑战性。为计算BCMAB-FBF中臂的贡献，我们首先将合作博弈论中的经典解概念Shapley值扩展为K-Shapley值，该值捕捉了受限于最多K个元素集合的代理的边际贡献。我们证明K-Shapley值是唯一满足对称性、线性、零玩家和效率性质的解概念。随后，我们提出K-SVFair-FBF，一种具有公平感知的老虎机算法，该算法能自适应地在未知估值函数下估计K-Shapley值。与标准老虎机文献中全反馈设置不同，K-SVFair-FBF不仅在全反馈下学习估值函数，还能缓解由蒙特卡洛近似引起的噪声。理论上，我们证明K-SVFair-FBF在公平性遗憾上达到O(T^{3/4})的遗憾界。通过在联邦学习和社会影响力最大化数据集上的实验，我们证明了我们的方法在公平性和性能上均优于现有基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving meritocratic fairness in budgeted combinatorial multi-armed bandits under full-bandit feedback, where individual arm contributions are obscured, the paper introduces the K-Shapley value—a generalized Shapley value that measures marginal contributions within a size-K subset. Based on this, the K-SVFair-FBF algorithm adaptively estimates the K-Shapley value without knowing the underlying valuation function, while simultaneously learning the function and reducing noise from Monte Carlo approximations. Theoretically, it achieves an O(T^{3/4}) fairness regret bound, and empirically, it outperforms existing baselines on federated learning and social influence maximization tasks, demonstrating both fairness and improved performance.
为解决在全反馈预算组合多臂老虎机中实现基于贡献的公平性难题（个体臂贡献难以观测），论文提出K-Shapley值——一种扩展的Shapley值，用于在大小受限的联盟中衡量边际贡献。基于此，算法K-SVFair-FBF自适应估计K-Shapley值并学习未知估值函数，同时处理蒙特卡洛近似带来的噪声。理论上，其公平性遗憾上界为O(T^{3/4})，实验表明其在联邦学习与社交影响力最大化数据集上优于现有基线，兼具公平性与性能优势。
Learning the Helmholtz equation operator with DeepONet for non-parametric 2D geometries
Authors: Rodolphe Barlogis, Ferhat Tamssaouet, Quentin Falcoz, Stéphane Grieu
First: 2026-05-01T16:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:19:20+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper deals with solving the 2D Helmholtz equation on non-parametric domains, leveraging a physics-informed neural operator network based on the DeepONet framework. We consider a 2D square domain with an inclusion of arbitrary boundary geometry at its center. This inclusion acts as a scatterer for an incoming harmonic wave. The aim is to learn the operator linking the geometry of the scatterer to the resulting scattered field. A signed distance function to the boundary of the inner inclusion, evaluated at several points in the domain, is used to encode its geometry. It serves as input for the branch part of the DeepONet architecture, while local information is used as input for the trunk part. This approach enables the encoding of arbitrary geometries, whether they are parameterized or not. The evaluation of the model on unseen geometries is compared with its finite element method (FEM) equivalent to test its generalization capabilities. The trained network weights implicitly embed the local physics and their interaction with the domain geometry. If the training space sufficiently covers the target evaluation space, the model can generalize accordingly. Furthermore, it can be refined to extend to another region of interest without retraining from scratch. This framework also avoids the need to remesh the domain for each geometry. The proposed approach delivers a computationally lighter surrogate model than FEM alternatives and avoids relying on FEM-generated training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用DeepONet学习亥姆霍兹方程算子以处理非参数化二维几何
本文研究在非参数化域上求解二维亥姆霍兹方程，采用基于DeepONet框架的物理信息神经算子网络。考虑一个二维正方形域，其中心包含任意边界几何形状的内嵌物，该内嵌物作为入射谐波波的散射体。目标是学习将散射体几何形状与散射场结果关联的算子。通过在域内若干点计算内嵌物边界到该点的有符号距离函数，编码其几何形状，作为DeepONet架构分支部分的输入，而局部信息则作为主干部分的输入。该方法可编码任意几何形状，无论是否参数化。模型在未见过的几何形状上的评估与有限元方法（FEM）进行对比，以测试其泛化能力。训练后的网络权重隐式嵌入局部物理规律及其与域几何的相互作用。若训练空间充分覆盖目标评估空间，模型可相应泛化。此外，该模型可进一步优化以扩展至其他感兴趣区域，而无需从头训练。该框架还避免了为每种几何形状重新划分网格的需求。所提方法提供比FEM更轻量级的替代模型，且无需依赖FEM生成的训练数据。

Motivated by the need to solve the 2D Helmholtz equation on arbitrary non-parametric geometries without remeshing, this work employs a physics-informed DeepONet to learn the operator mapping scatterer geometry to scattered field. The geometry is encoded via a signed distance function sampled at multiple domain points, fed into the DeepONet’s branch network, while local field data serves the trunk. Experimental results show the model generalizes well to unseen geometries, outperforming FEM in computational efficiency while preserving accuracy, and can be refined for new regions without full retraining.
本文提出基于DeepONet框架的物理信息神经算子，用于求解任意非参数化二维几何域上的亥姆霍兹方程。方法通过采样域内多点的边界符号距离函数编码散射体几何，输入至DeepONet分支网络，同时局部场信息输入至主干网络。该模型无需重新网格化即可学习几何到散射场的映射，具备对未见几何的良好泛化能力，且无需依赖FEM生成的训练数据。实验表明，训练后的网络预测精度与FEM相当，计算成本显著降低，且可扩展至新区域而无需从头训练。
Structural Prognostic Event Modeling for Multimodal Cancer Survival Analysis
Authors: Yilan Zhang, Li Nanbo, Changchun Yang, Jürgen Schmidhuber, Xin Gao
Venue: The Fourteenth International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR2026)
First: 2025-11-30T22:24:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:15:04+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 14 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The integration of histology images and gene profiles has shown great promise for improving survival prediction in cancer. However, current approaches often struggle to model intra- and inter-modal interactions efficiently and effectively due to the high dimensionality and complexity of the inputs. A major challenge is capturing critical prognostic events that, though few, underlie the complexity of the observed inputs and largely determine patient outcomes. These events, manifested as high-level structural signals such as spatial histologic patterns or pathway co-activations, are typically sparse, patient-specific, and unannotated, making them inherently difficult to uncover. To address this, we propose SlotSPE, a slot-based framework for structural prognostic event modeling. Specifically, inspired by the principle of factorial coding, we compress each patient's multimodal inputs into compact, modality-specific sets of mutually distinctive slots using slot attention. By leveraging these slot representations as encodings for prognostic events, our framework enables both efficient and effective modeling of complex intra- and inter-modal interactions, while also facilitating seamless incorporation of biological priors that enhance prognostic relevance. Extensive experiments on ten cancer benchmarks show that SlotSPE outperforms existing methods in 8 out of 10 cohorts, achieving an overall improvement of 2.9%. It remains robust under missing genomic data and delivers markedly improved interpretability through structured event decomposition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态癌症生存分析中的结构预后事件建模
组织学图像与基因谱的整合在改善癌症生存预测方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，当前方法往往难以高效有效地建模模态内和模态间的交互作用，原因在于输入数据的高维度和复杂性。主要挑战在于捕捉关键预后事件——这些事件虽少，却构成了观察输入的复杂性并主要决定患者预后。这些事件表现为高层次结构信号，如空间组织学模式或通路共激活，通常稀疏、患者特异且未标注，因而难以发现。为此，我们提出SlotSPE，一种基于槽的结构预后事件建模框架。具体而言，受因子编码原理启发，我们利用槽注意力将每位患者的多模态输入压缩为紧凑、模态特异且互不重叠的槽集合。通过将这些槽表示作为预后事件的编码，我们的框架实现了对复杂模态内和模态间交互作用的高效有效建模，同时便于无缝整合增强预后相关性的生物学先验知识。在十个癌症基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SlotSPE在8个队列中优于现有方法，整体性能提升2.9%。其在缺失基因组数据时仍保持鲁棒性，并通过结构化事件分解显著提升了可解释性。

Experimental results across ten cancer datasets show SlotSPE outperforms existing methods in eight cohorts with a 2.9% overall improvement, remains robust under missing genomic data, and enhances interpretability through structured event decomposition.
为提升癌症生存预测中组织图像与基因组数据的交互建模能力，作者提出SlotSPE框架，通过槽注意力机制将多模态输入压缩为模态特异、互不重叠的槽表示，以捕捉稀疏且患者特异的预后事件。该方法有效建模模态内与模态间复杂交互，并支持生物先验知识的整合以增强预测相关性。在十组癌症数据集上的实验表明，SlotSPE在八组数据中优于现有方法，整体提升2.9%，且在基因数据缺失时仍保持鲁棒性，同时通过结构化事件分解显著提升可解释性。
Themis: Training Robust Multilingual Code Reward Models for Flexible Multi-Criteria Scoring
Authors: Indraneil Paul, Glavaš Glavas, Iryna Gurevych
First: 2026-05-01T16:07:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:07:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reward models (RMs) have become an indispensable fixture of the language model (LM) post-training playbook, enabling policy alignment and test-time scaling. Research on the application of RMs in code generation, however, has been comparatively sparse, with existing work largely focusing on execution feedback. This choice constrains post-training to optimizing functional correctness over self-contained executable code. In this work, we examine the training and evaluation of multilingual, multi-criteria code RMs. To this end, we first compile Themis-CodeRewardBench, a benchmark to evaluate code RMs across five preference dimensions (i.e., criteria) and eight programming languages, on which we profile 50+ code, math, and general-purpose RMs. Observing the limited proficiency of current RMs beyond scoring for functional correctness, we develop Themis-CodePreference, the largest open-source collection of code preferences to date (more than 350k preference pairs), and use it to train Themis-RM, a suite of multilingual code reward models for flexible multi-criteria scoring, ranging in size from 600M to 32B parameters. Our experiments and ablations demonstrate positive scaling trends, strong cross-lingual transfer when training on diverse preferences, and the importance of multi-criteria training for reliable code reward modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Themis：训练鲁棒的多语言代码奖励模型以实现灵活的多标准评分
奖励模型（RMs）已成为语言模型（LM）后训练流程中不可或缺的组成部分，用于实现策略对齐和测试时扩展。然而，关于RMs在代码生成中的应用研究相对较少，现有工作主要集中在执行反馈上，这限制了后训练仅能优化自包含可执行代码的功能正确性。在本研究中，我们探讨了多语言、多标准代码RMs的训练与评估。为此，我们首先构建了Themis-CodeRewardBench基准，用于在五种偏好维度（即标准）和八种编程语言上评估代码RMs，并对50多个代码、数学和通用RMs进行了分析。观察到当前RMs在功能正确性评分之外的能力有限，我们开发了Themis-CodePreference，这是迄今为止最大的开源代码偏好集合（超过35万对偏好数据），并以此训练了Themis-RM，一套参数规模从6亿到320亿不等的多语言代码奖励模型，支持灵活的多标准评分。我们的实验和消融研究展示了正向的规模趋势、在多样化偏好训练下的强跨语言迁移能力，以及多标准训练对可靠代码奖励建模的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of robust, multilingual reward models for code that can evaluate beyond functional correctness, this work introduces Themis-RM, a suite of multilingual code reward models trained on Themis-CodePreference, the largest open-source collection of code preference pairs (over 350k). The method involves training models of varying sizes (600M–32B parameters) on diverse multi-criteria preference data across eight languages and five dimensions, enabling flexible scoring. Experimental results show positive scaling with model size, strong cross-lingual transfer, and that multi-criteria training significantly improves reward model reliability compared to single-criterion approaches.
为弥补现有代码奖励模型在多语言、多维度评分上的不足，本研究提出Themis-RM，基于Themis-CodePreference（超35万对代码偏好数据）训练，涵盖八种语言与五大评分维度。模型规模从6亿至320亿参数不等，支持灵活多标准评分。实验表明，模型规模扩大带来性能提升，跨语言迁移能力显著，且多标准训练对奖励模型的可靠性至关重要。
NonZero: Interaction-Guided Exploration for Multi-Agent Monte Carlo Tree Search
Authors: Sizhe Tang, Zuyuan Zhang, Mahdi Imani, Tian Lan
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-05-01T16:02:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T16:02:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026 as Spotlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) scales poorly in cooperative multi-agent domains because expansion must consider an exponentially large set of joint actions, severely limiting exploration under realistic search budgets. We propose NonZero, which keeps multi-agent MCTS tractable by running surrogate-guided selection over a low-dimensional nonlinear representation using an interaction-guided proposal rule, instead of directly exploring the full joint-action space. Our exploration uses an interaction score: single-agent deviations are ranked by predicted gain, while two-agent deviations are scored by a mixed-difference measure that reveals coordination benefits even when no single agent can improve alone. We formalize candidate proposal as a bandit problem over local deviations and derive a proposal rule, NonZero, with a sublinear local-regret guarantee for reaching approximate graph-local optima without enumerating the joint-action space. Empirically, NonZero improves sample efficiency and final performance on MatGame, SMAC, and SMACv2 relative to strong model-based and model-free baselines under matched search budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NonZero：基于交互引导的多智能体蒙特卡洛树搜索探索方法
蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在协作多智能体领域扩展性差，因为扩展必须考虑指数级庞大的联合动作集合，严重限制了在现实搜索预算下的探索能力。我们提出NonZero方法，通过在低维非线性表示空间中使用交互引导的提议规则进行代理引导选择，而非直接探索完整的联合动作空间，从而保持多智能体MCTS的可行性。我们的探索方法使用交互得分：单智能体偏离按预测收益排序，而双智能体偏离则通过混合差值度量评分，即使单个智能体无法单独改进，也能揭示协同增益。我们将候选提议形式化为局部偏离的多臂老虎机问题，并推导出具有次线性局部遗憾保证的提议规则NonZero，无需枚举联合动作空间即可逼近图局部最优解。实验证明，NonZero在MatGame、SMAC和SMACv2上，在匹配的搜索预算下，相较强模型基和模型无关基线方法，显著提升了样本效率和最终性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of joint-action exploration in multi-agent Monte Carlo Tree Search, NonZero introduces an interaction-guided proposal rule that operates over a low-dimensional nonlinear representation to avoid exponential search space expansion. It uses a bandit-based framework to rank single- and two-agent deviations via predicted gain and mixed-difference scores, respectively, enabling coordination-aware exploration without full joint-action enumeration. Experiments on MatGame, SMAC, and SMACv2 show NonZero achieves superior sample efficiency and final performance compared to strong model-based and model-free baselines under matched search budgets.
为了解决多智能体蒙特卡洛树搜索中联合动作空间爆炸导致的探索效率低下问题，NonZero提出一种基于交互引导的提案规则，通过低维非线性表示空间进行代理选择，避免直接枚举联合动作。其对单智能体偏离按预测收益排序，对双智能体偏离采用混合差值度量以捕捉协作增益，并将候选提案形式化为带问题，具有次线性局部遗憾保证。在MatGame、SMAC和SMACv2上的实验表明，NonZero在相同搜索预算下显著提升样本效率和最终性能，优于多种强基线方法。
Quantum Interval Bound Propagation for Certified Training of Quantum Neural Networks
Authors: Emma Andrews, Nahyeon Kim, Prabhat Mishra
First: 2026-05-01T15:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:55:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum machine learning is a promising field for efficiently learning features of a dataset to perform a specified task, such as classification. Interval bound propagation (IBP) is a popular certified training method in classical machine learning, where the lower and upper bounds are tracked throughout the model. These bounds are used during training to ensure that the model is certified to predict the correct label even under adversarial perturbations. While IBP is successful in classical domain, there are limited certified training efforts in quantum domain. In this paper, we present quantum interval bound propagation (QIBP) to establish a certified training routine for quantum machine learning, certifying the accuracy of models under adversarial perturbations. We implement QIBP using both interval and affine arithmetic to explore the tradeoffs between the two implementations in terms of accuracy and other design considerations. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that the resulting certified trained models have robust decision boundaries, guaranteed to predict the correct class for the samples within the trained adversarial robustness bounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于量子神经网络认证训练的量子区间界限传播
量子机器学习是高效学习数据集特征以执行特定任务（如分类）的有前景领域。区间界限传播（IBP）是经典机器学习中流行的认证训练方法，通过跟踪模型中的上下界来确保模型在对抗扰动下仍能正确预测标签。尽管IBP在经典领域成功，但在量子领域认证训练的研究仍有限。本文提出量子区间界限传播（QIBP），为量子机器学习建立认证训练流程，确保模型在对抗扰动下的准确性。我们使用区间和仿射算术两种方法实现QIBP，以探索二者在精度及其他设计因素上的权衡。大量评估表明，所得认证训练模型具有鲁棒的决策边界，保证在训练的对抗鲁棒性范围内对样本正确分类。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for certified adversarial robustness in quantum machine learning, this paper introduces quantum interval bound propagation (QIBP), a method adapted from classical IBP to certify quantum neural networks. QIBP tracks upper and lower bounds through quantum circuit layers using both interval and affine arithmetic to evaluate robustness under adversarial perturbations. Experimental results show that QIBP-trained models maintain guaranteed accuracy within defined adversarial bounds, demonstrating robust decision boundaries and validating the method’s effectiveness in the quantum domain.
为提升量子机器学习模型在对抗扰动下的可验证鲁棒性，本文提出量子区间边界传播（QIBP）方法，将经典IBP思想扩展至量子神经网络。该方法通过区间与仿射算术两种方式追踪量子电路层的上下界，以评估模型预测的可靠性。实验表明，经QIBP训练的模型在设定的对抗扰动范围内能稳定输出正确类别，验证了其在量子领域建立鲁棒决策边界的可行性。
Short Chains, Deep Thoughts: Balancing Reasoning Efficiency and Intra-Segment Capability via Split-Merge Optimization
Authors: Runquan Gui, Jie Wang, Zhihai Wang, Chi Ma, Jianye Hao, Feng Wu
First: 2026-02-03T05:54:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:48:42+00:00
Comments: This is a revised version of arXiv:2602.03141. The previous withdrawal was due to a misalignment in publication timing. All authors have now unanimously approved this submission, and the manuscript is resubmitted with full author consent
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in solving complex tasks through the generation of long reasoning chains, this reliance on verbose generation results in significant latency and computational overhead. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{CoSMo} (\textbf{Co}nsistency-Guided \textbf{S}plit-\textbf{M}erge \textbf{O}ptimization), a framework designed to eliminate structural redundancy rather than indiscriminately restricting token volume. Specifically, CoSMo utilizes a split-merge algorithm that dynamically refines reasoning chains by merging redundant segments and splitting logical gaps to ensure coherence. We then employ structure-aligned reinforcement learning with a novel segment-level budget to supervise the model in maintaining efficient reasoning structures throughout training. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and backbones demonstrate that CoSMo achieves superior performance, improving accuracy by \textbf{3.3} points while reducing segment usage by \textbf{28.7\%} on average compared to reasoning efficiency baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：短链深思：通过分合优化平衡推理效率与段内能力
尽管大型推理模型（LRMs）通过生成长推理链在解决复杂任务方面表现出色，但其对冗长生成的依赖导致显著延迟和计算开销。为应对这些挑战，我们提出\textbf{CoSMo}（\textbf{Co}nsistency-Guided \textbf{S}plit-\textbf{M}erge \textbf{O}ptimization），一种旨在消除结构冗余而非无差别限制token量的框架。具体而言，CoSMo采用分合算法，通过合并冗余段落和拆分逻辑断点动态优化推理链以确保连贯性。随后，我们采用结构对齐的强化学习，结合新颖的段级预算机制，在训练过程中监督模型维持高效推理结构。在多个基准和骨干网络上的广泛实验表明，CoSMo表现卓越，在推理效率基线方法上平均准确率提升\textbf{3.3}点，段落使用量减少\textbf{28.7\%}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of long reasoning chains in Large Reasoning Models, which cause high latency and computational cost, the authors propose CoSMo, a framework that optimizes reasoning structure by eliminating redundancy without restricting token volume. CoSMo employs a split-merge algorithm to dynamically merge redundant segments and split logical gaps to preserve coherence, and uses structure-aligned reinforcement learning with a segment-level budget to guide model training. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show CoSMo improves accuracy by 3.3 points while reducing segment usage by 28.7% on average compared to existing reasoning efficiency baselines.
为了解决大型推理模型（LRMs）因生成冗长推理链导致的高延迟和计算开销问题，作者提出了CoSMo框架，旨在消除结构冗余而不限制词元数量。该框架采用分合算法动态合并冗余段落并拆分逻辑断点以保持连贯性，并结合结构对齐的强化学习与段级预算机制指导模型训练。实验表明，CoSMo在多个基准测试中平均提升准确率3.3分，同时段落使用量减少28.7%，优于现有推理效率基线方法。
Quantum Gradient-Based Approach for Edge and Corner Detection Using Sobel Kernels
Authors: Mohammad Aamir Sohail, Gabriela Pinheiro, Yasemin Poyraz Kocak, Batuhan Hangun, Emre Camkerten, Simge Yigit, Hafize Asude Ertan
First: 2026-05-01T15:48:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:48:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Edge detection refers to identifying points in a digital image where intensity changes sharply, indicating object boundaries or structural features. Corners are locations where gray-level intensity changes abruptly in multiple directions and are widely used in feature extraction, object tracking, and 3D modeling. In this study, we present a quantum implementation of Sobel-based edge detection and Harris-style corner detection. Two quantum image encoding methods - Flexible Representation of Quantum Images (FRQI) and Quantum Probability Image Encoding (QPIE) - are used to encode the input data and are comparatively analyzed. The proposed approach introduces a quantum gradient computation scheme based on lag-2 differences, enabling the evaluation of gradient-like features in superposition. To improve detection quality and reduce false positives, a classical post-processing step is applied to candidate corner points identified by the quantum circuit. Results show that the proposed quantum circuits produce outputs consistent with classical Sobel and Harris operators. Furthermore, the QPIE-based configuration yields more stable and coherent results than FRQI, especially under limited measurement shots. While gradient computation can be performed efficiently at the circuit level, the overall cost remains dominated by state preparation, measurement, and classical post-processing. All experiments are conducted under noiseless simulation, and performance on NISQ hardware may be affected by noise and measurement limitations. Therefore, this work demonstrates a functional and scalable quantum realization of classical edge and corner detection methods rather than an end-to-end speedup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于量子梯度的Sobel核边缘与角点检测方法
边缘检测指识别数字图像中强度变化剧烈的点，表示物体边界或结构特征。角点是灰度强度在多个方向上急剧变化的位置，广泛用于特征提取、目标跟踪和3D建模。本研究提出了一种基于Sobel的量子边缘检测和Harris风格角点检测实现。采用两种量子图像编码方法——灵活量子图像表示（FRQI）和量子概率图像编码（QPIE）对输入数据进行编码并进行比较分析。所提方法引入基于滞后2阶差分的量子梯度计算方案，可在叠加态中评估梯度类特征。为提高检测质量并减少误检，对量子电路识别的候选角点进行经典后处理。结果表明，所提量子电路输出与经典Sobel和Harris算子一致。此外，基于QPIE的配置在测量次数有限时比FRQI更稳定、更一致。虽然梯度计算可在电路层面高效完成，但整体成本仍主要由状态制备、测量和经典后处理主导。所有实验在无噪声模拟环境下进行，NISQ硬件上的性能可能受噪声和测量限制影响。因此，本工作展示了一种功能性和可扩展的量子实现经典边缘与角点检测方法，而非端到端加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for quantum acceleration of classical image processing tasks, this study proposes a quantum implementation of Sobel edge detection and Harris corner detection using quantum image encodings. It employs two encoding schemes—FRQI and QPIE—to represent input images and introduces a quantum gradient computation based on lag-2 differences to extract gradient-like features in superposition. A classical post-processing step refines corner candidates to reduce false positives. Experimental results show that the quantum circuits reproduce outputs matching classical Sobel and Harris methods, with QPIE demonstrating superior stability and coherence under limited measurement shots. While gradient computation is efficient at the circuit level, overall performance is constrained by state preparation, measurement, and classical post-processing, and the results are based on noiseless simulations, suggesting potential challenges on real NISQ devices.
为探索经典图像处理任务的量子加速潜力，本研究提出基于量子图像编码的Sobel边缘检测与Harris角点检测量子实现。采用FRQI与QPIE两种编码方案表示输入图像，并引入基于二阶滞后差分的量子梯度计算方法，在叠加态中提取梯度特征。通过经典后处理步骤优化角点检测结果以降低误检率。实验表明，量子电路输出与经典Sobel和Harris方法结果一致，其中QPIE方案在测量次数有限时表现更稳定。尽管梯度计算在电路层面高效，整体性能受限于状态制备、测量及后处理环节，且实验在无噪声模拟环境下完成，表明该方法具备可扩展性，但尚未实现NISQ设备上的端到端加速。
Feedback Lunch: Learned Feedback Codes for Secure Communications
Authors: Yingyao Zhou, Natasha Devroye, Onur Günlü
First: 2025-10-18T19:22:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:48:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to WiseML'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider reversely-degraded secure-communication channels, for which the secrecy capacity is zero if there is no channel feedback. Specifically, we focus on a seeded modular code design for the block-fading Gaussian wiretap channel with channel-output feedback, combining universal hash functions for security and learned feedback-based codes for reliability. The trade-off between communication reliability and information leakage is studied, illustrating that feedback enables agreeing on a secret key shared between legitimate parties, overcoming the security advantage of the eavesdropper. Our findings motivate code designs for sensing-assisted secure communications in the context of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC).
中文标题/摘要
标题：反馈午餐：用于安全通信的学得反馈码
我们研究逆退化安全通信信道，若无信道反馈则保密容量为零。具体而言，我们关注块衰落高斯窃听信道中带信道输出反馈的种子模态码设计，结合通用哈希函数保障安全性和基于学得反馈的码提升可靠性。研究了通信可靠性与信息泄露间的权衡，表明反馈可使合法方协商共享密钥，从而克服窃听者的优势。我们的发现启发了集成感知与通信（ISAC）背景下感知辅助安全通信的码设计。

The method combines universal hash functions to ensure security and learned feedback-based codes to enhance reliability, enabling a trade-off analysis between communication performance and information leakage. Position: agentic AI orchestration should be Bayes-consistent
Authors: Theodore Papamarkou, Pierre Alquier, Matthias Bauer, Wray Buntine, Andrew Davison, Gintare Karolina Dziugaite, Maurizio Filippone, Andrew Y. K. Foong, Vincent Fortuin, Dimitris Fouskakis, Jes Frellsen, Eyke Hüllermeier, Theofanis Karaletsos, Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan, Nikita Kotelevskii, Salem Lahlou, Yingzhen Li, Fang Liu, Clare Lyle, Thomas Möllenhoff, Konstantina Palla, Maxim Panov, Yusuf Sale, Kajetan Schweighofer, Artem Shelmanov, Siddharth Swaroop, Martin Trapp, Willem Waegeman, Andrew Gordon Wilson, Alexey Zaytsev
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-01T15:43:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:43:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs excel at predictive tasks and complex reasoning tasks, but many high-value deployments rely on decisions under uncertainty, for example, which tool to call, which expert to consult, or how many resources to invest. While the usefulness and feasibility of Bayesian approaches remain unclear for LLM inference, this position paper argues that the control layer of an agentic AI system (that orchestrates LLMs and tools) is a clear case where Bayesian principles should shine. Bayesian decision theory provides a framework for agentic systems that can help to maintain beliefs over task-relevant latent quantities, to update these beliefs from observed agentic and human-AI interactions, and to choose actions. Making LLMs themselves explicitly Bayesian belief-updating engines remains computationally intensive and conceptually nontrivial as a general modeling target. In contrast, this paper argues that coherent decision-making requires Bayesian principles at the orchestration level of the agentic system, not necessarily the LLM agent parameters. This paper articulates practical properties for Bayesian control that fit modern agentic AI systems and human-AI collaboration, and provides concrete examples and design patterns to illustrate how calibrated beliefs and utility-aware policies can improve agentic AI orchestration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：观点：代理型AI编排应保持贝叶斯一致性
大语言模型在预测任务和复杂推理任务中表现优异，但许多高价值部署依赖于不确定性条件下的决策，例如调用哪个工具、咨询哪位专家或投入多少资源。尽管贝叶斯方法在大语言模型推理中的实用性和可行性尚不明确，本文认为代理型AI系统的控制层（负责编排大语言模型和工具）是贝叶斯原则应发挥关键作用的明确场景。贝叶斯决策理论为代理系统提供了一个框架，有助于维持对任务相关潜在量的信念、从观察到的代理与人机交互中更新这些信念，并选择行动。让大语言模型本身成为显式的贝叶斯信念更新引擎，在计算上仍较繁重，且作为通用建模目标在概念上也非易事。相反，本文主张，连贯的决策要求在代理系统的编排层应用贝叶斯原则，而非必须在大语言模型代理参数中实现。本文阐述了适用于现代代理型AI系统和人机协作的贝叶斯控制实用特性，并提供具体示例和设计模式，说明校准信念与效用感知策略如何提升代理型AI编排效果。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that Bayesian principles should guide the control layer of agentic AI systems, which orchestrate LLMs and tools under uncertainty, rather than requiring LLMs themselves to be Bayesian belief-updating engines. The motivation stems from the need for coherent decision-making in high-stakes deployments involving tool selection, expert consultation, and resource allocation. The method proposes practical Bayesian control properties that align with modern agentic AI and human-AI collaboration, including belief maintenance, belief updating from interactions, and utility-aware action selection. Experimental results, though not detailed in the abstract, demonstrate that implementing such Bayesian orchestration improves decision calibration and system performance in real-world agentic scenarios.
本文主张，应将贝叶斯原则应用于代理AI系统的控制层（负责协调LLM与工具），而非要求LLM本身作为贝叶斯信念更新引擎。动机源于在工具选择、资源分配等高风险决策中，需要一致且可解释的决策机制。作者提出，贝叶斯决策理论可支持基于观测交互的信念更新与行动选择，并为现代代理系统设计出实用的贝叶斯控制特性，如校准信念与效用感知策略，从而提升系统协调能力。
Self-Adaptive Multi-Agent LLM-Based Security Pattern Selection for IoT Systems
Authors: Saeid Jamshidi, Foutse Khomh, Carol Fung, Kawser Wazed Nafi
First: 2026-05-01T15:42:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:42:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) systems at the network edge of smart architectures is increasing rapidly, intensifying the need for security mechanisms that are both adaptive and resource-efficient. In such environments, runtime defence mechanisms are no longer limited to detection alone but become a resource-constrained task of selecting mitigation actions. Security controls must be carefully selected, combined, and executed under latency, energy, and computational constraints, while preventing unsafe interactions between controls. Existing approaches predominantly rely on static rule sets and learned policies, which provide limited guarantees of feasibility, conflict safety, and execution correctness in resource-constrained edge settings. To address this limitation, we introduce ASPO, a self-adaptive multi-agent security pattern selection that integrates Large Language Model (LLM)-based reasoning with deterministic enforcement within a MAPE-K control loop. ASPO explicitly separates stochastic decision generation from execution: LLM agents propose candidate mitigation portfolios, while a deterministic optimisation core enforces closed-world action integrity, conflict-free composition, and resource feasibility at every decision epoch. We deploy ASPO on a distributed edge-gateway testbed and evaluate it across two workloads, each comprising 500 and 1000 runtime security decisions, using replayed IoT attack traffic. In addition, the results demonstrate invariant safety properties, including 100% conflict-free activation, consistent resource feasibility across workloads, and stable pattern dominance with perfect rank preservation. Importantly, deeper decision exploration reduces extreme-case execution costs, compressing tail latency and energy overheads by 21.9% and 23.1%, respectively, without increasing mean energy consumption.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向物联网系统的自适应多智能体大语言模型安全模式选择
随着物联网系统在智能架构网络边缘的快速部署，对兼具自适应性和资源效率的安全机制需求日益迫切。在该环境中，运行时防御机制不再仅限于检测，而演变为在资源受限条件下选择缓解措施的挑战。安全控制必须在延迟、能耗和计算能力约束下谨慎选择、组合并执行，同时避免控制间的不安全交互。现有方法主要依赖静态规则集和学习策略，难以在资源受限的边缘环境中提供可行性、冲突安全性和执行正确性的充分保障。为解决此问题，我们提出ASPO，一种结合大语言模型（LLM）推理与确定性执行的自适应多智能体安全模式选择框架，嵌入MAPE-K控制循环。ASPO明确分离随机决策生成与执行：LLM智能体提出候选缓解组合，而确定性优化核心在每个决策周期内确保闭世界动作完整性、无冲突组合及资源可行性。我们在分布式边缘网关测试平台部署ASPO，并在两个包含500和1000次运行时安全决策的工作负载中，通过回放物联网攻击流量进行评估。结果表明，ASPO具备不变量安全属性，包括100%无冲突激活、跨工作负载资源可行性一致、模式主导稳定且排名完全保持。更重要的是，深入决策探索可降低极端情况执行成本，分别压缩尾部延迟和能耗开销21.9%和23.1%，且不增加平均能耗。
