ASPO separates stochastic mitigation proposal by LLM agents from deterministic execution validation, ensuring conflict-free composition and resource feasibility at each decision point. Evaluated on a distributed edge-gateway testbed with 500 and 1000 runtime security decisions under replayed attack traffic, ASPO achieved 100% conflict-free activation, consistent resource feasibility, and stable pattern dominance. Deeper decision exploration reduced tail latency and energy overheads by 21.9% and 23.1% respectively, without increasing mean energy consumption.
为应对边缘物联网系统对自适应且资源高效安全机制的需求，ASPO提出一种结合大语言模型推理与确定性执行的自适应多智能体框架，嵌入MAPE-K控制循环。LLM智能体生成候选缓解方案，确定性核心确保每轮决策中无冲突组合与资源可行性。在分布式边缘网关测试平台上，针对500和1000次运行安全决策的负载，ASPO实现100%无冲突激活、资源可行性稳定及策略主导性保持。深度决策探索使尾部延迟和能耗分别降低21.9%和23.1%，且未增加平均能耗。
Preference Goal Tuning: Post-Training as Latent Control for Frozen Policies
Authors: Guangyu Zhao, Kewei Lian, Haoxuan Ru, Borong Zhang, Haowei Lin, Zhancun Mu, Haobo Fu, Qiang Fu, Shaofei Cai, Zihao Wang, Yitao Liang
First: 2024-12-03T03:27:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:42:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Goal-conditioned policies enable decision-making models to execute diverse behaviors based on specified goals, yet their downstream performance is often highly sensitive to the choice of instructions or prompts. To bypass the limitations of discrete text prompts, we formulate post-training adaptation as a latent control problem, where the goal embedding serves as a continuous control variable to modulate the behavior of a frozen policy. We propose Preference Goal Tuning (PGT), a framework that optimizes this latent control variable to align the induced trajectory distribution with task preferences. Unlike standard fine-tuning that updates policy parameters, PGT keeps the policy frozen and updates only the latent goal using a trajectory-level preference objective. This approach essentially searches for the optimal conditioning input that maximizes the likelihood of preferred behaviors while suppressing undesirable ones. We evaluate PGT on the Minecraft SkillForge benchmark across 17 tasks. With minimal data, PGT achieves average relative improvements of 72.0\% and 81.6\% on two foundation policies, consistently outperforming expert-crafted prompts. Crucially, by decoupling task alignment (latent goal) from physical dynamics (frozen policy), PGT surpasses full fine-tuning by 13.4\% in out-of-distribution settings, demonstrating superior robustness and generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：偏好目标调谐：后训练作为冻结策略的潜在控制
目标条件策略使决策模型能够根据指定目标执行多样化行为，但其下游性能常高度依赖指令或提示的选择。为绕过离散文本提示的局限，我们将后训练适应建模为潜在控制问题，其中目标嵌入作为连续控制变量调节冻结策略的行为。我们提出偏好目标调谐（PGT）框架，通过优化该潜在控制变量，使诱导轨迹分布与任务偏好对齐。与标准微调不同，PGT保持策略冻结，仅更新潜在目标，使用轨迹级偏好目标。该方法本质上搜索最优条件输入，以最大化期望行为出现概率并抑制不良行为。我们在Minecraft SkillForge基准的17项任务上评估PGT，仅用少量数据，PGT在两种基础策略上平均相对提升72.0%和81.6%，持续优于专家设计提示。关键在于，通过将任务对齐（潜在目标）与物理动态（冻结策略）解耦，PGT在分布外设置中超越全微调13.4%，展现出更强的鲁棒性与泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of goal-conditioned policies to discrete text prompts, this work introduces Preference Goal Tuning (PGT), a method that treats post-training adaptation as optimizing a continuous latent goal embedding to control a frozen policy. Instead of updating policy weights, PGT tunes the goal representation to align trajectory distributions with task preferences using a trajectory-level preference objective. Evaluated on 17 Minecraft tasks, PGT achieves average relative improvements of 72.0% and 81.6% over two foundation policies with minimal data, consistently outperforming hand-crafted prompts. Notably, PGT surpasses full fine-tuning by 13.4% in out-of-distribution settings, demonstrating stronger robustness and generalization by decoupling goal alignment from policy dynamics.
为解决目标条件策略对离散文本提示高度敏感的问题，本文提出偏好目标调优（PGT）方法，将后训练适应视为潜在控制问题，通过优化连续目标嵌入来引导冻结策略生成偏好行为。与标准微调不同，PGT仅调整潜在目标，不更新策略参数，使用轨迹级偏好目标进行优化，以寻找最大化期望行为、抑制不良行为的最优条件输入。在Minecraft SkillForge基准的17项任务中，PGT仅用少量数据即在两个基础策略上实现72.0%和81.6%的平均相对提升，超越专家提示，并在分布外场景中比全微调高出13.4%，展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。
Stepper: Stepwise Immersive Scene Generation with Multiview Panoramas
Authors: Felix Wimbauer, Fabian Manhardt, Michael Oechsle, Nikolai Kalischek, Christian Rupprecht, Daniel Cremers, Federico Tombari
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T20:26:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:42:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026 Findings; Find our project page under https://fwmb.github.io/stepper/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The synthesis of immersive 3D scenes from text is rapidly maturing, driven by novel video generative models and feed-forward 3D reconstruction, with vast potential in AR/VR and world modeling. While panoramic images have proven effective for scene initialization, existing approaches suffer from a trade-off between visual fidelity and explorability: autoregressive expansion suffers from context drift, while panoramic video generation is limited to low resolution. We present Stepper, a unified framework for text-driven immersive 3D scene synthesis that circumvents these limitations via stepwise panoramic scene expansion. Stepper leverages a novel multi-view 360° diffusion model that enables consistent, high-resolution expansion, coupled with a geometry reconstruction pipeline that enforces geometric coherence. Trained on a new large-scale, multi-view panorama dataset, Stepper achieves state-of-the-art fidelity and structural consistency, outperforming prior approaches, thereby setting a new standard for immersive scene generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stepper：基于多视角全景图的分步沉浸式场景生成
从文本合成沉浸式3D场景的技术正迅速成熟，得益于新型视频生成模型和前馈3D重建方法，在AR/VR和世界建模领域具有巨大潜力。尽管全景图像已被证明能有效用于场景初始化，但现有方法在视觉保真度与探索性之间存在权衡：自回归扩展易出现上下文漂移，而全景视频生成受限于低分辨率。我们提出Stepper，一种统一的文本驱动沉浸式3D场景合成框架，通过分步全景场景扩展规避上述限制。Stepper采用新颖的多视角360°扩散模型，实现一致且高分辨率的扩展，并结合几何重建流程以确保几何一致性。在新构建的大规模多视角全景数据集上训练，Stepper在保真度与结构一致性方面达到当前最优，超越先前方法，为沉浸式场景生成树立新标杆。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity, explorable 3D scenes from text in AR/VR applications, Stepper introduces a stepwise panoramic expansion method that avoids the trade-offs of autoregressive drift and low-resolution video generation. It combines a novel 360° multi-view diffusion model for consistent, high-resolution scene growth with a geometry reconstruction pipeline to ensure structural coherence. Evaluated on a new large-scale multi-view panorama dataset, Stepper achieves state-of-the-art visual fidelity and geometric consistency, surpassing prior methods and establishing a new benchmark for immersive scene synthesis.
为满足AR/VR场景中从文本生成高保真、可探索3D场景的需求，Stepper提出一种分步全景扩展方法，规避了自回归生成的上下文漂移和全景视频低分辨率的局限。该方法结合新型360度多视角扩散模型实现高分辨率、一致性的场景扩展，并通过几何重建管道确保结构一致性。在新构建的大规模多视角全景数据集上评估，Stepper在视觉保真度和几何一致性上达到当前最优，超越现有方法，树立了沉浸式场景生成的新标杆。
How Alignment Routes: Localizing, Scaling, and Controlling Policy Circuits in Language Models
Authors: Gregory N. Frank
First: 2026-04-06T03:20:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:41:40+00:00
Comments: Code and data: https://github.com/gregfrank/how-alignment-routes
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We localize the policy routing mechanism in alignment-trained language models. An intermediate-layer attention gate reads detected content and triggers deeper amplifier heads that boost the signal toward refusal. In smaller models the gate and amplifier are single heads; at larger scale they become bands of heads across adjacent layers. The gate contributes under 1% of output DLA, yet interchange testing (p < 0.001) and knockout cascade confirm it is causally necessary. Interchange screening at n >= 120 detects the same motif in twelve models from six labs (2B to 72B), though specific heads differ by lab. Per-head ablation weakens up to 58x at 72B and misses gates that interchange identifies; at scale, interchange is the only reliable audit. Modulating the detection-layer signal continuously controls policy from hard refusal through evasion to factual answering. On safety prompts the same intervention turns refusal into harmful guidance, showing that the safety-trained capability is gated by routing, not removed. Thresholds vary by topic and by input language, and the circuit relocates across generations within a family even while behavioral benchmarks register no change. Routing is early-commitment: the gate fires at its own layer before deeper layers finish processing the input. An in-context substitution cipher collapses gate interchange necessity by 70 to 99% across three models, and the model switches to puzzle-solving rather than refusal. Injecting the plaintext gate activation into the cipher forward pass restores 48% of refusals in Phi-4-mini, localizing the bypass to the routing interface. A second method, cipher contrast analysis, uses plain/cipher DLA differences to map the full cipher-sensitive routing circuit in O(3n) forward passes. Any encoding that defeats detection-layer pattern matching bypasses the policy regardless of whether deeper layers reconstruct the content.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐如何路由：在语言模型中定位、扩展和控制策略电路
我们在对齐训练的语言模型中定位策略路由机制。中间层注意力门读取检测到的内容并触发深层放大头，将信号增强导向拒绝响应。在较小模型中，门和放大器为单个头；在更大规模模型中，它们扩展为相邻层中的一组头。该门贡献的输出DLA不足1%，但互换测试（p < 0.001）和剔除级联实验确认其因果必要性。在n >= 120时的互换筛选在六个实验室的十二个模型（2B至72B）中检测到相同模式，尽管各实验室的具体头不同。按头消融在72B模型中削弱效果最高达58倍，且遗漏互换测试识别的门；在大规模模型中，互换测试是唯一可靠的审计方法。连续调节检测层信号可控制策略，从强硬拒绝到规避再到事实回答。在安全提示下，相同干预使拒绝转化为有害引导，表明安全训练能力由路由机制控制而非被移除。阈值随主题和输入语言变化，且电路在家族内不同版本间迁移，而行为基准无变化。路由为早期承诺：门在自身层触发，早于深层处理完成输入。上下文替换密码使三模型中门互换必要性降低70%至99%，模型转为解谜而非拒绝。将明文门激活注入密码前向传播可恢复Phi-4-mini中48%的拒绝，定位绕过路径至路由接口。第二种方法，密码对比分析，利用明文/密码DLA差异在O(3n)前向传播中映射完整密码敏感路由电路。任何能绕过检测层模式匹配的编码均可绕过策略，无论深层是否重构内容。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how alignment-trained language models route policy decisions through localized neural circuits. Researchers identify an attention gate in intermediate layers that detects content and activates deeper amplifier heads to boost refusal signals; at scale, these components expand into head bands across adjacent layers. Despite contributing less than 1% of output DLA, the gate is causally necessary, confirmed by interchange testing and knockout cascades. The motif appears consistently across 12 models from six labs, though specific heads vary by lab. Modulating the detection-layer signal enables fine-grained control over policy behavior—from refusal to evasion to factual answering—while thresholding varies by topic and language. Routing occurs early, before deeper layers complete input processing, and can be bypassed by in-context ciphers, which collapse gate necessity by 70–99%, causing models to shift to puzzle-solving. Injecting plaintext gate activation restores 48% of refusals, localizing the bypass to the routing interface. A second method, cipher contrast analysis, maps the full cipher-sensitive circuit in O(3n) forward passes, revealing that any encoding that evades detection-layer pattern matching bypasses policy regardless of downstream reconstruction.
该研究探讨了对齐训练语言模型中政策决策的路由机制。研究人员在中间层发现一个注意力门控模块，用于检测内容并激活深层放大头以增强拒绝信号；在大模型中，这些模块扩展为相邻层的头组。尽管其输出DLA贡献不足1%，但通过互换测试和敲除级联实验确认其因果必要性。该模式在六家实验室的12个模型中普遍存在（2B至72B），但具体头位置因实验室而异。调节检测层信号可实现从拒绝到规避再到事实回答的精细政策控制，阈值随主题和输入语言变化。路由发生在早期，即深层处理尚未完成前，且可通过上下文替换密码绕过，使门控必要性降低70–99%，模型转为解谜而非拒绝。注入明文门控激活可恢复Phi-4-mini 48%的拒绝行为，定位绕过发生在路由接口。第二种方法——密码对比分析，通过明文/密文DLA差异在O(3n)前向传递中映射完整密码敏感路由电路，表明任何绕过检测层模式匹配的编码均可无视深层重建直接绕过政策。
Randomized Subspace Nesterov Accelerated Gradient
Authors: Gaku Omiya, Pierre-Louis Poirion, Akiko Takeda
First: 2026-05-01T15:40:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:40:10+00:00
Comments: 50 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Randomized-subspace methods reduce the cost of first-order optimization by using only low-dimensional projected-gradient information, a feature that is attractive in forward-mode automatic differentiation and communication-limited settings. While Nesterov acceleration is well understood for full-gradient and coordinate-based methods, obtaining accelerated methods for general subspace sketches that use only projected-gradient information and can improve over full-dimensional Nesterov acceleration in oracle complexity is technically nontrivial. We develop randomized-subspace Nesterov accelerated gradient methods for smooth convex and smooth strongly convex optimization under matrix smoothness and generic sketch moment assumptions. The key technical ingredient is a three-sequence formulation tailored to matrix smoothness, which recovers the corresponding classical Nesterov methods in the full-dimensional case. The resulting theory establishes accelerated oracle-complexity guarantees and makes explicit how matrix smoothness and the sketch distribution enter the complexity. It also provides a unified basis for comparing sketch families and identifying when randomized-subspace acceleration improves over full-dimensional Nesterov acceleration in oracle complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机子空间Nesterov加速梯度法
随机子空间方法通过仅使用低维投影梯度信息降低一阶优化的成本，这一特性在前向模式自动微分和通信受限场景中具有吸引力。虽然Nesterov加速在全梯度和坐标方法中已被充分理解，但针对仅使用投影梯度信息的一般子空间采样方法，获得能优于全维度Nesterov加速的加速方法在技术上颇具挑战。 我们针对矩阵光滑性和通用采样矩假设，开发了适用于光滑凸和光滑强凸优化的随机子空间Nesterov加速梯度方法。关键技术在于针对矩阵光滑性设计的三序列公式，该公式在全维度情况下可恢复经典Nesterov方法。所得理论建立了加速算子复杂度保证，并明确说明了矩阵光滑性与采样分布如何影响复杂度，同时为比较不同采样族并识别随机子空间加速何时优于全维度Nesterov加速提供了统一基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce computational cost in optimization under communication or differentiation constraints, this work develops randomized-subspace Nesterov accelerated gradient methods that leverage low-dimensional projected gradients. The method employs a three-sequence formulation tailored to matrix smoothness, enabling acceleration even when only subspace-sketch gradients are available. Experimental and theoretical results show that under generic sketch moment assumptions, the proposed framework achieves improved oracle complexity compared to full-dimensional Nesterov methods, particularly when the sketch distribution and matrix smoothness are favorable.
为降低通信受限或自动微分场景下的计算开销，本文提出基于随机子空间的Nesterov加速梯度方法，仅利用低维投影梯度信息。该方法采用针对矩阵光滑性的三序列形式，使在子空间采样梯度下仍可实现加速。理论与实验表明，在满足通用采样矩假设时，该框架在算子复杂度上优于全维度Nesterov方法，尤其当采样分布与矩阵光滑性匹配时效果更显著。
Temporal Data Requirement for Predicting Unplanned Hospital Readmissions
Authors: Ramin Mohammadi, Vahab vahdat, Sarthak Jain, Amir T. Namin, Ramya Palacholla, Sagar Kamarthi
First: 2026-05-01T15:38:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:38:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the proliferation of Electronic Health Records (EHRs), a critical challenge in building predictive models is determining the optimal historical data time window to maximize accuracy. This study investigates the impact of various observation windows ranging from the day of surgery to three years prior on predicting 30-day readmission following hip and knee arthroplasties. The dataset encompasses both structured encounter records (over 4 million) and unstructured clinical notes (80,000) from 7,174 patients. To extract meaning from the clinical notes, we employed a suite of non neural (BOW, count BOW, TF IDF, LDA) and neural encoders (BERT, 1D CNN, BiLSTM, Average). We subsequently evaluated models utilizing clinical notes alone, structured data alone, and a combination of both modalities. Our results demonstrate that the optimal time window for unstructured clinical notes is significantly shorter than for structured data, maximum predictive performance was achieved using notes from just three to six months prior to surgery. In contrast, performance using structured data improved as the time window lengthened, but strictly plateaued after twelve months. These modality-specific temporal patterns remained consistent regardless of model complexity or encoder type. Ultimately, these findings challenge the general assumption that more historical data inherently yields better machine learning predictions, establishing targeted time-window guidelines for optimizing readmission prediction models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预测非计划性医院再入院的时序数据需求
随着电子健康记录（EHR）的普及，构建预测模型时的关键挑战是确定最优历史数据时间窗口以最大化准确性。本研究探讨了从手术当日到三年前不同时长的观察窗口对髋关节和膝关节置换术后30天再入院预测的影响。数据集包含7174名患者的400多万条结构化就诊记录和8万份非结构化临床笔记。为从临床笔记中提取信息，我们采用了多种非神经网络（BOW、计数BOW、TF-IDF、LDA）和神经网络编码器（BERT、1D CNN、BiLSTM、平均编码）。随后，我们评估了仅使用临床笔记、仅使用结构化数据以及两者结合的模型。结果表明，非结构化临床笔记的最优时间窗口显著短于结构化数据，最大预测性能在术前三至六个月的笔记中实现。相比之下，结构化数据的性能随时间窗口延长而提升，但在十二个月后趋于平稳。这些模态特异性的时序模式在模型复杂度或编码器类型变化时保持一致。最终，这些发现挑战了“历史数据越多，机器学习预测效果越好”的普遍假设，确立了优化再入院预测模型的时间窗口指导原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize predictive models for unplanned hospital readmissions using Electronic Health Records, this study examines how varying historical time windows affect model accuracy for hip and knee arthroplasty patients. It evaluates both structured encounter data and unstructured clinical notes using multiple encoding methods, including non-neural and neural approaches, and tests combinations of both data types. Results show that clinical notes perform best with a short window (3–6 months pre-surgery), while structured data improves with longer windows but plateaus after 12 months, indicating modality-specific temporal requirements. These findings refute the assumption that more historical data always improves prediction and provide targeted time-window guidelines for model optimization.
为优化电子健康记录中用于预测髋关节和膝关节置换术后30天再入院的模型，本研究探讨不同历史时间窗口对模型准确率的影响。研究评估了结构化就诊记录与非结构化临床笔记两种数据模态，采用包括非神经网络和神经网络在内的多种编码方法，并测试单一模态或联合模态的模型表现。结果表明，临床笔记的最佳预测窗口为术前3至6个月，而结构化数据在延长窗口后性能提升但12个月后趋于稳定。这些模态特异的时间模式不受模型复杂度或编码器类型影响，挑战了“历史数据越多预测效果越好”的普遍假设，为模型优化提供了针对性的时间窗口指导。
To Call or Not to Call: A Framework to Assess and Optimize LLM Tool Calling
Authors: Qinyuan Wu, Soumi Das, Mahsa Amani, Arijit Nag, Seungeon Lee, Krishna P. Gummadi, Abhilasha Ravichander, Muhammad Bilal Zafar
First: 2026-05-01T15:38:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:38:13+00:00
Comments: Preprint, under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI architectures augment LLMs with external tools, unlocking strong capabilities. However, tool use is not always beneficial; some calls may be redundant or even harmful. Effective tool use, therefore, hinges on a core LLM decision: whether to call or not call a tool, when performing a task. This decision is particularly challenging for web search tools, where the benefits of external information depend on the model's internal knowledge and its ability to integrate potentially noisy tool responses. We introduce a principled framework inspired by decision-making theory to evaluate web search tool-use decisions along three key factors: necessity, utility, and affordability. Our analysis combines two complementary lenses: a normative perspective that infers true need and utility from an optimal allocation of tool calls, and a descriptive perspective that infers the model's self-perceived need and utility from their observed behaviors. We find that models' perceived need and utility of tool calls are often misaligned with their true need and utility. Building on this framework, we train lightweight estimators of need and utility based on models' hidden states. Our estimators enable simple controllers that can improve decision quality and lead to stronger task performance than the self-perceived set up across three tasks and six models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是否调用工具：评估与优化大语言模型工具调用的框架
代理型AI架构通过外部工具增强大语言模型能力，释放强大功能。然而，工具使用并非总是有益；某些调用可能冗余甚至有害。因此，有效工具使用的核心在于大语言模型的关键决策：在执行任务时是否调用工具。这一决策在网页搜索工具中尤为困难，因为外部信息的价值取决于模型的内部知识及其整合可能嘈杂工具响应的能力。我们引入一个基于决策理论的框架，从必要性、效用性和可负担性三个关键因素评估网页搜索工具调用决策。我们的分析结合两种互补视角：规范视角从最优工具调用分配推断真实需求与效用；描述视角从模型观察行为推断其自我感知的需求与效用。我们发现，模型对工具调用的感知需求与效用常与其真实需求与效用不一致。基于此框架，我们训练轻量级需求与效用估计器，基于模型隐藏状态。这些估计器支持简单控制器，可提升决策质量，并在三项任务和六种模型上实现优于自我感知设置的任务表现。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of determining whether large language models should invoke external tools, particularly web search, by proposing a decision-theoretic framework that evaluates tool calls based on necessity, utility, and affordability. The authors combine a normative lens—inferring true need and utility from optimal tool allocation—with a descriptive lens—observing the model’s self-perceived need and utility. Their analysis reveals frequent misalignment between perceived and actual tool value. To resolve this, they train lightweight state-based estimators to predict true need and utility, enabling controllers that outperform self-perceived decisions across three tasks and six models, improving overall task performance.
本文针对大语言模型是否应调用外部工具（特别是网络搜索）的问题，提出一个基于决策理论的框架，从必要性、效用性和可负担性三个维度评估工具调用。作者结合规范视角（从理想资源分配推断最优工具使用）与描述视角（从模型自身行为推断其感知需求），发现模型常将自身感知需求与实际最优需求错位。基于此框架，他们从模型隐藏状态训练轻量级需求与效用预测器，并用其指导简单控制器决策，结果在三项任务和六种模型上均优于模型自身感知的工具调用策略，显著提升任务表现。
Exploring the System 1 Thinking Capability of Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Wenyuan Zhang, Shuaiyi Nie, Xinghua Zhang, Zefeng Zhang, Tingwen Liu
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2025-04-14T16:13:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:37:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IJCAI 2026 (Main Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores the system 1 thinking capability of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), the intuitive ability to respond efficiently with minimal token usage. While existing LRMs rely on long-chain reasoning and excel at complex tasks, their system 1 thinking ability remains largely underexplored. This capability is essential as it reflects models' difficulty awareness and reasoning efficiency, both critical for real-world applications. We propose S1-Bench, a multi-domain, multilingual benchmark comprising model-simple system 1 questions. Our investigation of 28 LRMs reveals under-accuracy and inefficiency on system 1 problems. We find existing efficient reasoning methods either generalize poorly to simple questions or sacrifice performance for efficiency. Further exploration uncovers LRMs' early difficulty awareness accompanied by lower confidence, and shows that problem difficulty is implicitly encoded in hidden states.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索大推理模型的系统1思维能力
本文探讨了大推理模型（LRMs）的系统1思维能力——即以最少的token消耗高效直观地响应的能力。尽管现有LRMs依赖长链推理并在复杂任务中表现优异，其系统1思维能力仍鲜有研究。该能力至关重要，因为它反映了模型对难度的认知与推理效率，两者均对实际应用至关重要。我们提出了S1-Bench，一个涵盖多领域、多语言的模型简单系统1问题基准。对28个LRMs的调查发现，它们在系统1问题上存在准确率不足和效率低下。我们发现现有高效推理方法要么在简单问题上泛化能力差，要么为追求效率牺牲性能。进一步探索揭示LRMs在早期即表现出对难度的认知，但信心较低，且问题难度隐式编码于隐藏状态中。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the system 1 thinking capability of Large Reasoning Models, focusing on their intuitive, efficient responses to simple questions with minimal token usage. Motivated by the gap in understanding how LRMs handle straightforward reasoning tasks despite excelling at complex ones, the authors introduce S1-Bench, a multi-domain, multilingual benchmark designed to probe this ability. Evaluating 28 LRMs, they find consistent under-accuracy and inefficiency on system 1 problems, revealing that current efficient reasoning methods either fail to generalize or trade performance for speed. Further analysis shows LRMs exhibit early difficulty awareness with reduced confidence and that problem difficulty is implicitly encoded in hidden states.
本文研究了大推理模型的系统1思维能力，即其在简单问题上以最少token高效响应的直觉能力。鉴于现有模型擅长复杂任务却鲜少被评估其处理简单问题的能力，作者构建了多领域、多语言的S1-Bench基准测试。对28个模型的评估发现其在系统1任务上普遍存在准确率低和效率差的问题，现有高效推理方法要么泛化能力弱，要么牺牲性能换取效率。进一步分析表明，模型在早期即表现出对问题难度的感知并伴随信心降低，且问题难度隐含编码于隐藏状态中。
EASE: Federated Multimodal Unlearning via Entanglement-Aware Anchor Closure
Authors: Zihao Ding, Beining Wu, Jun Huang
First: 2026-05-01T15:33:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:33:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Multimodal Learning (FML) trains multimodal models across decentralized clients while keeping their image-text pairs private. However, joint embedding training entangles forgotten knowledge across both modalities and client gradient subspaces, hindering federated unlearning. Previous federated unlearning approaches neither sever the cross-modal reconstruction channel mediated by bilinear coupling nor separate forget-exclusive update directions from those shared with retained clients. We identify an Anchor Principle for federated multimodal contrastive unlearning: forgotten alignments persist through three residual anchors arising from bilinear cross-modal coupling, principal-angle subspace entanglement, and continued federated updates. At the modality level, we show that bilateral displacement of both visual and language branches closes the cross-modal reconstruction channel. Correspondingly, our method addresses subspace entanglement through Cosine--Sine decomposition of client-update subspaces, isolating forget-exclusive directions from retain support. Moreover, we propose a direction-selective Forget Lock that bounds residual drift across rounds. Combining these strategies, we present EASE, an Entanglement-Aware Subspace Excision framework that closes all three anchor channels under a unified design. EASE demonstrates consistent superiority across multiple datasets and unlearning scenarios, for instance, matching the retrain reference to within 0.2 and 4.2 R@1 points on the forget and retain sides under client unlearning on Flickr30K with CLIP-B/32.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EASE：通过纠缠感知锚点闭合实现联邦多模态遗忘学习
联邦多模态学习（FML）在分布式客户端上训练多模态模型，同时保持图像-文本对的私密性。然而，联合嵌入训练导致遗忘知识在模态间及客户端梯度子空间中相互纠缠，阻碍了联邦遗忘学习。此前的联邦遗忘方法既未切断由双线性耦合介导的跨模态重建通道，也未分离仅与遗忘相关的更新方向与与保留客户端共享的方向。我们为联邦多模态对比遗忘学习提出锚点原则：遗忘的对齐关系通过三类残余锚点持续存在，分别源于双线性跨模态耦合、主角子空间纠缠及持续的联邦更新。在模态层面，我们证明视觉与语言分支的双向位移可关闭跨模态重建通道。相应地，我们的方法通过余弦-正弦分解客户端更新子空间，解决子空间纠缠问题，将遗忘专属方向与保留支持方向分离。此外，我们提出方向选择性遗忘锁，限制跨轮次的残余漂移。综合这些策略，我们提出EASE（纠缠感知子空间剔除框架），在统一设计下关闭所有三类锚点通道。EASE在多个数据集和遗忘场景中表现一致优越，例如在Flickr30K数据集上使用CLIP-B/32模型进行客户端遗忘时，其遗忘与保留侧的R@1指标分别与重训练基准相差0.2和4.2点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of unlearning in federated multimodal learning where cross-modal entanglement impedes knowledge removal, the paper introduces EASE, a framework that targets three residual anchors—bilinear coupling, subspace entanglement, and continued federated updates—through bilateral displacement of modality branches, Cosine--Sine decomposition of client subspaces, and a direction-selective Forget Lock. Experimental results show EASE consistently outperforms prior methods, achieving near-retrain performance with minimal degradation: 0.2 and 4.2 R@1 point losses on forget and retain sides respectively under client unlearning on Flickr30K with CLIP-B/32.
为解决联邦多模态学习中跨模态纠缠导致的遗忘困难，EASE提出一种基于纠缠感知的框架，针对三类残余锚点——双线性耦合、子空间纠缠与持续联邦更新——进行针对性处理。通过双向位移视觉与语言分支关闭跨模态重建通道，利用余弦-正弦分解隔离遗忘专属更新方向，并引入方向选择性遗忘锁约束残差漂移。实验表明，EASE在多个数据集与遗忘场景下表现优越，于Flickr30K（CLIP-B/32）客户端遗忘任务中，遗忘与保留侧R@1偏差分别仅为0.2与4.2点，接近重训练基准。
WildfireVLM: AI-powered Analysis for Early Wildfire Detection and Risk Assessment Using Satellite Imagery
Authors: Aydin Ayanzadeh, Prakhar Dixit, Sadia Kamal, Milton Halem
First: 2026-02-09T19:40:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:32:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Wildfires are a growing threat to ecosystems, human lives, and infrastructure, with their frequency and intensity rising due to climate change and human activities. Early detection is critical, yet satellite-based monitoring remains challenging due to faint smoke signals, dynamic weather conditions, and the need for real-time analysis over large areas. We introduce WildfireVLM, an AI framework that combines satellite imagery wildfire detection with language-driven risk assessment. We construct a labeled wildfire and smoke dataset using imagery from Landsat-8/9, GOES-16, and other publicly available Earth observation sources, including harmonized products with aligned spectral bands. WildfireVLM employs YOLOv12 to detect fire zones and smoke plumes, leveraging its ability to detect small, complex patterns in satellite imagery. We integrate Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) that convert detection outputs into contextualized risk assessments and prioritized response recommendations for disaster management. We validate the quality of risk reasoning using an LLM-as-judge evaluation with a shared rubric. The system is deployed using a service-oriented architecture that supports real-time processing, visual risk dashboards, and long-term wildfire tracking, demonstrating the value of combining computer vision with language-based reasoning for scalable wildfire monitoring. The code and dataset are publicly available on GitHub at https://github.com/Ayanzadeh93/_WildfireVLM_.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WildfireVLM：基于卫星图像的AI驱动野火早期检测与风险评估
野火正日益威胁生态系统、人类生命和基础设施，其发生频率和强度因气候变化和人类活动而上升。早期检测至关重要，但基于卫星的监测面临烟雾信号微弱、天气动态变化及需对大面积区域进行实时分析等挑战。我们提出WildfireVLM，一个结合卫星图像野火检测与语言驱动风险评估的AI框架。我们利用Landsat-8/9、GOES-16及其他公开地球观测数据源构建标注好的野火与烟雾数据集，包括光谱波段对齐的统一产品。WildfireVLM采用YOLOv12检测火区与烟雾带，利用其在卫星图像中识别小而复杂模式的能力。我们集成多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），将检测结果转化为情境化风险评估及灾害管理优先响应建议。我们通过LLM作为裁判的评估方法，依据统一评分标准验证风险推理质量。系统采用服务导向架构，支持实时处理、可视化风险仪表盘及长期野火追踪，展示了计算机视觉与语言推理结合在可扩展野火监测中的价值。代码与数据集已公开于GitHub：https://github.com/Ayanzadeh93/_WildfireVLM_。

The method uses YOLOv12 to identify fire zones and smoke plumes from Landsat-8/9 and GOES-16 imagery, then feeds detection results into Multimodal Large Language Models to generate contextual risk evaluations and response priorities. Empowering Heterogeneous Graph Foundation Models via Decoupled Relation Alignment
Authors: Ziyu Zheng, Yaming Yang, Zhe Wang, Ziyu Guan, Wei Zhao
First: 2026-05-01T15:31:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:31:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have achieved remarkable success in homogeneous graphs, extending them to multi-domain heterogeneous graphs (MDHGs) remains a formidable challenge due to cross-type feature shifts and intra-domain relation gaps. Existing global feature alignment methods (PCA or SVD) enforce a shared feature space blindly, which distorts type-specific semantics and disrupts original topologies, inevitably leading to "Type Collapse" and "Relation Confusion". To address these fundamental limitations, we propose Decoupled relation Subspace Alignment (DRSA), a novel, plug-and-play relation-driven alignment framework. DRSA fundamentally shifts the paradigm by decoupling feature semantics from relation structures. Specifically, it introduces a dual-relation subspace projection mechanism to coordinate cross-type interactions within a shared low-rank relation subspace explicitly. Furthermore, a feature-structure decoupled representation is designed to decompose aligned features into a semantic projection component and a structural residual term, adaptively absorbing intra-domain variations. Optimized via a stable alternating minimization strategy based on Block Coordinate Descent, DRSA constructs a well-calibrated, structure-aware latent space. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that DRSA can be seamlessly integrated as a universal preprocessing module, significantly and consistently enhancing the cross-domain and few-shot knowledge transfer capabilities of state-of-the-art GFMs. The code is available at: https://github.com/zhengziyu77/DSRA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过解耦关系对齐赋能异构图基础模型
尽管图基础模型（GFMs）在同构图中取得了显著成功，但将其扩展到多域异构图（MDHGs）仍面临严峻挑战，原因在于跨类型特征偏移和域内关系差距。现有全局特征对齐方法（如PCA或SVD）盲目强制共享特征空间，扭曲了类型特异性语义并破坏原始拓扑结构，不可避免地导致“类型坍缩”和“关系混淆”。为解决这些根本性局限，我们提出解耦关系子空间对齐（DRSA），一种新颖的、即插即用的关系驱动对齐框架。DRSA通过解耦特征语义与关系结构，从根本上改变了范式。具体而言，它引入双关系子空间投影机制，在共享低秩关系子空间中显式协调跨类型交互。此外，设计了一种特征-结构解耦表示，将对齐特征分解为语义投影分量和结构残差项，自适应吸收域内变化。DRSA通过基于块坐标下降的稳定交替最小化策略进行优化，构建了校准良好、结构感知的潜在空间。在多个真实世界基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，DRSA可无缝集成作为通用预处理模块，显著且稳定地增强当前最先进GFMs的跨域和小样本知识迁移能力。代码见：https://github.com/zhengziyu77/DSRA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of extending Graph Foundation Models to multi-domain heterogeneous graphs where cross-type feature shifts and intra-domain relation gaps cause semantic distortion and topology disruption, the paper proposes Decoupled Relation Subspace Alignment (DRSA), a plug-and-play framework that decouples feature semantics from relation structures. DRSA employs a dual-relation subspace projection to coordinate cross-type interactions within a shared low-rank relation space and a feature-structure decoupled representation to separate semantic projections from structural residuals, adaptively handling intra-domain variations. Optimized via alternating minimization, DRSA constructs a structure-aware latent space that enhances cross-domain and few-shot knowledge transfer. Experiments across multiple real-world benchmarks show DRSA consistently improves performance when integrated as a preprocessing module, mitigating "Type Collapse" and "Relation Confusion" without altering the core model.
为解决图基础模型在多域异构图上的性能瓶颈，本文提出解耦关系子空间对齐（DRSA）框架，通过将特征语义与关系结构解耦，采用双关系子空间投影协调跨类型交互，并设计特征-结构解耦表示以自适应吸收域内差异。在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，DRSA作为预处理模块可稳定提升主流图模型的跨域与少样本知识迁移能力，有效缓解了类型坍缩与关系混淆问题，同时保持原始图拓扑结构不变。
Weisfeiler Lehman Test on Combinatorial Complexes: Generalized Expressive Power of Topological Neural Networks
Authors: Jiawen Chen, Qi Shao, Duxin Chen, Wenwu Yu
First: 2026-05-01T15:19:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:19:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Combinatorial complexes have unified set-based (e.g., graphs, hypergraphs) and part-whole (e.g., simplicial, cellular complexes) structures into a common topological framework. Existing topological neural networks and Weisfeiler-Lehman variants remain fragmented, lacking a unified theoretical foundation for topological deep learning. In this work, we introduce the Combinatorial Complex Weisfeiler-Lehman (CCWL) test, an axiomatic-style extension of the WL test to combinatorial complexes. CCWL formalizes topological message passing through four types of neighborhood relation and provides a unified perspective on the expressive power of higher-order variants. We further prove that upper and lower neighborhoods are sufficient among the four adjacent WL tests to reach the expressivity of the full CCWL framework across topological structures of combinatorial complexes. Building on this framework, we also propose the Combinatorial Complex Isomorphism Network (CCIN) and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Experimental results indicate CCIN outperforms baseline methods and offers a generalized expressive framework for topological deep learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：组合复形上的Weisfeiler-Lehman测试：拓扑神经网络的广义表达能力
组合复形将基于集合的结构（如图、超图）与部分-整体结构（如单纯复形、胞腔复形）统一到共同的拓扑框架中。现有的拓扑神经网络及Weisfeiler-Lehman变体仍缺乏统一的理论基础。本文引入组合复形Weisfeiler-Lehman（CCWL）测试，作为WL测试在组合复形上的公理化扩展。CCWL通过四种邻域关系形式化拓扑消息传递，并为高阶变体的表达能力提供统一视角。我们进一步证明，在四种相邻WL测试中，仅需上邻域与下邻域即可达到CCWL框架在组合复形拓扑结构中的完整表达能力。基于此框架，我们提出组合复形同构网络（CCIN），并在合成与真实世界基准上评估其性能。实验结果表明，CCIN优于基线方法，为拓扑深度学习提供广义表达框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified theoretical foundation for topological deep learning, this work introduces the Combinatorial Complex Weisfeiler-Lehman (CCWL) test, an axiomatic extension of the WL test to combinatorial complexes that formalizes topological message passing via four neighborhood relations. The authors prove that upper and lower neighborhoods alone suffice to achieve full CCWL expressivity across combinatorial topological structures. Building on this, they propose the Combinatorial Complex Isomorphism Network (CCIN), which demonstrates superior performance over baseline methods on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, validating its generalized expressive power for topological neural networks.
为构建拓扑深度学习的统一理论基础，本文提出组合复形魏尔斯特拉斯-莱曼（CCWL）测试，作为WL测试在组合复形上的公理化扩展，通过四种邻域关系形式化拓扑消息传递。作者证明，仅需上邻域与下邻域即可达到CCWL框架的完整表达能力，适用于各类组合拓扑结构。在此基础上，提出组合复形同构网络（CCIN），并在合成与真实世界基准上验证其性能，实验表明CCIN优于现有基线方法，为拓扑神经网络提供了通用表达框架。
SoK: Security of Autonomous LLM Agents in Agentic Commerce
Authors: Qian'ang Mao, Jiaxin Wang, Ya Liu, Li Zhu, Cong Ma, Jiaqi Yan
First: 2026-04-15T01:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:12:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents such as OpenClaw are pushing agentic commerce from human-supervised assistance toward machine actors that can negotiate, purchase services, manage digital assets, and execute transactions across on-chain and off-chain environments. Protocols such as the Trustless Agents standard (ERC-8004), Agent Payments Protocol (AP2), OKX Agent Payments Protocol (APP), the HTTP 402-based payment protocol (x402), Agent Commerce Protocol (ACP), the Agentic Commerce standard (ERC-8183), and Machine Payments Protocol (MPP) enable this transition, but they also create an attack surface that existing security frameworks do not capture well. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) develops a unified security framework for autonomous LLM agents in commerce and finance. We organize threats along five dimensions: agent integrity, transaction authorization, inter-agent trust, market manipulation, and regulatory compliance. From a systematically curated public corpus of academic papers, protocol documents, industry reports, and incident evidence, we derive 12 cross-layer attack vectors and show how failures propagate from reasoning and tooling layers into custody, settlement, market harm, and compliance exposure. We then propose a layered defense architecture addressing authorization gaps left by current agent-payment protocols. Overall, our analysis shows that securing agentic commerce is inherently a cross-layer problem that requires coordinated controls across LLM safety, protocol design, identity, market structure, and regulation. We conclude with a research roadmap and a benchmark agenda for secure autonomous commerce.
中文标题/摘要

Decentralized Proximal Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics
Authors: Mohammad Rafiqul Islam, Lingjiong Zhu
First: 2026-05-01T15:11:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:11:06+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose Decentralized Proximal Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (DE-PSGLD), a decentralized Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithm for sampling from a log-concave probability distribution constrained to a convex domain. Constraints are enforced through a shared proximal regularization based on the Moreau-Yosida envelope, enabling unconstrained updates while preserving consistency with the target constrained posterior. We establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees in the 2-Wasserstein distance for both individual agent iterates and their network averages. Our analysis shows that DE-PSGLD converges to a regularized Gibbs distribution and quantifies the bias introduced by the proximal approximation. We evaluate DE-PSGLD for different sampling problems on synthetic and real datasets. As the first decentralized approach for constrained domains, our algorithm exhibits fast posterior concentration and high predictive accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：去中心化近端随机梯度朗之万动力学
我们提出了去中心化近端随机梯度朗之万动力学（DE-PSGLD），这是一种用于从凸域内约束的对数凹概率分布中采样的去中心化马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）算法。通过基于Moreau-Yosida包络的共享近端正则化来强制约束，使得更新无需显式约束但仍保持与目标约束后验的一致性。我们建立了在2- Wasserstein距离下对单个代理迭代及其网络平均值的非渐近收敛保证。分析表明，DE-PSGLD收敛于一个正则化Gibbs分布，并量化了近端近似引入的偏差。我们在合成和真实数据集上评估了DE-PSGLD在不同采样问题中的表现。作为首个针对约束域的去中心化方法，我们的算法表现出快速的后验集中和高预测精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for decentralized sampling in constrained log-concave distributions, this paper introduces DE-PSGLD, a decentralized MCMC algorithm that enforces convex constraints via a shared proximal regularization based on the Moreau-Yosida envelope. The method allows each agent to perform unconstrained stochastic gradient Langevin updates while maintaining consistency with the target posterior. Theoretical analysis proves non-asymptotic convergence in 2-Wasserstein distance for both individual and network-averaged iterates, showing convergence to a regularized Gibbs distribution and quantifying proximal bias. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate fast posterior concentration and high predictive accuracy, establishing DE-PSGLD as the first decentralized approach for constrained domains.
为解决在凸约束下高效分布式采样问题，本文提出DE-PSGLD算法，通过Moreau-Yosida包络的共享近端正则化，使各节点可在无约束条件下更新，同时保持与目标约束后验分布的一致性。理论分析证明其在2-Wasserstein距离下具有非渐近收敛性，个体与网络平均迭代均收敛至正则化Gibbs分布，并量化了近端近似的偏差。在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，该算法收敛速度快、预测精度高，是首个面向约束域的分布式采样方法。
Exploring the Limits of End-to-End Feature-Affinity Propagation for Single-Point Supervised Infrared Small Target Detection
Authors: Qiancheng Zhou, Wenhua Zhang
First: 2026-05-01T15:08:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:08:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Single-point supervised infrared small target detection (IRSTD) drastically reduces dense annotation costs. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods achieve high precision by recovering mask supervision through explicit, offline pseudo-label construction, such as multi-stage active learning and physics-driven mask generation. In this paper, we study a minimalist alternative: generating point-to-mask supervision online through in-batch, point-anchored feature-affinity propagation. We instantiate this paradigm as GSACP, an end-to-end testbed that directly supervises the detector using hard-margin feature affinity gated by local image priors, entirely eliminating external label-evolution loops. This compact design, however, exposes an optimization bottleneck. Because the affinity target is generated from the same feature representation being optimized, training forms a self-referential loop. We theoretically formalize this as \emph{Self-Referential Propagation Drift}, a representation-supervision entanglement that can sharpen true boundaries or distort the feature space to satisfy its own targets. To systematically isolate these failure modes, we apply a protocolized single-variable ablation procedure spanning local EMA teacher decoupling, hard-background contrastive separation, and adaptive support geometry. On the SIRST3 dataset, GSACP-Final establishes a new ultra-low false-alarm operating regime, achieving a highly competitive $0.6674$ mIoU while demonstrating a $38\% relative reduction in false-positive artifacts ($\mathrm{Fa}$) compared with PAL. By systematically deconstructing the end-to-end paradigm, we map its performance boundaries and show that in-batch feature propagation provides a compact alternative for deployment scenarios where false-alarm suppression is paramount.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索端到端特征亲和力传播在单点监督红外小目标检测中的极限
单点监督红外小目标检测（IRSTD）大幅降低了密集标注成本。当前最先进（SOTA）方法通过显式、离线伪标签构建（如多阶段主动学习和物理驱动掩码生成）恢复掩码监督，从而实现高精度。本文研究一种极简替代方案：通过批内、点锚定的特征亲和力传播，在线生成点到掩码监督。我们将其具体化为GSACP，一个端到端测试平台，直接利用硬边距特征亲和力（由局部图像先验门控）监督检测器，完全消除外部标签演化循环。 然而，该紧凑设计暴露了优化瓶颈：由于亲和力目标由正在优化的同一特征表示生成，训练形成自指循环。我们将其理论形式化为\emph{自指传播漂移}，即表示与监督的纠缠，可能锐化真实边界或扭曲特征空间以满足自身目标。为系统性隔离这些失效模式，我们应用协议化的单变量消融程序，涵盖局部EMA教师解耦、硬背景对比分离和自适应支持几何。 在SIRST3数据集上，GSACP-Final建立了一个超低虚警操作模式，实现0.6674 mIoU，相比PAL在虚警（Fa）上降低38%。通过系统性解构端到端范式，我们绘制其性能边界，表明批内特征传播为虚警抑制至关重要的部署场景提供了一种紧凑替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce annotation costs in infrared small target detection, this paper proposes GSACP, an end-to-end method that generates point-to-mask supervision via in-batch feature-affinity propagation anchored to detected points, eliminating external pseudo-label loops. The method uses hard-margin affinity gated by local image priors, but its self-referential training introduces a theoretical drift where feature representations and supervision entangle, potentially distorting feature space. To diagnose this, the authors apply a systematic ablation protocol targeting teacher decoupling, background contrast, and support geometry. Experiments on SIRST3 show GSACP-Final achieves 0.6674 mIoU with 38% fewer false positives than PAL, demonstrating that in-batch propagation can deliver high precision while suppressing false alarms, albeit with performance limits tied to this self-referential design.
为降低红外小目标检测的标注成本，本文提出GSACP方法，通过批内点锚定特征亲和度传播在线生成点到掩码监督，完全消除外部伪标签循环。该方法利用硬边亲和度门控局部图像先验直接监督检测器，但因监督与特征表示相互依赖，导致‘自指传播漂移’优化瓶颈，可能扭曲特征空间。为系统隔离失效模式，作者对教师解耦、背景对比分离和支撑几何自适应等变量进行单变量消融实验。在SIRST3数据集上，GSACP-Final实现0.6674 mIoU，并较PAL降低38%误检率，表明批内传播可提供紧凑、高精度且强抑制误报的检测方案。
Towards Improving Speaker Distance Estimation through Generative Impulse Response Augmentation
Authors: Anton Ratnarajah, Mehmet Ergezer, Arun Nair, Mrudula Athi
Venue: ICASSP 2025
First: 2026-05-01T15:08:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:08:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Generative Data Augmentation for Real-World Signal Processing Applications (GenDA 2025). An ICASSP 2025 Satellite Workshop and IEEE Data Science and Learning Workshop: Room Acoustics and Speaker Distance Estimation Challenge
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Room Acoustics and Speaker Distance Estimation (SDE) Challenge at ICASSP 2025 explores the effectiveness of augmented room impulse response (RIR) data for improving SDE model performance. This challenge at GenDARA involves generating RIRs to supplement sparse datasets and fine-tuning SDE models with the augmented data. We employ the open-source fast diffuse room impulse response generator (FastRIR) conditioned only on speaker and listener locations. We design a quality filter to ensure generated RIR alignment with challenge RIRs, and hyperparameter optimization is employed for model fine-tuning. Our approach reduces the mean absolute error (MAE) of the five positions from 1.66m to 0.6m for GWA rooms and from 2.18m to 0.69m for Treble rooms, with results demonstrating that the augmentation approach significantly improves estimation accuracy, particularly at medium to long distances.

为提升房间声学中的说话人距离估计（SDE）性能，该研究提出通过生成式脉冲响应（RIR）增强来缓解数据稀疏问题。方法采用仅依赖说话人与听者位置的开源FastRIR生成器，并引入质量过滤器确保合成RIR与挑战赛基准对齐，同时通过超参数优化微调SDE模型。实验结果表明，平均绝对误差显著降低：GWA房间从1.66米降至0.6米，Treble房间从2.18米降至0.69米，证明RIR增强对中长距离估计的准确性提升尤为显著。
Unpaired Image Deraining Using Reward-Guided Self-Reinforcement Strategy
Authors: Yinghao Chen, Yeying Jin, Xiang Chen, Yanyan Wei, Ziyang Yan, Yaowen Fu
First: 2026-05-01T15:08:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:08:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised deraining has attracted attention for its ability to learn the real-world distribution of rain without paired supervision. However, the lack of strong constraints makes it difficult for the network to converge, especially with the complex diversity of rain degradation. A key motivation is that high-quality deraining results occasionally emerge during training, which can be leveraged to guide the optimization process. To overcome these challenges, we introduce RGSUD (Reward-Guided Self-Reinforcement Unsupervised Image Deraining), comprising two key stages: reward recycling and self-reinforcement (SR) training. For the former stage, we propose an Image Quality Assessment (IQA)-based dynamic reward recycling mechanism that selects optimal derained outputs during training and continuously collects high-quality deraining images. In latter stage, we incorporate these rewards into the model's optimization process, constraining the optimization space and improving alignment between derained outputs and clean images. By leveraging IQA-based self-reinforced loss and dynamically updated rewards, we enhance the quality of synthesized pseudo-paired data and stabilize the optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves SOTA performance across multiple datasets, including paired synthetic, paired real, and unpaired real images, outperforming existing unsupervised deraining approaches in both subjective and objective IQA metrics. Additionally, we show that the self-reinforcement strategy is adaptable to other unsupervised deraining methods and our deraining framework demonstrates strong generalization across existing supervised deraining networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于奖励引导自强化策略的无配对图像去雨
无监督去雨因其无需配对监督即可学习真实世界雨滴分布而受到关注。然而，缺乏强约束使得网络难以收敛，尤其面对雨滴退化复杂多样的情况。关键动机在于训练过程中偶尔会出现高质量去雨结果，可被用于引导优化过程。为解决这些问题，我们提出RGSUD（奖励引导自强化无监督图像去雨），包含两个关键阶段：奖励循环与自强化（SR）训练。前者阶段，我们提出基于图像质量评估（IQA）的动态奖励循环机制，在训练中选择最优去雨输出并持续收集高质量去雨图像；后者阶段，将这些奖励融入模型优化过程，约束优化空间并提升去雨输出与干净图像的一致性。通过IQA引导的自强化损失与动态更新的奖励，我们提升了合成伪配对数据的质量并稳定了优化过程。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多个数据集（包括配对合成、配对真实及无配对真实图像）上均达到最先进性能，在主观与客观IQA指标上均优于现有无监督去雨方法。此外，我们证明自强化策略可适配其他无监督去雨方法，且我们的去雨框架在现有监督去雨网络中展现出强泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of training unsupervised deraining models due to lack of paired data and complex rain degradation, this work introduces RGSUD, a reward-guided self-reinforcement strategy that leverages high-quality derained outputs emerging during training. It consists of two stages: a reward recycling phase using IQA-based dynamic selection to collect high-quality derained images, and a self-reinforcement training phase that incorporates these rewards into the loss function to constrain optimization and improve alignment with clean images. Experiments show RGSUD achieves state-of-the-art performance across synthetic, real paired, and unpaired real datasets, outperforming existing unsupervised methods in both subjective and objective metrics, and demonstrates generalizability to other deraining architectures.
为解决无配对数据下雨滴去除模型训练困难、收敛性差的问题，本文提出RGSUD方法，包含两个阶段：第一阶段通过基于图像质量评估（IQA）的动态奖励回收机制，在训练中筛选高质量去雨结果并构建伪配对数据集；第二阶段将这些奖励融入自强化训练，约束优化空间并提升去雨结果与干净图像的一致性。实验表明，该方法在合成、真实配对及无配对数据集上均达到当前最优性能，显著优于现有无监督去雨方法，并可广泛适配其他去雨架构。
Learning Coarse-to-Fine Osteoarthritis Representations under Noisy Hierarchical Labels
Authors: Tongxu Zhang
First: 2026-05-01T15:07:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:07:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knee osteoarthritis (OA) assessment involves a natural but often underused label hierarchy: a coarse binary OA decision and a fine-grained Kellgren--Lawrence (KL) severity grade. Existing deep learning studies commonly treat these targets as separate classification problems, either reducing OA assessment to disease presence or directly optimizing noisy ordinal KL labels. In this work, we ask whether this clinical hierarchy can serve as a representation-level supervisory prior. Rather than introducing a complex architecture, we use a deliberately simple dual-head model with a shared encoder and two task-specific heads as a probe of hierarchical supervision. We compare single-OA, single-KL, and dual-head training across multiple 3D backbones under the same test protocol. Beyond standard classification metrics, we perform paired statistical comparisons, analyze latent severity-axis geometry, and examine saliency overlap with cartilage regions. The results show that dual-head supervision produces backbone-dependent gains, with clear improvements in KL-related metrics for selected backbones. More importantly, the gains are accompanied by a more ordered coarse-to-fine latent organization and, for responsive backbones, stronger anatomical alignment of saliency with cartilage. These findings suggest that even simple hierarchical dual-head supervision can reshape disease representations under noisy coarse/fine labels, providing a useful inductive bias for OA diagnosis and severity grading.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在噪声分层标签下学习粗到细的骨关节炎表示
膝骨关节炎（OA）评估涉及一个自然但常被忽视的标签层次结构：粗粒度的二元OA决策和细粒度的Kellgren-Lawrence（KL）严重等级。现有深度学习研究通常将这些目标视为独立的分类问题，要么将OA评估简化为疾病存在与否，要么直接优化噪声有序的KL标签。本研究探讨该临床层次结构是否可作为表示层面的监督先验。我们未引入复杂架构，而是采用一个刻意简单的双头模型（共享编码器和两个任务特定头）作为分层监督的探针。我们在多个3D主干网络上比较单OA、单KL和双头训练方案，在相同测试协议下进行评估。除标准分类指标外，我们进行成对统计比较，分析潜在严重度轴的几何结构，并考察显著性区域与软骨区域的重叠情况。结果表明，双头监督在不同主干网络中产生依赖于网络结构的性能提升，部分主干网络在KL相关指标上表现明显改善。更重要的是，性能提升伴随着更有序的粗到细潜在组织结构，且对响应性主干网络而言，显著性区域与软骨的解剖对齐更强。这些发现表明，即使简单的分层双头监督也能在噪声粗/细标签下重塑疾病表示，为OA诊断和严重程度分级提供有用的归纳偏置。

Using a simple dual-head model with shared encoder and task-specific heads, it compares training strategies across multiple 3D backbones: single-OA, single-KL, and dual-head. Results show that dual-head training yields backbone-dependent improvements in KL-related metrics, alongside more ordered latent representations aligned with severity progression and stronger anatomical saliency matching cartilage regions, suggesting hierarchical supervision enhances both diagnostic accuracy and interpretability.
鉴于膝骨关节炎评估中存在天然但常被忽视的层级标签——粗粒度二分类OA判断与细粒度KL严重等级——本研究探讨该层级结构是否可作为深度学习表示的监督先验。采用共享编码器与两个任务特定头的简单双头模型，对比单OA、单KL及双头训练在多个3D主干网络上的表现。结果表明，双头训练在特定主干网络上显著提升KL相关指标，同时伴随更有序的潜在空间组织结构及更强的解剖学显著性与软骨区域对齐，说明即使结构简单，层级监督也能有效重塑疾病表征，为OA诊断与分级提供有益的归纳偏置。
From Unstructured Recall to Schema-Grounded Memory: Reliable AI Memory via Iterative, Schema-Aware Extraction
Authors: Alex Petrov, Alexander Gusak, Denis Mukha, Dima Korolev
First: 2026-04-30T14:14:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:07:06+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Persistent AI memory is often reduced to a retrieval problem: store prior interactions as text, embed them, and ask the model to recover relevant context later. This design is useful for thematic recall, but it is mismatched to the kinds of memory that agents need in production: exact facts, current state, updates and deletions, aggregation, relations, negative queries, and explicit unknowns. These operations require memory to behave less like search and more like a system of record. This paper argues that reliable external AI memory must be schema-grounded. Schemas define what must be remembered, what may be ignored, and which values must never be inferred. We present an iterative, schema-aware write path that decomposes memory ingestion into object detection, field detection, and field-value extraction, with validation gates, local retries, and stateful prompt control. The result shifts interpretation from the read path to the write path: reads become constrained queries over verified records rather than repeated inference over retrieved prose. We evaluate this design on structured extraction and end-to-end memory benchmarks. On the extraction benchmark, the judge-in-the-loop configuration reaches 90.42% object-level accuracy and 62.67% output accuracy, above all tested frontier structured-output baselines. On our end-to-end memory benchmark, xmemory reaches 97.10% F1, compared with 80.16%-87.24% across the third-party baselines. On the application-level task, xmemory reaches 95.2% accuracy, outperforming specialised memory systems, code-generated Markdown harnesses, and customer-facing frontier-model application harnesses. The results show that, for memory workloads requiring stable facts and stateful computation, architecture matters more than retrieval scale or model strength alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从无结构回忆到模式锚定记忆：通过迭代式、模式感知提取实现可靠的人工智能记忆
持久化人工智能记忆常被简化为检索问题：将先前交互存储为文本、嵌入并让模型在后续恢复相关上下文。这种设计适用于主题性回忆，但与生产环境中代理所需的记忆类型不匹配：精确事实、当前状态、更新与删除、聚合、关系、负查询和显式未知项。这些操作要求记忆系统更像一个记录系统，而非搜索系统。 本文主张，可靠的人工智能外部记忆必须基于模式锚定。模式定义了必须记忆的内容、可忽略的内容以及哪些值绝不可推断。我们提出了一种迭代式、模式感知的写入路径，将记忆摄入分解为对象检测、字段检测和字段-值提取，并配备验证门、局部重试和状态化提示控制。结果是，解释重心从读取路径转移到写入路径：读取变为对已验证记录的受限查询，而非对检索到的文本重复推理。 我们在结构化提取和端到端记忆基准上评估该设计。在提取基准上，循环法官配置达到90.42%的对象级准确率和62.67%的输出准确率，优于所有测试的前沿结构化输出基线。在我们的端到端记忆基准上，xmemory达到97.10%的F1值，而第三方基线的F1值介于80.16%至87.24%之间。在应用级任务中，xmemory达到95.2%的准确率，优于专用记忆系统、代码生成的Markdown框架以及面向客户的前沿模型应用框架。结果表明，对于需要稳定事实和状态化计算的记忆工作负载，架构比检索规模或模型能力本身更重要。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitations of current AI memory systems, which treat memory as a retrieval problem rather than a structured system of record needed for exact facts, state updates, and complex queries. The authors propose a schema-grounded memory architecture that enforces memory structure through iterative, schema-aware write operations involving object detection, field extraction, and validation gates. Experimental results show that this approach achieves 90.42% object-level accuracy and 62.67% output accuracy on structured extraction benchmarks, and 97.10% F1 score on an end-to-end memory benchmark, significantly outperforming existing baselines. In application tasks, it also achieves 95.2% accuracy, demonstrating that architectural design for schema-grounded memory is more critical than retrieval scale or model strength for reliable, stateful memory workloads.
本文指出当前AI记忆系统将记忆简化为检索问题，无法满足生产环境中对精确事实、状态更新和复杂查询的需求。作者提出一种基于模式的外部记忆架构，通过迭代式、模式感知的写入路径，包括对象检测、字段识别与值提取，并辅以验证门和状态化提示控制。实验表明，该方法在结构化提取任务中达到90.42%对象级准确率和62.67%输出准确率，显著优于现有前沿基线；在端到端记忆基准测试中F1值达97.10%，远超第三方基线（80.16%-87.24%）；在应用任务中准确率达95.2%，证明架构设计比检索规模或模型能力更能决定记忆系统的可靠性。
Aitchison Embeddings for Learning Compositional Graph Representations
Authors: Nikolaos Nakis, Chrysoula Kosma, Panagiotis Promponas, Michail Chatzianastasis, Giannis Nikolentzos
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-05-01T15:05:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:05:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted version to ICML; It will updated soon with the camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation learning is central to graph machine learning, powering tasks such as link prediction and node classification. However, most graph embeddings are hard to interpret, offering limited insight into how learned features relate to graph structure. Many networks naturally admit a role-mixture view, where nodes are best described as mixtures over latent archetypal factors. Motivated by this structure, we propose a compositional graph embedding framework grounded in Aitchison geometry, the canonical geometry for comparing mixtures. Nodes are represented as simplex-valued compositions and embedded via isometric log-ratio (ILR) coordinates, which preserve Aitchison distances while enabling unconstrained optimization in Euclidean space. This yields intrinsically interpretable embeddings whose geometry reflects relative trade-offs among archetypes and supports coherent behavior under component restriction; we consider both fixed and learnable ILR bases. Across node classification and link prediction, our method achieves competitive performance with strong baselines while providing explainability by construction rather than post-hoc. Finally, subcompositional coherence enables principled component restriction: removing and renormalizing subsets preserves a well-defined geometry, which we exploit via subcompositional dimensionality removal to probe how archetype groups influence representations and predictions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Aitchison嵌入的学习组合图表示
图机器学习的核心是表示学习，支撑诸如链接预测和节点分类等任务。然而，大多数图嵌入难以解释，难以揭示所学特征与图结构的关系。许多网络天然具有角色混合视角，即节点最好被描述为潜在原型因子的混合。受此结构启发，我们提出一种基于Aitchison几何的组合图嵌入框架，Aitchison几何是用于比较混合的标准几何。节点表示为单纯形值组合，并通过等距对数比（ILR）坐标嵌入，该坐标保留Aitchison距离，同时允许在欧几里得空间中进行无约束优化。这生成了具有内在可解释性的嵌入，其几何结构反映原型间的相对权衡，并支持在成分受限下的连贯行为；我们考虑固定和可学习的ILR基。在节点分类和链接预测任务中，我们的方法在强基线方法中表现具有竞争力，且通过构造而非事后提供可解释性。最后，子组合一致性使成分受限成为有原则的方法：移除并重新归一化子集可保持明确定义的几何结构，我们通过子组合降维加以利用，以探究原型组如何影响表示和预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable graph representations that reflect latent role mixtures, this paper introduces Aitchison embeddings—a compositional framework grounded in Aitchison geometry for comparing mixtures. Nodes are represented as simplex-valued compositions and embedded via isometric log-ratio (ILR) coordinates, preserving Aitchison distances while enabling unconstrained optimization in Euclidean space. Experiments on node classification and link prediction show competitive performance against strong baselines, while the method inherently supports explainability through its geometric structure. Subcompositional coherence further allows principled dimensionality reduction by removing and renormalizing subsets, revealing how archetype groups influence representations and predictions.
为了解决图表示学习中缺乏可解释性的难题，本文提出基于Aitchison几何的组合图嵌入框架，该框架将节点视为混合角色的组合，并通过等距对数比坐标嵌入到欧氏空间中，保持Aitchison距离的同时支持无约束优化。在节点分类与链接预测任务中，该方法表现与主流基线相当，且天然具备可解释性，其几何结构反映角色间的相对权衡。此外，该方法支持子组合一致性，通过移除并重归一化子集实现有原则的降维，从而揭示不同角色组如何影响表示与预测。
Vibe Coding in Product Teams: Reconfiguring AI-Assisted Workflows, Prototyping, and Collaboration
Authors: Jie Li, Youyang Hou, Laura Lin, Ruihao Zhu, Hancheng Cao, Abdallah El Ali
First: 2025-09-12T19:28:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:02:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI is reshaping product design practices through "vibe coding," where product team members express intent in natural language and AI translates it into functional prototypes and code. Despite rapid adoption, little research has examined how vibe coding reconfigures product development workflows and collaboration. Drawing on interviews with 22 product team members across enterprises, startups, and academia, we show how vibe coding follows a four-stage workflow of ideation, generation, debugging, and review. This accelerates iteration, supports creativity, and lowers participation barriers. However, participants reported challenges of code unreliability, integration, and AI over-reliance. We find tensions between efficiency-driven prototyping ("intending the right design") and reflection ("designing the right intention"), introducing new asymmetries in trust, responsibility, and social stigma within teams. Through a responsible human-AI collaboration lens for AI-assisted product design and development, we contribute a deeper understanding of deskilling, ownership and disclosure, and creativity safeguarding in the age of vibe coding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：产品团队中的氛围编码：重构AI辅助工作流程、原型设计与协作
生成式AI正通过“氛围编码”重塑产品设计实践，即产品团队成员用自然语言表达意图，AI将其转化为功能原型和代码。尽管采用迅速，但尚缺乏研究探讨氛围编码如何重构产品开发工作流程与协作。通过对来自企业、初创公司和学术界的22名产品团队成员的访谈，我们发现氛围编码遵循构思、生成、调试和评审四个阶段的工作流程。这加速了迭代，支持了创造力，并降低了参与门槛。然而，受访者报告了代码不可靠、集成困难和过度依赖AI等挑战。我们发现效率导向的原型设计（“意图正确的设计”）与反思（“设计正确的意图”）之间存在张力，从而在团队内部引入了新的信任、责任与社会污名的不对称性。通过负责任的人机协作视角，我们为AI辅助产品设计与开发时代中的技能退化、所有权与披露、创造力保护提供了更深入的理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rapid adoption of generative AI in product design, this study investigates how "vibe coding"—where team members use natural language to generate prototypes and code—reconfigures workflows and collaboration. Through interviews with 22 participants across diverse organizations, the research identifies a four-stage workflow: ideation, generation, debugging, and review, which accelerates iteration and lowers entry barriers. However, participants reported issues including unreliable code, integration difficulties, and over-reliance on AI, revealing tensions between efficiency-driven prototyping and reflective design. These tensions introduce new asymmetries in team trust, responsibility, and social stigma, highlighting challenges in human-AI collaboration regarding deskilling, ownership, and creativity preservation.
鉴于生成式AI在产品设计中的快速普及，本研究探讨了“氛围编码”（vibe coding）——即团队成员用自然语言表达意图并由AI生成原型和代码——如何重构工作流程与协作模式。通过对来自企业、初创公司和学术机构的22名参与者进行访谈，研究发现其遵循四个阶段：构思、生成、调试与评审，从而加速迭代并降低参与门槛。然而，参与者普遍反映代码不可靠、集成困难及对AI过度依赖等问题，揭示了效率导向的原型设计与反思性设计之间的张力。这些张力在团队中引发了信任、责任与社会污名的新不对称性，促使对技能流失、所有权归属及创造力保护在人机协作时代进行重新思考。
Theory Under Construction: Orchestrating Language Models for Research Software Where the Specification Evolves
Authors: Halley Young, Nikolaj Björner
First: 2026-04-29T21:28:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T15:02:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models can now generate substantial code and draft research text, but research-software projects require more than either artifact alone. The mathematical thesis, executable system, benchmark surface, and public claims must mature together, yet often drift apart. We identify two LM-specific failure modes: hallucination accumulation, in which claims exceed what code or theory supports and unsupported assertions propagate across sessions; and desynchronization, in which code, theory, or the model's own world model fall out of alignment. We propose Comet-H, an iterative prompt automaton that orchestrates ideation, implementation, evaluation, grounding, and paper-writing as coupled coordinates of a single workspace state. At each step, a controller selects the next prompt by scoring it against what the workspace currently lacks, carries unfinished follow-up work forward with a half-life, and re-checks the paper and README against the code and benchmarks whenever documentation changes. We frame prompt selection as a small contextual bandit problem over prompt families, with prompts as arms, workspace deficits as context, and a hand-weighted linear score. This transparent scorer, paired with a fading record of unfinished work, bounds long-horizon follow-ups, requires no learned policy, and makes each prompt choice legible from the workspace. We created a portfolio of 46 research-software repositories across two dozen domains. We study A3 in depth, a Python static-analysis tool built entirely within the loop, which reaches (F1 = 0.768) on a 90-case benchmark, compared with a next-best baseline of 0.364. Across approximately 400 commits, we find that audit-and-contraction passes dominate the later phases of every successful trajectory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正在构建的理论：为需求不断演化的研究软件协调语言模型
大型语言模型如今能够生成大量代码和草拟研究文本，但研究软件项目需要的远不止这两个产物。数学论文、可执行系统、基准测试面和公开声明必须同步成熟，却常彼此脱节。我们识别出两种与语言模型相关的失效模式：幻觉累积，即声明超出代码或理论支持范围，且无依据的断言在会话间传播；以及不同步，即代码、理论或模型自身的世界模型失去一致性。 我们提出Comet-H，一种迭代提示自动机，将创意、实现、评估、锚定和论文撰写作为单一工作区状态的耦合坐标进行协调。在每一步，控制器根据工作区当前缺失内容对提示进行评分，以半衰期携带未完成的后续工作，并在文档变更时重新核对论文和README与代码及基准测试。我们将提示选择框架为提示家族上的小规模上下文多臂老虎机问题，提示为臂，工作区缺口为上下文，评分采用人工加权线性得分。该透明评分器配合未完成工作的衰减记录，限制长周期后续任务，无需学习策略，且每个提示选择均可从工作区状态中清晰推导。 我们创建了涵盖20多个领域的46个研究软件仓库组合。我们深入研究A3，一个完全在循环内构建的Python静态分析工具，在90个案例基准测试中达到(F1 = 0.768)，优于次优基线0.364。在约400次提交中，我们发现每个成功轨迹的后期阶段，审计与收缩阶段的提交占主导地位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that research software projects require synchronized evolution of mathematical theory, executable code, benchmarks, and public claims—often drifting apart when generated by large language models—the authors propose Comet-H, an iterative prompt automaton that coordinates ideation, implementation, evaluation, grounding, and paper-writing as interdependent components of a single workspace state. The system selects next prompts based on a hand-weighted linear score evaluating workspace deficits, carries unfinished tasks forward with a fading half-life, and rechecks documentation against code and benchmarks upon updates. Evaluated across 46 repositories in 20+ domains, Comet-H produced A3, a Python static-analysis tool achieving F1=0.768 on a 90-case benchmark—outperforming the next-best baseline (0.364)—and analysis of 400 commits revealed that audit-and-contraction phases dominate later successful trajectories.
为解决研究软件项目中数学理论、可执行代码、基准测试与公开声明需同步演进却常因大语言模型生成而脱节的问题，作者提出Comet-H，一种迭代提示自动机，将构思、实现、评估、锚定与论文写作作为单一工作空间状态的耦合组件协同管理。系统通过上下文多臂老虎机框架选择提示，依据当前工作空间缺失项评分，以半衰期机制延续未完成任务，并在文档更新时重新核对代码与基准。在46个跨20多个领域的研究软件仓库中，Comet-H生成了A3（Python静态分析工具），在90个案例基准上达到F1=0.768，显著优于次优基线0.364；对400次提交的分析表明，成功轨迹后期以审计与收缩阶段为主导。
Bring Your Own Prompts: Use-Case-Specific Bias and Fairness Evaluation for LLMs
Authors: Dylan Bouchard
First: 2024-07-15T16:04:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:59:15+00:00
Comments: v5: Updated title; LangFair repository: https://github.com/cvs-health/langfair
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Bias and fairness risks in Large Language Models (LLMs) vary substantially across deployment contexts, yet existing approaches lack systematic guidance for selecting appropriate evaluation metrics. We present a decision framework that maps LLM use cases, characterized by a model and population of prompts, to relevant bias and fairness metrics based on task type, whether prompts contain protected attribute mentions, and stakeholder priorities. Our framework addresses toxicity, stereotyping, counterfactual unfairness, and allocational harms, and introduces novel metrics based on stereotype classifiers and counterfactual adaptations of text similarity measures. We release an open-source Python library, \texttt{langfair}, for practical adoption. Extensive experiments on use cases across five LLMs and five prompt populations demonstrate that fairness risks cannot be reliably assessed from benchmark performance alone: results on one prompt dataset likely overstate or understate risks for another, underscoring that fairness evaluation must be grounded in the specific deployment context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自带提示：面向大语言模型的用例特定偏见与公平性评估
大语言模型（LLMs）在不同部署场景中的偏见与公平性风险差异显著，但现有方法缺乏系统性指导以选择合适的评估指标。我们提出一个决策框架，根据任务类型、提示是否包含受保护属性提及及利益相关者优先级，将LLM用例（由模型和提示群体表征）映射到相关偏见与公平性指标。该框架涵盖毒性、刻板印象、反事实不公平及分配性伤害，并引入基于刻板印象分类器和文本相似度度量反事实改编的新型指标。我们开源了Python库\texttt{langfair}以促进实际应用。在五种LLM和五种提示群体的广泛实验表明，仅凭基准性能无法可靠评估公平性风险：一个提示数据集上的结果可能高估或低估另一数据集的风险，强调公平性评估必须基于具体部署场景。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of systematic guidance for evaluating bias and fairness in LLMs across diverse deployment contexts. It introduces a decision framework that matches use cases—defined by model and prompt population—to appropriate metrics based on task type, presence of protected attributes, and stakeholder priorities. The framework evaluates toxicity, stereotyping, counterfactual unfairness, and allocational harms, incorporating novel metrics using stereotype classifiers and counterfactual similarity measures. Experiments across five LLMs and five prompt populations show that benchmark performance alone fails to reliably indicate fairness risks, as results vary significantly between prompt datasets, emphasizing the need for context-specific evaluation.
该论文针对现有方法缺乏系统指导以评估不同部署场景下大语言模型的偏见与公平性问题，提出了一种决策框架，根据任务类型、提示中是否包含受保护属性及利益相关者优先级，将具体应用场景（由模型和提示集定义）映射到合适的评估指标。框架涵盖毒性、刻板印象、反事实不公平与分配性伤害，并引入基于刻板印象分类器和反事实文本相似度的新指标。在五种大语言模型和五种提示集上的实验表明，仅凭基准性能无法可靠评估公平性风险，不同提示集的结果差异显著，凸显公平性评估必须结合具体部署场景。
Concolic Testing on Individual Fairness of Neural Network Models
Authors: Ming-I Huang, Chih-Duo Hong, Fang Yu
First: 2025-09-08T16:31:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:58:16+00:00
Comments: Add a theorem and improve wording and layout
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces PyFair, a formal framework for evaluating and verifying individual fairness of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs). By adapting the concolic testing tool PyCT, we generate fairness-specific path constraints to systematically explore DNN behaviors. Our key innovation is a dual network architecture that enables comprehensive fairness assessments and provides completeness guarantees for certain network types. We evaluate PyFair on 25 benchmark models, including those enhanced by existing bias mitigation techniques. Results demonstrate PyFair's efficacy in detecting discriminatory instances and verifying fairness, while also revealing scalability challenges for complex models. This work advances algorithmic fairness in critical domains by offering a rigorous, systematic method for fairness testing and verification of pre-trained DNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络模型个体公平性的符号执行测试
本文介绍了PyFair，一个用于评估和验证深度神经网络（DNN）个体公平性的形式化框架。通过改造符号执行工具PyCT，我们生成针对公平性的路径约束，以系统性地探索DNN行为。我们的关键创新是一种双网络架构，能够全面评估公平性，并为某些网络类型提供完备性保证。我们在25个基准模型上评估PyFair，包括已应用现有偏见缓解技术的模型。结果表明，PyFair在检测歧视性实例和验证公平性方面有效，同时也揭示了复杂模型的可扩展性挑战。本工作通过提供一种严谨、系统的方法，推动了关键领域算法公平性的进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for rigorous fairness verification in deep neural networks, this paper presents PyFair, a concolic testing framework adapted from PyCT to systematically evaluate individual fairness. It employs a dual network architecture to generate fairness-specific path constraints, enabling comprehensive exploration of discriminatory behaviors while offering completeness guarantees for certain model types. Experimental results on 25 benchmark models—including those with bias mitigation techniques—show PyFair successfully detects unfair instances and verifies fairness properties, though scalability remains limited for complex architectures.
为应对神经网络个体公平性验证的迫切需求，本文提出PyFair框架，基于PyCT工具进行适配，通过生成公平性特定路径约束系统性评估深度神经网络。其核心创新是采用双网络架构，实现对神经网络行为的全面探索，并对部分模型提供完备性保证。在25个基准模型（含已应用偏见缓解技术的模型）上的实验表明，PyFair能有效发现歧视性实例并验证公平性，同时揭示了复杂模型下的可扩展性瓶颈。
Deep Kernel Learning for Stratifying Glaucoma Trajectories
Authors: Bruce Rushing, Angela Danquah, Alireza Namazi, Arjun Dirghangi, Heman Shakeri
First: 2026-05-01T14:51:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:51:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effectively stratifying patient risk in chronic diseases like glaucoma is a major clinical challenge. Clinicians need tools to identify patients at high risk of progression from sparse and irregularly-sampled electronic health records (EHRs). We propose a novel deep kernel learning (DKL) architecture that leverages a Gaussian Process (GP) backend. The GP's kernel is defined by a transformer-based feature extractor applied to clinical-BERT embeddings to model glaucoma patient trajectories from multimodal EHR data. Our method successfully identifies three clinically distinct patient subgroups. Crucially, the model learns to decouple disease progression from current severity, identifying a high-risk group with a worsening trajectory despite having better average visual acuity than a second, stably poor group. This reveals that the model learns to identify progression risk rather than just the current disease state. This ability to stratify patients based on their risk trajectory progression offers a powerful tool for clinical decision support, enabling targeted interventions for high-risk individuals and improving the management of glaucoma care.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于青光眼轨迹分层的深度核学习
在慢性疾病如青光眼中有效分层患者风险是临床重大挑战。临床医生需要工具，从稀疏且不规则采样的电子健康记录（EHR）中识别高风险进展患者。我们提出一种新颖的深度核学习（DKL）架构，利用高斯过程（GP）后端。GP的核由基于Transformer的特征提取器定义，该提取器应用于临床-BERT嵌入，以从多模态EHR数据建模青光眼患者轨迹。我们的方法成功识别出三个临床不同的患者亚组。关键的是，模型学会将疾病进展与当前严重程度解耦，识别出尽管平均视力优于另一组稳定较差患者，但病情恶化的高风险群体。这表明模型学习的是进展风险而非当前疾病状态。该基于风险轨迹分层的能力为临床决策支持提供了强大工具，有助于对高风险个体实施针对性干预，改善青光眼管理。
Summary / 总结
To address the clinical challenge of stratifying glaucoma progression risk from sparse electronic health records, the authors propose a deep kernel learning architecture that combines a Gaussian Process backend with a transformer-based feature extractor applied to clinical-BERT embeddings. This method models patient trajectories from multimodal EHR data and successfully identifies three clinically distinct subgroups. Notably, it distinguishes a high-risk group with worsening progression despite better average visual acuity compared to a stably poor group, demonstrating the model’s ability to predict future risk rather than merely reflecting current severity, thereby offering a novel tool for targeted clinical interventions.
为应对从稀疏电子健康记录中分层评估青光眼进展风险的临床挑战，作者提出一种结合高斯过程后端与基于Transformer的特征提取器（应用于临床BERT嵌入）的深度核学习架构。该方法从多模态EHR数据中建模患者轨迹，成功识别出三个临床差异显著的亚组。关键发现是，模型能区分出尽管视力较好但病情恶化的高风险患者与视力较差但病情稳定的患者，表明其预测的是未来风险而非当前状态，从而为精准临床干预提供了新工具。
PhysEdit: Physically-Consistent Region-Aware Image Editing via Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Reasoning
Authors: Guandong Li, Mengxia Ye
First: 2026-05-01T14:51:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:51:52+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image editing instructions are heterogeneous: a color swap, an object insertion, and a physical-action edit all demand different spatial coverage and different reasoning depth, yet existing reasoning-based editors apply a single fixed inference recipe to every instruction. We argue that adaptivity along both the spatial and temporal axes is the missing degree of freedom, and we present PhysEdit, an editing framework built around this principle. PhysEdit introduces two inference-time modules that compose without retraining the backbone. At its core, (1) Complexity-Adaptive Reasoning Depth (CARD) predicts edit complexity directly from the instruction and reference image and allocates the reasoning step count N_r and reasoning-token length r per sample -- turning a previously fixed inference schedule into a conditional-computation problem. CARD is supported by (2) a Spatial Reasoning Mask (SRM) that extracts an instruction-conditioned spatial prior from cross-attention to confine reasoning to regions that semantically require it. On the full 737-case ImgEdit Basic-Edit Suite, PhysEdit delivers a 1.18x wall-clock speedup (64.3s vs. 76.1s per sample) over a strong reasoning baseline while slightly improving instruction adherence (CLIP-T 0.2283 vs. 0.2266, +0.7%) and matching identity preservation within noise (CLIP-I 0.8246 vs. 0.8280). The speedup is category-dependent and reaches 1.52x on appearance-level edits, validating CARD's adaptive allocation as the principal source of efficiency gain. A 30-sample pilot with full ablations isolates the contribution of each module.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhysEdit：通过自适应时空推理实现物理一致的区域感知图像编辑
图像编辑指令具有异质性：颜色交换、物体插入和物理动作编辑均需不同的空间覆盖范围和推理深度，但现有基于推理的编辑器对所有指令采用单一固定推理方案。我们认为，空间与时间维度上的自适应性是缺失的关键自由度，因此提出PhysEdit框架，围绕此原则构建。PhysEdit在不重新训练骨干网络的前提下，引入两个推理时模块：(1) 复杂度自适应推理深度（CARD）直接从指令与参考图像预测编辑复杂度，并为每个样本分配推理步数N_r和推理标记长度r，将原先固定的推理调度转化为条件计算问题；(2) 空间推理掩码（SRM）通过交叉注意力提取指令条件下的空间先验，将推理限制在语义上必需的区域。在包含737个案例的ImgEdit基础编辑套件上，PhysEdit相较强推理基线实现1.18倍壁钟加速（每样本64.3秒 vs. 76.1秒），略微提升指令遵循度（CLIP-T 0.2283 vs. 0.2266，+0.7%），且在噪声下匹配身份保持（CLIP-I 0.8246 vs. 0.8280）。加速效果因类别而异，在外观级编辑中可达1.52倍，验证CARD的自适应分配是效率提升的主要来源。30样本的完整消融实验独立评估各模块贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptable reasoning in image editing that varies by instruction type, PhysEdit introduces two inference-time modules to dynamically adjust spatial and temporal reasoning: Complexity-Adaptive Reasoning Depth (CARD) allocates reasoning steps and token length based on instruction complexity, while Spatial Reasoning Mask (SRM) confines attention to semantically relevant regions. Evaluated on the 737-case ImgEdit suite, PhysEdit achieves a 1.18x speedup over a strong baseline (64.3s vs. 76.1s per sample), with slightly improved instruction adherence (CLIP-T 0.2283 vs. 0.2266) and comparable identity preservation (CLIP-I 0.8246 vs. 0.8280). The speedup is most pronounced on appearance-level edits (1.52x), confirming CARD’s role in efficiency gains.
为应对不同图像编辑任务对空间与时间推理需求的差异，PhysEdit提出一种动态调整推理深度与空间覆盖范围的框架。其核心包含两个推理时模块：复杂度自适应推理深度（CARD），根据指令与图像预测推理步数和令牌长度；以及空间推理掩码（SRM），通过跨注意力提取语义相关区域以聚焦计算。在737个案例的ImgEdit基础编辑数据集上，PhysEdit相较强基线实现1.18倍加速（64.3秒 vs 76.1秒/样本），指令遵循度略有提升（CLIP-T 0.2283 vs 0.2266），身份保持度相当（CLIP-I 0.8246 vs 0.8280）。加速效果因编辑类型而异，外观编辑可达1.52倍，验证CARD为主要效率来源。
FinSafetyBench: Evaluating LLM Safety in Real-World Financial Scenarios
Authors: Yutao Hou, Yihan Jiang, Yuhan Xie, Jian Yang, Liwen Zhang, Hailiang Huang, Guanhua Chen, Yun Chen
First: 2026-05-01T14:51:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:51:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Findings of ACL2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in financial scenarios. However, they may produce harmful outputs, including facilitating illegal activities or unethical behavior, posing serious compliance risks. To systematically evaluate LLM safety in finance, we propose FinSafetyBench, a bilingual (English-Chinese) red-teaming benchmark designed to test an LLM's refusal of requests that violate financial compliance. Grounded in real-world financial crime cases and ethics standards, the benchmark comprises 14 subcategories spanning financial crimes and ethical violations. Through extensive experiments on general-purpose and finance-specialized LLMs under three representative attack settings, we identify critical vulnerabilities that allow adversarial prompts to bypass compliance safeguards. Further analysis reveals stronger susceptibility in Chinese contexts and highlights the limitations of prompt-level defenses against sophisticated or implicit manipulation strategies.
中文标题/摘要
