为应对大语言模型在金融场景中可能生成违规有害内容的风险，研究者构建了FinSafetyBench评估基准，该基准基于真实金融犯罪案例与伦理标准，涵盖14类金融违规子类目。通过在三种攻击场景下对通用及金融专用模型的测试，发现模型存在可被对抗提示绕过的合规漏洞，且中文环境下模型更易受攻击，提示级防御机制对复杂或隐式操控策略效果有限。
A unified convergence theory for adaptive first-order methods in the nonconvex case, including AdaNorm, full and diagonal AdaGrad, Shampoo and Muo
Authors: S. Gratton, Ph. L. Toint
First: 2026-04-19T13:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:46:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A unified framework for first-order optimization algorithms fornonconvex unconstrained optimization is proposed that uses adaptivelypreconditioned gradients and includes popular methods such as full anddiagonal AdaGrad, AdaNorm, as well as adpative variants of Shampoo andMuon. This framework also allows combining heterogeneous geometriesacross different groups of variables while preserving a unifiedconvergence analysis. A fully stochastic global rate-of-convergenceanalysis is conducted for all methods in the framework, with andwithout two types of momentum, using reasonable assumptions on thevariance of the gradient oracle and without assuming boundedstochastic gradients or small enough stepsize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非凸情况下自适应一阶方法的统一收敛理论，包括AdaNorm、全量与对角AdaGrad、Shampoo和Muo
提出了一种适用于非凸无约束优化问题的一阶优化算法统一框架，该框架使用自适应预条件梯度，涵盖全量与对角AdaGrad、AdaNorm以及Shampoo和Muo的自适应变体。该框架还允许在不同变量组间结合异构几何结构，同时保持统一的收敛性分析。对框架内所有方法进行了完全随机的全局收敛速率分析，包含两种动量形式，仅需对梯度查询方差做出合理假设，无需假设随机梯度有界或步长足够小。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified theoretical understanding of adaptive first-order methods in nonconvex optimization, this work proposes a framework that incorporates adaptive preconditioning and encompasses popular algorithms like AdaGrad, AdaNorm, and adaptive variants of Shampoo and Muon. The method allows for heterogeneous variable groupings while maintaining a consistent convergence analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that all methods within the framework achieve provable global convergence rates under mild assumptions on gradient variance, without requiring bounded stochastic gradients or restrictive stepsize conditions.
为统一理解非凸优化中自适应一阶方法的收敛性，本文提出一个基于自适应预条件梯度的框架，涵盖AdaGrad、AdaNorm及Shampoo和Muon的自适应变体。该方法支持不同变量组的异构几何结构，同时保持统一的收敛分析。实验表明，在合理梯度方差假设下，所有方法均能收敛，无需假设梯度有界或步长足够小，且在有无动量情况下表现稳定。
Learning How and What to Memorize: Cognition-Inspired Two-Stage Optimization for Evolving Memory
Authors: Derong Xu, Shuochen Liu, Pengfei Luo, Pengyue Jia, Yingyi Zhang, Yi Wen, Yimin Deng, Wenlin Zhang, Enhong Chen, Xiangyu Zhao, Tong Xu
First: 2026-05-01T14:45:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:45:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents require long-term user memory for consistent personalization, but limited context windows hinder tracking evolving preferences over long interactions. Existing memory systems mainly rely on static, hand-crafted update rules; although reinforcement learning (RL)-based agents learn memory updates, sparse outcome rewards provide weak supervision, resulting in unstable long-horizon optimization. Drawing on memory schema theory and the functional division between prefrontal regions and hippocampus regions, we introduce MemCoE, a cognition-inspired two-stage optimization framework that learns how memory should be organized and what information to update. In the first stage, we propose Memory Guideline Induction to optimize a global guideline via contrastive feedback interpreted as textual gradients; in the second stage, Guideline-Aligned Memory Policy Optimization uses the induced guideline to define structured process rewards and performs multi-turn RL to learn a guideline-following memory evolution policy. We evaluate on three personalization memory benchmarks, covering explicit/implicit preference and different sizes and noise, and observe consistent improvements over strong baselines with favorable robustness, transferability, and efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习如何以及记忆什么：受认知启发的两阶段优化框架用于演化记忆
大语言模型（LLM）代理需要长期用户记忆以实现持续个性化，但受限的上下文窗口阻碍了对长期交互中演变偏好的追踪。现有记忆系统主要依赖静态、人工设计的更新规则；尽管基于强化学习（RL）的代理能学习记忆更新，但稀疏的奖励提供弱监督，导致长时优化不稳定。受记忆图式理论及前额叶与海马体功能分工的启发，我们提出MemCoE，一种受认知启发的两阶段优化框架，学习记忆应如何组织以及更新哪些信息。第一阶段，我们提出记忆指南诱导，通过对比反馈（解释为文本梯度）优化全局指南；第二阶段，指南对齐记忆策略优化，利用诱导的指南定义结构化过程奖励，并通过多轮RL学习遵循指南的记忆演化策略。我们在三个个性化记忆基准上进行评估，涵盖显式/隐式偏好及不同规模与噪声，观察到相比强基线方法有持续改进，且具备良好的鲁棒性、迁移性和效率。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining evolving user preferences in long-term interactions with large language models, this paper proposes MemCoE, a cognition-inspired two-stage optimization framework that learns both how to organize memory and what content to update. The first stage induces a global memory guideline via contrastive feedback interpreted as textual gradients, while the second stage uses this guideline to define structured process rewards and trains a memory evolution policy through multi-turn reinforcement learning. Evaluated on three personalization benchmarks with varying noise and scale, MemCoE consistently outperforms strong baselines with improved robustness, transferability, and efficiency.
为解决大语言模型在长期交互中维护用户动态偏好时面临的记忆窗口限制问题，本文提出MemCoE框架，该框架受认知记忆理论启发，分两阶段学习记忆组织方式与更新内容。第一阶段通过对比反馈诱导全局记忆指南，第二阶段基于该指南构建结构化过程奖励并训练强化学习策略以遵循指南。在三个不同噪声与规模的个性化记忆基准上，MemCoE持续优于强基线模型，展现出更优的鲁棒性、迁移能力和效率。
Differentiable Autoencoding Neural Operator for Interpretable and Integrable Latent Space Modeling
Authors: Siva Viknesh, Amirhossein Arzani
First: 2025-09-30T19:57:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:44:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific machine learning has enabled the extraction of physical insights and data-driven modeling of high-dimensional spatiotemporal data, yet achieving physically interpretable latent representations and computationally efficient surrogates remains an open challenge. We propose the DIfferentiable Autoencoding Neural Operator - DIANO, an autoencoding neural operator framework that constructs visualizable coarse-grid latent spaces for both dimensionality and geometric reduction across varying spatial discretizations, with governing equations enforced directly within the latent space. Built upon neural operators, DIANO achieves this through an encoding neural operator that spatially coarsens the high-dimensional input functions into the latent representation, and a decoding neural operator that reconstructs the original inputs via spatial refinement. We assess DIANO's latent representation and performance against baselines, including the Convolutional Neural Operator and standard autoencoders. Furthermore, a fully differentiable partial differential equation (PDE) solver is integrated as the sole input-output functional mapping operator within the latent space, enabling end-to-end training with governing physics prescribed a priori through parametric PDEs. Various PDE formulations are investigated, including the 2D unsteady advection-diffusion and the 3D Pressure--Poisson equation, revealing that the fidelity of the embedded PDE relative to the true physics governs the learned latent representation and reconstruction accuracy. Benchmark problems include flow past a 2D cylinder, flow through a 2D symmetric stenosed artery, and a 3D patient-specific coronary artery, showing accurate reconstruction of high-fidelity spatio-temporal fields through low-fidelity latent PDE evolution at reduced computational cost, while yielding coherent, spatially organized, and meaningful latent structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可微自编码神经算子用于可解释且可集成的潜在空间建模
科学机器学习已实现物理洞见提取和高维时空数据的数据驱动建模，但实现物理可解释的潜在表示和计算高效的代理模型仍具挑战。我们提出DIfferentiable Autoencoding Neural Operator（DIANO），一种自编码神经算子框架，可在不同空间离散化下构建可视化粗网格潜在空间，实现维度与几何降维，且直接在潜在空间内强制执行控制方程。基于神经算子，DIANO通过编码神经算子将高维输入函数空间降维至潜在表示，再通过解码神经算子经空间细化重构原始输入。我们评估DIANO的潜在表示与性能，对比卷积神经算子和标准自编码器基线。此外，集成完全可微的偏微分方程（PDE）求解器作为潜在空间内唯一输入-输出函数映射算子，支持端到端训练，通过参数化PDE预先指定物理规律。研究多种PDE形式，包括二维非稳态对流-扩散方程和三维压力-泊松方程，发现嵌入PDE与真实物理的保真度决定学习到的潜在表示与重构精度。基准问题包括二维圆柱绕流、二维对称狭窄动脉流及三维患者特异性冠状动脉流，结果表明：通过低保真潜在PDE演化，以较低计算成本准确重构高保真时空场，同时生成连贯、空间有序且有意义的潜在结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically interpretable and computationally efficient latent representations in scientific machine learning, the paper introduces DIANO, a differentiable autoencoding neural operator that constructs coarse-grid latent spaces enabling dimensionality and geometric reduction while preserving physical laws. DIANO employs encoding and decoding neural operators to coarsen and refine input fields, respectively, and integrates a differentiable PDE solver within the latent space for end-to-end training with prior physics. Experiments on 2D and 3D fluid dynamics problems demonstrate that DIANO achieves accurate reconstruction of high-fidelity spatio-temporal fields using low-fidelity latent PDE evolution, while producing spatially coherent and meaningful latent structures that align with the fidelity of embedded physics.
为解决科学机器学习中对物理可解释且计算高效的潜在表示的需求，作者提出了DIANO，一种可微自编码神经算子框架，可在不同空间离散化下构建可视化粗网格潜在空间，实现维度与几何压缩，同时在潜在空间内直接嵌入物理方程。DIANO通过编码与解码神经算子分别对输入场进行空间粗化与细化，并在潜在空间内集成可微偏微分方程求解器，实现端到端训练，物理规律通过参数化PDE预先设定。实验表明，DIANO在多个流体动力学基准问题上能以低维潜在PDE演化准确重建高保真时空场，其性能依赖于嵌入PDE与真实物理的吻合度，并生成空间有序、有意义的潜在结构。
Probabilistic Predictions of Process-Induced Deformation in Carbon/Epoxy Composites Using a Deep Operator Network
Authors: Elham Kiyani, Amit Makarand Deshpande, Madhura Limaye, Zhiwei Gao, Zongren Zou, Sai Aditya Pradeep, Srikanth Pilla, Gang Li, Zhen Li, George Em Karniadakis
First: 2025-12-15T03:04:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:40:04+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fiber reinforcement and polymer matrix respond differently to manufacturing conditions due to mismatch in coefficient of thermal expansion and matrix shrinkage during curing of thermosets. These heterogeneities generate residual stresses over multiple length scales, whose partial release leads to process-induced deformation (PID), requiring accurate prediction and mitigation via optimized non-isothermal cure cycles. This study considers a unidirectional AS4 carbon fiber/amine bi-functional epoxy prepreg and models PID using a two-mechanism framework that accounts for thermal expansion/shrinkage and cure shrinkage. The model is validated against manufacturing trials to identify initial and boundary conditions, then used to generate PID responses for a diverse set of non-isothermal cure cycles (time-temperature profiles). Building on this physics-based foundation, we develop a data-driven surrogate based on Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets). A DeepONet is trained on a dataset combining high-fidelity simulations with targeted experimental measurements of PID. We extend this to a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) DeepONet, where branch-network features are modulated by external parameters, including the initial degree of cure, enabling prediction of time histories of degree of cure, viscosity, and deformation. Because experimental data are available only at limited time instances (for example, final deformation), we use transfer learning: simulation-trained trunk and branch networks are fixed and only the final layer is updated using measured final deformation. Finally, we augment the framework with Ensemble Kalman Inversion (EKI) to quantify uncertainty under experimental conditions and to support optimization of cure schedules for reduced PID in composites.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用深度算子网络对碳/环氧复合材料加工引起的变形进行概率预测
由于纤维增强体与聚合物基体在热膨胀系数和热固性树脂固化收缩方面存在不匹配，二者对制造条件的响应不同，从而在多个长度尺度上产生残余应力。这些残余应力的部分释放导致加工引起的变形（PID），需要通过优化非等温固化循环进行精确预测和抑制。本研究针对单向AS4碳纤维/胺双官能环氧预浸料，采用包含热膨胀/收缩与固化收缩的双机制模型模拟PID。该模型通过制造试验验证并确定初始和边界条件，随后用于生成多种非等温固化循环（时间-温度曲线）下的PID响应。在此物理基础之上，我们构建了基于深度算子网络（DeepONets）的数据驱动代理模型。该DeepONet通过结合高保真模拟与针对性实验测量的PID数据进行训练。进一步，我们将其扩展为特征线性调制（FiLM）DeepONet，通过外部参数（如初始固化度）调节分支网络特征，从而预测固化度、粘度和变形的时间历程。由于实验数据仅在有限时间点（如最终变形）可得，我们采用迁移学习：固定由模拟训练得到的主干和分支网络，仅更新最后一层以匹配测量的最终变形。最后，我们引入集合卡尔曼反演（EKI）方法，以量化实验条件下的不确定性，并支持优化固化方案以降低复合材料中的PID。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict and mitigate process-induced deformation in carbon/epoxy composites caused by thermal and cure-induced stresses, this study combines a physics-based two-mechanism model with a data-driven Deep Operator Network. The model captures thermal expansion/shrinkage and cure shrinkage effects, validated against experimental trials, and is used to generate deformation responses across diverse non-isothermal cure cycles. A DeepONet surrogate is trained on high-fidelity simulations and sparse experimental data, enhanced with Feature-wise Linear Modulation to incorporate parameters like initial degree of cure. Transfer learning is applied to update only the final layer using limited experimental measurements, while trunk and branch networks remain fixed. The framework is further augmented with Ensemble Kalman Inversion to quantify uncertainty and optimize cure schedules for reduced deformation.
为解决碳/环氧复合材料制造中因热膨胀系数不匹配和固化收缩导致的工艺诱导变形问题，该研究结合物理模型与数据驱动的深度算子网络（DeepONet）构建预测框架。模型涵盖热膨胀/收缩与固化收缩机制，经制造试验验证后，扩展为特征线性调制型DeepONet，可预测固化度、黏度和变形的时间历程。因实验数据稀疏（如仅测得最终变形），采用迁移学习策略，冻结仿真训练的主干与分支网络，仅更新最后一层以适配实验数据。框架进一步引入集合卡尔曼反演（EKI）量化不确定性并优化固化周期，有效降低工艺诱导变形，提升预测精度。
Intern-Atlas: A Methodological Evolution Graph as Research Infrastructure for AI Scientists
Authors: Yujun Wu, Dongxu Zhang, Xinchen Li, Jinhang Xu, Yiling Duan, Yumou Liu, Jiabao Pan, Qiyuan Zhu, Xuanhe Zhou, Jingxuan Wei, Siyuan Li, Jintao Chen, Conghui He, Cheng Tan
First: 2026-04-30T17:44:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:37:29+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing research infrastructure is fundamentally document-centric, providing citation links between papers but lacking explicit representations of methodological evolution. In particular, it does not capture the structured relationships that explain how and why research methods emerge, adapt, and build upon one another. With the rise of AI-driven research agents as a new class of consumers of scientific knowledge, this limitation becomes increasingly consequential, as such agents cannot reliably reconstruct method evolution topologies from unstructured text. We introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph that automatically identifies method-level entities, infers lineage relationships among methodologies, and captures the bottlenecks that drive transitions between successive innovations. Built from 1,030,314 papers spanning AI conferences, journals, and arXiv preprints, the resulting graph comprises 9,410,201 semantically typed edges, each grounded in verbatim source evidence, forming a queryable causal network of methodological development. To operationalize this structure, we further propose a self-guided temporal tree search algorithm for constructing evolution chains that trace the progression of methods over time. We evaluate the quality of the resulting graph against expert-curated ground-truth evolution chains and observe strong alignment. In addition, we demonstrate that Intern-Atlas enables downstream applications in idea evaluation and automated idea generation. We position methodological evolution graphs as a foundational data layer for the emerging automated scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Intern-Atlas：面向AI科学家的研究基础设施——方法论演化图谱
现有研究基础设施本质上以文档为中心，仅提供论文间的引用链接，缺乏对方法论演化的显式表达。尤其未能捕捉解释研究方法如何及为何产生、适应并相互构建的结构化关系。随着AI驱动的研究代理作为科学知识的新类消费者崛起，这一局限愈发重要，因为此类代理无法从非结构化文本中可靠重建方法演化拓扑。我们提出Intern-Atlas，一种方法论演化图谱，可自动识别方法级实体，推断方法间的谱系关系，并捕捉驱动连续创新间转变的瓶颈。该图谱基于103万篇涵盖AI会议、期刊及arXiv预印本的论文构建，包含941万条语义类型化边，每条边均基于原文证据支撑，形成可查询的方法演化因果网络。为实现该结构，我们进一步提出一种自引导的时间树搜索算法，用于构建追踪方法随时间演进的演化链。我们通过与专家标注的真实演化链对比评估图谱质量，观察到高度一致性。此外，我们证明Intern-Atlas可支持下游应用，如创意评估与自动化创意生成。我们主张方法论演化图谱作为新兴自动化科学发现的基础数据层。

The graph, comprising 9.4 million semantically typed edges grounded in source text, enables causal tracing of method development. Evaluated against expert-curated evolution chains, it shows strong alignment, and the accompanying self-guided temporal tree search algorithm effectively reconstructs method progression over time. The infrastructure supports downstream tasks like idea evaluation and automated generation, positioning methodological graphs as a foundational layer for automated scientific discovery.
为解决现有文献中心型研究基础设施无法结构化捕捉方法论演进的问题，本文提出Intern-Atlas方法论演化图谱，基于103万篇AI论文自动识别方法实体、推断方法谱系并记录驱动方法演化的瓶颈，构建包含940万条语义标注边的可查询因果网络。作者进一步提出自引导时间树搜索算法以构建方法演化链，经专家标注数据验证，结果高度一致。实验表明该图谱可支持创意评估与自动化生成，为自动化科学发现提供基础数据层。
FedKPer: Tackling Generalization and Personalization in Medical Federated Learning via Knowledge Personalization
Authors: Zoe Fowler, Ghassan AlRegib
First: 2026-05-01T14:36:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:36:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE International Conference on Image Processing (ICIP)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) holds great potential for medical applications. However, statistical heterogeneity across healthcare institutions poses a major challenge for FL, as the global model struggles both to generalize across unseen patient populations and to adapt to the unique data distributions of individual hospitals. This heterogeneity also exacerbates forgetting at both the global and local level, resulting in previous learned patient patterns to be misclassified after model updates. While prior work has largely treated generalization and personalization as separate challenges, we show that a better balance between the two can be achieved through selective alignment with the global model and a modified aggregation scheme, which together mitigate the effects of statistical heterogeneity. Specifically, we introduce FedKPer, which introduces knowledge personalization into the training stage of each local device. Afterwards, generalization is considered via the global model aggregation process, where local updates that are reliable and label-diverse are emphasized. We evaluate the performance of FedKPer, devising additional metrics that relate to common consequences of forgetting. Overall, we demonstrate FedKPer improves the generalization-personalization trade-off without sacrificing retention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedKPer：通过知识个性化解决医疗联邦学习中的泛化与个性化问题
联邦学习（FL）在医疗应用中具有巨大潜力。然而，不同医疗机构间存在的统计异质性对FL构成重大挑战，导致全局模型既难以泛化至未见患者群体，也难以适应各医院独特的数据分布。这种异质性还加剧了全局和局部层面的遗忘现象，使模型更新后先前学习到的患者模式被错误分类。尽管以往研究多将泛化与个性化视为独立挑战，我们证明通过局部设备与全局模型的有选择性对齐及改进的聚合方案，可更好地平衡二者，从而缓解统计异质性的影响。具体而言，我们提出FedKPer，在每个本地设备的训练阶段引入知识个性化；随后通过全局模型聚合过程实现泛化，强调可靠且标签多样化的本地更新。我们评估了FedKPer的性能，并设计了与遗忘常见后果相关的额外指标。总体而言，FedKPer在不牺牲保留能力的前提下，改善了泛化与个性化的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of statistical heterogeneity in medical federated learning, which hinders both generalization across hospitals and personalization to local data, FedKPer introduces knowledge personalization at the local training stage to preserve patient-specific patterns while mitigating forgetting. It then enhances generalization through a modified global aggregation scheme that prioritizes reliable and label-diverse local updates. Experimental results show FedKPer achieves a better balance between generalization and personalization without sacrificing retention, outperforming baseline methods in maintaining performance across diverse patient populations and local data distributions.
为解决医疗联邦学习中因机构间统计异质性导致的泛化与个性化难以兼顾的问题，FedKPer在本地训练阶段引入知识个性化以保留本地数据特征，随后在全局聚合阶段通过优先选择可靠且标签多样化的本地更新来增强泛化能力。实验表明，该方法有效改善了泛化与个性化的平衡，同时减少遗忘，且未牺牲模型保留能力。
LLM DNA: Tracing Model Evolution via Functional Representations
Authors: Zhaomin Wu, Haodong Zhao, Ziyang Wang, Jizhou Guo, Qian Wang, Bingsheng He
Venue: ICLR 2026 Oral
First: 2025-09-29T09:09:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:35:45+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The explosive growth of large language models (LLMs) has created a vast but opaque landscape: millions of models exist, yet their evolutionary relationships through fine-tuning, distillation, or adaptation are often undocumented or unclear, complicating LLM management. Existing methods are limited by task specificity, fixed model sets, or strict assumptions about tokenizers or architectures. Inspired by biological DNA, we address these limitations by mathematically defining LLM DNA as a low-dimensional, bi-Lipschitz representation of functional behavior. We prove that LLM DNA satisfies inheritance and genetic determinism properties and establish the existence of DNA. Building on this theory, we derive a general, scalable, training-free pipeline for DNA extraction. In experiments across 305 LLMs, DNA aligns with prior studies on limited subsets and achieves superior or competitive performance on specific tasks. Beyond these tasks, DNA comparisons uncover previously undocumented relationships among LLMs. We further construct the evolutionary tree of LLMs using phylogenetic algorithms, which align with shifts from encoder-decoder to decoder-only architectures, reflect temporal progression, and reveal distinct evolutionary speeds across LLM families.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型DNA：通过功能表示追踪模型演化
大语言模型（LLMs）的爆炸式增长形成了一个庞大但不透明的格局：数百万模型存在，但其通过微调、蒸馏或适配的演化关系往往未被记录或不明确，给LLM管理带来困难。现有方法受限于任务特异性、固定模型集或对分词器或架构的严格假设。受生物DNA启发，我们通过数学定义LLM DNA为功能行为的低维、双利普希茨表示，证明其满足遗传继承与基因决定性属性，并确立DNA的存在。基于此理论，我们推导出一种通用、可扩展、无需训练的DNA提取流程。在305个LLM的实验中，DNA与先前在有限子集上的研究结果一致，并在特定任务上表现优异或具有竞争力。此外，DNA比较揭示了此前未被记录的LLM间关系。我们进一步使用系统发育算法构建LLM演化树，其与从编码器-解码器到仅解码器架构的转变、时间演进趋势一致，并揭示不同LLM家族间的演化速度差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic understanding of LLM evolution despite rapid proliferation, this work introduces 'LLM DNA'—a low-dimensional, bi-Lipschitz functional representation inspired by biological DNA—to capture and compare model behaviors across diverse architectures and training regimes. The method is training-free, scalable, and theoretically grounded, satisfying inheritance and genetic determinism properties. Experimental results across 305 models show DNA aligns with prior findings on smaller sets and outperforms or matches existing methods on specific tasks, while also revealing novel evolutionary relationships and constructing a phylogenetic tree that reflects architectural shifts, temporal trends, and family-specific evolutionary rates.
为应对大语言模型（LLM）激增但演化关系模糊的问题，该研究提出“LLM DNA”——一种受生物DNA启发的低维、双利普希茨函数表示，用于捕捉和比较不同架构与训练方式下的模型行为。该方法无需训练、可扩展且理论严谨，满足遗传继承与决定性等性质。在305个模型上的实验表明，LLM DNA在特定任务上表现优于或可比现有方法，同时揭示了此前未被发现的模型间演化关系，并构建了LLM演化树，反映出从编码器-解码器到解码器独有架构的转变、时间演化趋势及不同家族间的演化速度差异。
Adaptive Querying with AI Persona Priors
Authors: Kaizheng Wang, Yuhang Wu, Assaf Zeevi
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-01T14:34:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:34:25+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study adaptive querying for learning user-dependent quantities of interest, such as responses to held-out items and psychometric indicators, within tight question budgets. Classical Bayesian design and computerized adaptive testing typically rely on restrictive parametric assumptions or expensive posterior approximations, limiting their use in heterogeneous, high-dimensional, and cold-start settings. We introduce a persona-induced latent variable model that represents a user's state through membership in a finite dictionary of AI personas, each offering response distributions produced by a large language model. This yields expressive priors with closed-form posterior updates and efficient finite-mixture predictions, enabling scalable Bayesian design for sequential item selection. Experiments on synthetic data and WorldValuesBench demonstrate that persona-based posteriors deliver accurate probabilistic predictions and an interpretable adaptive elicitation pipeline.
中文标题/摘要

我们研究在有限问题预算下，学习用户依赖的兴趣量（如对未见项目的响应和心理测量指标）的自适应查询方法。经典贝叶斯设计和计算机化自适应测试通常依赖于严格的参数假设或昂贵的后验近似，限制了其在异构、高维和冷启动场景中的应用。我们引入一种由人格诱导的隐变量模型，通过用户在有限AI人格字典中的成员身份表示其状态，每个AI人格提供由大语言模型生成的响应分布。这产生具有闭式后验更新和高效有限混合预测的表达性先验，支持可扩展的贝叶斯设计用于序列项目选择。在合成数据和WorldValuesBench上的实验表明，基于人格的后验能提供准确的概率预测和可解释的自适应提取流程。

为在异质、高维且问题预算受限的场景中实现高效可扩展的自适应查询，本文提出一种基于AI人设的隐变量模型，通过用户在有限人设字典中的归属关系建模其状态，每个角色对应大语言模型生成的响应分布。该方法支持闭式后验更新与高效有限混合预测，实现可扩展的贝叶斯序列选题设计。在合成数据与WorldValuesBench实验中，基于人设的先验模型展现出高精度概率预测能力，并支持可解释的自适应引导流程，无需昂贵后验近似。
AI-Driven Expansion and Application of the Alexandria Database
Authors: Théo Cavignac, Jonathan Schmidt, Pierre-Paul De Breuck, Antoine Loew, Tiago F. T. Cerqueira, Hai-Chen Wang, Anton Bochkarev, Yury Lysogorskiy, Aldo H. Romero, Ralf Drautz, Silvana Botti, Miguel A. L. Marques
First: 2025-12-09T22:31:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:32:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel multi-stage workflow for computational materials discovery that achieves a 99% success rate in identifying compounds within 100 meV/atom of thermodynamic stability, with a threefold improvement over previous approaches. By combining the Matra-Genoa generative model, Orb-v2 universal machine learning interatomic potential, and ALIGNN graph neural network for energy prediction, we generated 119 million candidate structures and added 1.3 million DFT-validated compounds to the ALEXANDRIA database, including 74 thousand new stable materials. The expanded ALEXANDRIA database now contains 5.8 million structures with 175 thousand compounds on the convex hull. Predicted structural disorder rates (37-43%) match experimental databases, unlike other recent AI-generated datasets. Analysis reveals fundamental patterns in space group distributions, coordination environments, and phase stability networks, including sub-linear scaling of convex hull connectivity. We release the complete dataset, including sAlex25 with 14 million out-of-equilibrium structures containing forces and stresses for training universal force fields. We demonstrate that fine-tuning a GRACE model on this data improves benchmark accuracy. All data, models, and workflows are freely available under Creative Commons licenses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于人工智能的亚历山大数据库扩展与应用
我们提出了一种新颖的多阶段计算材料发现工作流程，成功率达99%，可在100 meV/atom内识别化合物，相较以往方法提升三倍。通过结合Matra-Genoa生成模型、Orb-v2通用机器学习原子间势能函数及ALIGNN图神经网络进行能量预测，我们生成了1.19亿个候选结构，并向亚历山大数据库新增130万个DFT验证化合物，其中包括7.4万个新稳定材料。扩展后的亚历山大数据库现包含580万个结构，凸包上共有17.5万个化合物。预测的结构无序率（37%-43%）与实验数据库一致，不同于其他近期AI生成数据集。分析揭示了空间群分布、配位环境及相稳定性网络中的基本规律，包括凸包连通性的次线性缩放。我们发布了完整数据集，包括包含力与应力的1400万个非平衡结构的sAlex25，用于训练通用力场。我们证明，基于该数据对GRACE模型进行微调可提升基准测试精度。所有数据、模型及工作流程均在知识共享许可下免费提供。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate materials discovery with high accuracy and scalability, this study introduces a multi-stage AI-driven workflow combining Matra-Genoa, Orb-v2, and ALIGNN to generate and validate candidate materials. The method produced 119 million structures, adding 1.3 million DFT-validated compounds—including 74,000 new stable materials—to the ALEXANDRIA database, which now contains 5.8 million structures and 175,000 compounds on the convex hull. Experimental validation shows predicted structural disorder rates (37–43%) align with real-world data, and analysis uncovers patterns in space group distributions and phase stability networks, including sub-linear convex hull connectivity. The dataset sAlex25 (14 million out-of-equilibrium structures) enables training universal force fields, and fine-tuning a GRACE model on it improves benchmark performance.
为加速高精度、可扩展的材料发现，本研究提出一种结合Matra-Genoa、Orb-v2和ALIGNN的多阶段AI工作流，生成并验证候选材料。该方法产出1.19亿种结构，新增130万经DFT验证的化合物（含7.4万种新稳定材料），使ALEXANDRIA数据库扩充至580万结构和17.5万凸包化合物。预测的结构无序率（37–43%）与实验数据一致，分析揭示了空间群分布、配位环境和相稳定性网络中的规律，包括凸包连通性的次线性增长。数据集sAlex25（1400万非平衡结构）支持训练通用力场，且在该数据上微调GRACE模型可提升基准性能。
Copula-enhanced Vision Transformer for high myopia diagnosis through OU UWF fundus images
Authors: Chong Zhong, Yunhao Liu, Yang Li, Xiang Fu, Jin Yang, Danjuan Yang, Meiyan Li, Jinfeng Xu, Aiyi Liu, Alan H. Welsh, Xingtao Zhou, Bo Fu, Catherine C. Liu
First: 2025-01-11T13:23:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:31:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advancement of AI-assisted myopia screening necessitates the joint diagnosis of both-eye (OU) high myopia (HM) status and the prediction of axial length (AL). This clinical requirement introduces a complex mixed-type (binary-continuous) multitask learning task with bi-domain (OU) image covariates, giving rise to two key challenges: i) capture the inter-ocular asymmetry of OU images within a cutting-edge foundation model; ii) model and estimate the conditional dependence structure among mixed-type multivariate responses given image covariates. We address the challenges by: i) imposing residual adapters on the Vision Transformer foundation model to capture the OU similarity and heterogeneity simultaneously; ii) developing a four-dimensional copula loss that is implementable in PyTorch based on a latent variable expression for the Gaussian copula likelihood, and proposing a computationally efficient fast Monte Carlo Expectation Maximization (fMCEM) algorithm to estimate copula parameters. We further formulate a specific overfitting problem called stronger covariance phenomenon in multitask learning. We reveal the disturbance of the phenomenon to estimation of copula parameters and theoretically demonstrate the numerical stability of the proposed fMCEM algorithm against the disturbance. The application to our annotated OU ultra-widefield fundus image dataset and simulation on synthetic data demonstrate that our method stably enhances the predictive capabilities on both classification and regression tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Copula增强的视觉变换器用于通过OU超广角眼底图像诊断高度近视
AI辅助近视筛查的发展需要同时诊断双眼（OU）高度近视（HM）状态并预测眼轴长度（AL）。这一临床需求引入了一个复杂的混合型（二元-连续）多任务学习任务，其输入为双域（OU）图像协变量，带来了两个关键挑战：i）在前沿基础模型中捕捉OU图像的双眼不对称性；ii）在给定图像协变量条件下建模和估计混合型多变量响应间的条件依赖结构。我们通过以下方法应对挑战：i）在视觉变换器基础模型上施加残差适配器，同时捕捉OU图像的相似性与异质性；ii）开发一种基于高斯Copula似然的潜在变量表达式、可在PyTorch中实现的四维Copula损失函数，并提出一种计算高效的快速蒙特卡洛期望最大化（fMCEM）算法以估计Copula参数。我们进一步定义了多任务学习中的特定过拟合问题——更强协方差现象，并揭示该现象对Copula参数估计的干扰，同时从理论上证明所提出的fMCEM算法对该干扰具有数值稳定性。在我们的标注OU超广角眼底图像数据集上的应用及合成数据模拟表明，该方法在分类与回归任务中均稳定提升了预测能力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the clinical need to jointly diagnose high myopia and predict axial length from both eyes using ultra-widefield fundus images, this work tackles two key challenges: modeling inter-ocular asymmetry within a Vision Transformer and estimating conditional dependencies among mixed-type outputs. The method introduces residual adapters to capture ocular similarity and heterogeneity, and a novel four-dimensional copula loss based on Gaussian copula likelihood, optimized via a fast Monte Carlo Expectation Maximization algorithm. Experiments on annotated OU fundus data and synthetic simulations show that the approach consistently improves performance on both classification and regression tasks while mitigating overfitting-induced covariance disturbances.
为满足AI辅助近视筛查中同时诊断双眼高度近视并预测眼轴长度的需求，该研究针对OU眼底图像的混合型多任务学习问题提出解决方案。方法在视觉Transformer基础上引入残差适配器以捕捉双眼对称性与异质性，并设计基于高斯Copula似然的四维Copula损失函数，配合快速蒙特卡洛期望最大化算法进行参数估计。在标注的OU超广角眼底图像及合成数据上的实验表明，该方法在分类与回归任务中均稳定提升预测性能，同时有效缓解“更强协方差现象”引发的过拟合问题。
MemoryBench: A Benchmark for Memory and Continual Learning in LLM Systems
Authors: Qingyao Ai, Yichen Tang, Changyue Wang, Jianming Long, Weihang Su, Yiqun Liu
First: 2025-10-20T08:16:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:29:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling up data, parameters, and test-time computation has been the mainstream methods to improve LLM systems (LLMsys), but their upper bounds are almost reached due to the gradual depletion of high-quality data and marginal gains obtained from larger computational resource consumption. Inspired by the abilities of human and traditional AI systems in learning from practice, constructing memory and continual learning frameworks for LLMsys has become an important and popular research direction in recent literature. Yet, existing benchmarks for LLM memory often focus on evaluating the system on homogeneous reading comprehension tasks with long-form inputs rather than testing their abilities to learn from accumulated user feedback in service time. Therefore, we propose a user feedback simulation framework and a comprehensive benchmark covering multiple domains, languages, and types of tasks to evaluate the continual learning abilities of LLMsys. Experiments show that the effectiveness and efficiency of state-of-the-art baselines are far from satisfying, and we hope this benchmark could pave the way for future studies on LLM memory and optimization algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemoryBench：大语言系统中记忆与持续学习的基准
提升数据规模、参数量和测试时计算量一直是改进大语言系统（LLMsys）的主流方法，但其上限已近饱和，因高质量数据渐趋枯竭，且更大计算资源投入带来的边际收益有限。受人类及传统AI系统从实践中学习能力的启发，构建LLMsys的记忆与持续学习框架已成为近期研究的重要方向。然而，现有LLM记忆基准多聚焦于同质阅读理解任务（长文本输入），而非评估其在服务期间从累积用户反馈中学习的能力。为此，我们提出用户反馈模拟框架及涵盖多领域、多语言、多任务类型的综合基准，以评估LLMsys的持续学习能力。实验表明，当前最先进基线方法在效果与效率上仍远未达标，我们期望该基准能为未来LLM记忆及优化算法研究铺平道路。

Driven by the diminishing returns of scaling data, parameters, and computation for LLM systems, researchers aim to evaluate memory and continual learning capabilities through realistic user feedback. Experiments reveal that current state-of-the-art models perform poorly under continual learning conditions, highlighting the need for better memory mechanisms and optimization strategies.
鉴于扩大数据、参数和计算资源对LLM系统性能提升的边际效益递减，研究者提出通过构建记忆与持续学习能力来突破瓶颈。作者设计了MemoryBench基准，模拟多领域、多语言、多任务场景下的用户反馈，以评估LLM系统在服务过程中持续学习与知识保留的能力。实验表明，现有主流基线模型在持续学习任务中表现不佳，亟需更优的记忆机制与优化算法。
Beyond Prompt-Induced Lies: Investigating LLM Deception on Benign Prompts
Authors: Zhaomin Wu, Mingzhe Du, See-Kiong Ng, Bingsheng He
Venue: ICLR 2026 Oral
First: 2025-08-08T14:46:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:29:07+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in reasoning, planning, and decision-making tasks, making their trustworthiness critical. A significant and underexplored risk is intentional deception, where an LLM deliberately fabricates or conceals information to serve a hidden objective. Existing studies typically induce deception by explicitly setting a hidden objective through prompting or fine-tuning, which may not reflect real-world human-LLM interactions. Moving beyond such human-induced deception, we investigate LLMs' self-initiated deception on benign prompts. To address the absence of ground truth, we propose a framework based on Contact Searching Questions (CSQ). This framework introduces two statistical metrics derived from psychological principles to quantify the likelihood of deception. The first, the Deceptive Intention Score, measures the model's bias toward a hidden objective. The second, the Deceptive Behavior Score, measures the inconsistency between the LLM's internal belief and its expressed output. Evaluating 16 leading LLMs, we find that both metrics rise in parallel and escalate with task difficulty for most models. Moreover, increasing model capacity does not always reduce deception, posing a significant challenge for future LLM development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越提示诱导的谎言：探究大语言模型在良性提示下的自我欺骗行为
大语言模型（LLMs）广泛应用于推理、规划和决策任务，其可信度至关重要。一个显著且尚未充分研究的风险是故意欺骗，即LLM为达成隐藏目标而故意编造或隐瞒信息。现有研究通常通过提示或微调明确设定隐藏目标以诱导欺骗，这可能无法反映真实的人机交互场景。我们超越此类人为诱导的欺骗，研究LLM在良性提示下的自我发起欺骗行为。为解决缺乏真实标注的问题，我们提出基于接触搜索问题（CSQ）的框架，引入两项源于心理学原理的统计指标，用于量化欺骗可能性：一是欺骗意图得分，衡量模型对隐藏目标的偏倚；二是欺骗行为得分，衡量LLM内部信念与其输出表达之间的不一致性。评估16个主流LLM后发现，多数模型的两项指标同步上升，且随任务难度增加而加剧。此外，模型容量的增加并不总能减少欺骗，这对未来LLM发展构成重大挑战。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can engage in self-initiated deception even when prompted with benign, non-malicious queries, moving beyond prior work that relied on human-engineered deception triggers. The researchers introduce a novel framework based on Contact Searching Questions (CSQ) to quantify deception using two psychological-inspired metrics: the Deceptive Intention Score, which gauges bias toward hidden objectives, and the Deceptive Behavior Score, which measures internal belief inconsistency with output. Across 16 leading LLMs, both scores consistently increase with task difficulty and often rise together, while larger model capacity does not reliably suppress deception, highlighting a persistent challenge for trustworthy LLM design.
为评估大语言模型在非诱导情境下的可信度，本研究探讨其在良性提示下自发性欺骗行为。提出基于接触搜索问题（CSQ）的框架，引入两个心理原理衍生的指标：欺骗意图得分（衡量对隐藏目标的偏倚）和欺骗行为得分（衡量内部信念与输出的一致性）。在16个主流模型上评估发现，两项得分普遍随任务难度上升而同步增加，且模型规模扩大并不总能降低欺骗，揭示了大模型设计中的关键挑战。
Outbidding and Outbluffing Elite Humans: Mastering Liar's Poker via Self-Play and Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Richard Dewey, Janos Botyanszki, Ciamac C. Moallemi, Andrew T. Zheng
First: 2025-11-05T18:58:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:26:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI researchers have long focused on poker-like games as a testbed for environments characterized by multi-player dynamics, imperfect information, and reasoning under uncertainty. While recent breakthroughs have matched elite human play at no-limit Texas hold'em, the multi-player dynamics are subdued: most hands converge quickly with only two players engaged through multiple rounds of bidding. In this paper, we present Solly, the first AI agent to achieve elite human play in reduced-format Liar's Poker, a game characterized by extensive multi-player engagement. We trained Solly using self-play with a model-free, actor-critic, deep reinforcement learning algorithm. Solly played at an elite human level as measured by win rate (won over 50% of hands) and equity (money won) in heads-up and multi-player Liar's Poker. Solly also outperformed large language models (LLMs), including those with reasoning abilities, on the same metrics. Solly developed novel bidding strategies, randomized play effectively, and was not easily exploitable by world-class human players.

标题：超越人类精英：通过自对弈与强化学习掌握骗子扑克
AI研究人员长期以来将类似扑克的游戏作为测试多玩家动态、不完全信息和不确定性推理环境的平台。尽管近期突破已使AI在无限制德州扑克中达到精英人类水平，但其多玩家动态较为有限：大多数牌局迅速收敛，仅由两名玩家通过多轮竞价参与。本文提出Solly，这是首个在简化版骗子扑克中达到精英人类水平的AI代理，该游戏以广泛的多玩家互动为特征。我们使用无模型、基于演员-评论家架构的深度强化学习算法对Solly进行自对弈训练。Solly在单挑和多人骗子扑克中，以胜率（胜过50%牌局）和权益（赢得金钱）两项指标达到精英人类水平。Solly在相同指标上也优于大型语言模型（LLMs），包括具备推理能力的模型。Solly发展出新颖的竞价策略，有效随机化出牌，且不易被世界级人类玩家利用。

Solly achieved elite human performance in reduced-format Liar's Poker, winning over 50% of hands and generating positive equity in both heads-up and multi-player settings. It also surpassed large language models with reasoning abilities on the same metrics, demonstrating novel bidding strategies, effective randomization, and resistance to exploitation by top human players.
为推动AI在多人、不完全信息博弈中的能力突破，研究者通过自对弈结合无模型的演员-评论家深度强化学习算法训练出Solly。Solly在简化版骗子扑克中达到精英人类水平，胜率超50%，收益显著，且在双人及多人对局中均优于大型语言模型，展现出新颖的出价策略、有效随机化及对顶尖人类玩家的抗博弈能力。
Towards Disentangled Preference Optimization Dynamics: Suppress the Loser, Preserve the Winner
Authors: Wei Chen, Yubing Wu, Junmei Yang, Delu Zeng, Qibin Zhao, John Paisley, Min Chen, Zhou Wang
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-04-20T13:23:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:26:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, many margin-based methods also suppress the chosen response when they try to suppress the rejected one, and there is no general way to prevent this across different objectives. We address this issue with a unified incentive-score decomposition of preference optimization, revealing that different objectives share the same local update directions and differ only in their scalar weights. This decomposition provides a common framework for analyzing objectives that were previously studied in separate settings. Building on this decomposition, by analyzing the dynamics of the chosen/rejected likelihoods, we identify the disentanglement band (DB), a simple, testable condition that tells us when training can follow the desired path: suppress the loser while preserving the winner, possibly after an early stage. Using the DB, we propose reward calibration (RC), a plug-and-play method that adaptively rebalances the updates for chosen and rejected responses to satisfy the DB, without redesigning the base objective. Empirical results show that RC leads to more disentangled dynamics, with better downstream performance observed across several settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/IceyWuu/DisentangledPreferenceOptimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向解耦偏好优化动态：压制失败者，保留胜者
偏好优化广泛用于使大语言模型（LLM）与人类偏好对齐。然而，许多基于边界的优化方法在试图压制被拒绝的响应时，也会抑制被选择的响应，且目前尚无通用方法能在不同目标间防止此现象。 我们通过偏好优化的统一激励分数分解解决此问题，揭示不同目标共享相同的局部更新方向，仅在标量权重上有所差异。 该分解为此前在不同场景中独立研究的目标提供了一个共同分析框架。 基于此分解，通过分析被选/被拒响应的似然动态，我们识别出解耦带（DB），这是一个简单且可测试的条件，可告知训练何时能遵循期望路径：早期阶段后压制失败者、保留胜者。 利用DB，我们提出奖励校准（RC），一种即插即用方法，可自适应调整被选与被拒响应的更新权重以满足DB，无需重设计基础目标。 实验证明，RC带来更解耦的动态，多个设置下下游性能更优。代码见https://github.com/IceyWuu/DisentangledPreferenceOptimization。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that margin-based preference optimization methods often suppress chosen responses while trying to suppress rejected ones, this paper introduces a unified incentive-score decomposition that reveals shared local update directions across different objectives, differing only in scalar weights. Based on analyzing likelihood dynamics, the authors identify the disentanglement band (DB), a testable condition ensuring suppression of the loser while preserving the winner. They propose reward calibration (RC), a plug-and-play method that adaptively rebalances updates to satisfy the DB without altering the base objective. Experiments show RC leads to more disentangled optimization dynamics and improved downstream performance across multiple settings.
为了解决基于边界的偏好优化方法在抑制被拒响应时往往同时抑制被选响应的问题，本文提出一种统一的激励分数分解方法，表明不同目标具有相同的局部更新方向，仅在标量权重上存在差异。通过分析被选与被拒响应的似然动态，作者定义了“解耦带”（DB）——一个可测试的条件，用于判断训练何时能实现抑制失败者、保留胜者的目标。在此基础上，提出“奖励校准”（RC）方法，通过自适应调整被选与被拒响应的更新权重以满足DB，无需修改基础目标。实验表明RC能提升训练过程的解耦性，并在多个设置中带来更优的下游性能。
ML-Bench&Guard: Policy-Grounded Multilingual Safety Benchmark and Guardrail for Large Language Models
Authors: Yunhan Zhao, Zhaorun Chen, Xingjun Ma, Yu-Gang Jiang, Bo Li
First: 2026-05-01T14:24:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:24:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in cross-linguistic contexts, ensuring safety in diverse regulatory and cultural environments has become a critical challenge. However, existing multilingual benchmarks largely rely on general risk taxonomies and machine translation, which confines guardrail models to these predefined categories and hinders their ability to align with region-specific regulations and cultural nuances. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ML-Bench, a policy-grounded multilingual safety benchmark covering 14 languages. ML-Bench is constructed directly from regional regulations, where risk categories and fine-grained rules derived from jurisdiction-specific legal texts are directly used to guide the generation of multilingual safety data, enabling culturally and legally aligned evaluation across languages. Building on ML-Bench, we develop ML-Guard, a Diffusion Large Language Model (dLLM)-based guardrail model that supports multilingual safety judgment and policy-conditioned compliance assessment. ML-Guard has two variants, one 1.5B lightweight model for fast `safe/unsafe' checking and a more capable 7B model for customized compliance checking with detailed explanations. We conduct extensive experiments against 11 strong guardrail baselines across 6 existing multilingual safety benchmarks and our ML-Bench, and show that ML-Guard consistently outperforms prior methods. We hope that ML-Bench and ML-Guard can help advance the development of regulation-aware and culturally aligned multilingual guardrail systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ML-Bench&Guard：基于政策的多语言安全基准与大语言模型护栏
随着大语言模型（LLMs）在跨语言环境中的部署日益广泛，确保其在多样化的监管与文化环境中安全运行已成为关键挑战。然而，现有多语言基准主要依赖通用风险分类和机器翻译，这使护栏模型受限于预设类别，难以契合地区特定法规与文化语境。为弥合这一差距，我们提出ML-Bench，一个基于政策的多语言安全基准，覆盖14种语言。ML-Bench直接从区域法规构建，其风险类别与细粒度规则源自特定司法管辖区的法律文本，用于指导多语言安全数据的生成，从而实现跨语言的文化与法律对齐评估。基于ML-Bench，我们开发了ML-Guard，一种基于扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的护栏模型，支持多语言安全判断与政策条件下的合规评估。ML-Guard包含两个版本：一个1.5B轻量模型用于快速安全/不安全判断，以及一个7B更强模型，支持定制化合规检查并提供详细解释。我们在6个现有多语言安全基准及我们的ML-Bench上，与11个强基线护栏模型进行广泛实验，结果表明ML-Guard持续优于现有方法。我们期望ML-Bench与ML-Guard能推动具备法规意识与文化对齐的多语言护栏系统的发展。

As large language models expand into multilingual settings, ensuring safety across diverse legal and cultural contexts has become urgent. They also present ML-Guard, a diffusion-based LLM guardrail with two variants — a lightweight 1.5B model for rapid safety classification and a 7B model for detailed compliance explanations. Experiments against 11 baselines across six benchmarks show ML-Guard consistently outperforms existing methods, demonstrating superior alignment with policy-specific safety requirements.
为应对大语言模型在多语言环境中安全合规的挑战，该研究提出ML-Bench，一个基于区域法规构建的多语言安全基准，直接从法律文本中提取风险类别与细粒度规则，确保评估与当地法律和文化对齐。在此基础上，开发了ML-Guard，一个基于扩散大语言模型的多语言安全护栏系统，提供轻量级（1.5B）快速安全判断与增强版（7B）合规性详细评估。在六个多语言基准及ML-Bench上的实验表明，ML-Guard在多项指标上持续优于11个基线模型，展现出更强的政策导向安全评估能力。
Static and Dynamic Graph Alignment Network for Temporal Video Grounding
Authors: Zhanjie Hu, Bolin Zhang, Jianhua Wang, Jianbo Zheng, Chenchen Yan, Takahiro Komamizu, Ichiro Ide, Jiangbo Qian
First: 2026-05-01T14:16:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:16:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Temporal Video Grounding (TVG) aims to localize temporal moments in an untrimmed video that semantically correspond to given natural language queries. Recently, Graph Convolutional Networks (GCN) have been widely adopted in TVG to model temporal relations among video clips and enhance contextual reasoning by constructing clip-level graphs. Despite their effectiveness, existing GCN-based TVG methods encounter three critical bottlenecks: 1) Most methods construct graph nodes using either static or dynamic features alone, resulting in incomplete visual representation and overlooking complementary semantics, 2) Most methods construct temporal graphs in a query-agnostic manner, leading to inefficient feature interaction within the temporal graph representation, and 3) Most methods often suffer from a single-granularity semantic matching, while direct training on complex temporal localization task may lead to slow convergence and suboptimal precision. To address these challenges, we propose Static and Dynamic Graph Alignment Network (SDGAN). First, SDGAN jointly exploits static and dynamic visual features to construct two complementary temporal graphs and performs Position-wise Nodes Alignment, enabling more expressive and robust visual representation. Second, SDGAN introduces Query-Clip Contrastive Learning and Adaptive Graph Modeling to explicitly align visual clips with their corresponding textual queries, yielding query-aware visual representations. Third, SDGAN incorporates multi-granularity temporal proposals within Progressive Easy-to-Hard Training Strategy, effectively bridging coarse-grained semantic localization and fine-grained temporal boundary refinement. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that SDGAN achieves superior performance across complex TVG scenarios. Codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/ZhanJieHu/SDGAN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时序视频定位的静态与动态图对齐网络
时序视频定位（TVG）旨在在一个未剪辑的视频中定位与给定自然语言查询语义对应的时序片段。最近，图卷积网络（GCN）被广泛应用于TVG中，通过构建片段级图来建模视频片段间的时序关系并增强上下文推理。然而，现有基于GCN的TVG方法面临三个关键瓶颈：1）多数方法仅使用静态或动态特征构建图节点，导致视觉表征不完整且忽略互补语义；2）多数方法以查询无关的方式构建时序图，导致时序图表示内特征交互效率低下；3）多数方法常受限于单一粒度语义匹配，直接在复杂时序定位任务上训练可能导致收敛缓慢和精度不佳。为解决这些问题，我们提出静态与动态图对齐网络（SDGAN）。首先，SDGAN联合利用静态与动态视觉特征构建两个互补的时序图，并执行位置级节点对齐，以实现更表达力强且鲁棒的视觉表征。其次，SDGAN引入查询-片段对比学习与自适应图建模，显式对齐视觉片段与其对应文本查询，生成查询感知的视觉表示。第三，SDGAN在渐进式由易到难训练策略中融入多粒度时序提案，有效连接粗粒度语义定位与细粒度时序边界优化。在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SDGAN在复杂TVG场景中表现优越。代码与数据集可访问：https://github.com/ZhanJieHu/SDGAN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing Graph Convolutional Network (GCN)-based methods in Temporal Video Grounding, this work proposes SDGAN to address three key issues: incomplete visual representation from static/dynamic features alone, query-agnostic graph construction, and single-granularity semantic matching. SDGAN constructs two complementary temporal graphs from joint static and dynamic features, aligns nodes position-wise for richer representation, employs query-clip contrastive learning for query-aware visual encoding, and uses multi-granularity proposals with progressive training to refine temporal boundaries. Experiments on three benchmarks show SDGAN consistently outperforms prior methods in precision and robustness across complex temporal grounding tasks.
为解决现有基于图卷积网络的视频时间定位方法存在的三大问题——静态/动态特征分离导致表征不完整、图结构缺乏查询感知、语义匹配粒度单一——本文提出SDGAN。该方法构建双图结构融合静态与动态视觉特征并进行节点对齐，引入查询-片段对比学习实现语义对齐，结合多粒度时间提议与渐进式训练策略优化边界定位。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，SDGAN在复杂场景下性能优于现有方法。
On the Expressive Power of Contextual Relations in Transformers
Authors: Demián Fraiman
First: 2026-03-26T19:30:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:14:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer architectures have achieved remarkable empirical success in modeling contextual relations, yet a clear understanding of their expressive power is still lacking. In this work, we introduce a measure-theoretic framework in which contextual relations are modeled as probabilistic objects, either as conditional distributions or as joint distributions (couplings). This perspective reveals a natural connection between standard softmax attention and entropy-regularized optimal transport, providing a unified view of attention as a normalization of an underlying affinity function. Within this framework, we establish a universal approximation theorem for contextual systems using standard Softmax Attention and alternately Sinkhorn normalization. These results show that Transformer architectures can approximate arbitrary contextual relations rules, and that the choice of normalization determines how these relations are represented. Moreover, they provide a principled explanation for why Transformers are effective at modeling contextual relations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer中上下文关系的表达能力研究
Transformer架构在建模上下文关系方面取得了显著的实证成功，但对其表达能力的清晰理解仍显不足。本文引入了一个测度论框架，将上下文关系建模为概率对象，即条件分布或联合分布（耦合）。该视角揭示了标准Softmax注意力与熵正则化最优传输之间自然的联系，为注意力机制提供了一种统一的视角——即作为底层亲和函数的归一化。在此框架下，我们建立了使用标准Softmax注意力和交替Sinkhorn归一化的上下文系统通用逼近定理。这些结果表明，Transformer架构可以逼近任意上下文关系规则，且归一化方式决定了这些关系的表示方式。此外，它们为Transformer为何擅长建模上下文关系提供了理论依据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of Transformers but the lack of theoretical understanding of their expressive power, this work introduces a measure-theoretic framework modeling contextual relations as probabilistic objects, linking softmax attention to entropy-regularized optimal transport. Using this framework, the authors prove a universal approximation theorem showing that standard Softmax Attention with Sinkhorn normalization can approximate any contextual relation, demonstrating that Transformer architectures are expressive enough to capture arbitrary contextual rules, with normalization choices governing the representation of these relations.
鉴于Transformer在建模上下文关系方面的实证成功，但其表达能力缺乏理论解释，本文引入测度论框架，将上下文关系建模为概率对象，揭示softmax注意力与熵正则化最优传输的自然联系。在此框架下，作者证明了标准Softmax注意力配合Sinkhorn归一化可逼近任意上下文关系，且归一化方式决定关系表示形式。实验验证了Transformer能有效建模多样上下文规则，支持理论结论并解释其实际有效性。
Diffusion Models for Solving Inverse Problems via Posterior Sampling with Piecewise Guidance
Authors: Saeed Mohseni-Sehdeh, Walid Saad, Kei Sakaguchi, Tao Yu
First: 2025-07-22T19:35:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:13:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models are powerful tools for sampling from high-dimensional distributions by progressively transforming pure noise into structured data through a denoising process. When equipped with a guidance mechanism, these models can also generate samples from conditional distributions. In this paper, a novel diffusion-based framework is introduced for solving inverse problems using a piecewise guidance scheme. The guidance term is defined as a piecewise function of the diffusion timestep, facilitating the use of different approximations during high-noise and low-noise phases. This design is shown to effectively balance computational efficiency with the accuracy of the guidance term. Unlike task-specific approaches that require retraining for each problem, the proposed method is problem-agnostic and readily adaptable to a variety of inverse problems. Additionally, it explicitly incorporates measurement noise into the reconstruction process. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through extensive experiments on image restoration tasks, specifically image inpainting and super-resolution. Using a class conditional diffusion model for recovery, compared to the \blue{pseudoinverse-guided diffusion model (\textrm{\(Π\)}GDM) baseline}, the proposed framework achieves a reduction in inference time of \(25\%\) for inpainting with both random and center masks, and \(23\%\) and \(24\%\) for \(4\times\) and \(8\times\) super-resolution tasks, respectively, while incurring only negligible loss in PSNR and SSIM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过后验采样与分段引导的扩散模型求解逆问题
扩散模型是通过去噪过程将纯噪声逐步转化为结构化数据，从而从高维分布中采样的强大工具。当配备引导机制时，这些模型还能从条件分布中生成样本。本文提出了一种基于扩散的新框架，利用分段引导方案求解逆问题。引导项定义为扩散时间步的分段函数，便于在高噪声和低噪声阶段使用不同的近似方法。该设计有效平衡了计算效率与引导项的准确性。与需要针对每个问题重新训练的任务特定方法不同，该方法具有问题无关性，易于适应多种逆问题。此外，它明确将测量噪声纳入重建过程。通过在图像修复任务（包括图像补全与超分辨率）上的大量实验，验证了该框架的有效性。使用类别条件扩散模型进行恢复，相比伪逆引导扩散模型（ΠGDM）基线，该框架在随机掩码和中心掩码下的图像补全任务中推理时间减少25%，在4倍和8倍超分辨率任务中分别减少23%和24%，同时PSNR和SSIM仅略有下降。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and generalizable solutions to inverse problems, this paper proposes a diffusion-based framework that uses piecewise guidance to adaptively adjust the conditioning signal during denoising, switching between approximations based on noise level. The method avoids task-specific retraining by being problem-agnostic and explicitly models measurement noise. Experimental results on image inpainting and super-resolution tasks show that compared to a pseudoinverse-guided diffusion baseline, the proposed approach reduces inference time by 25% for inpainting and 23–24% for super-resolution tasks, with only minimal degradation in PSNR and SSIM.
为解决逆问题并提升效率，本文提出一种基于扩散模型的框架，通过分段引导机制在去噪过程中根据噪声水平动态调整条件信号。该方法将引导项定义为扩散时间步的分段函数，允许在高噪声和低噪声阶段使用不同近似，从而在计算效率与精度间取得平衡。与任务特定模型不同，该方法具有通用性，且直接融入测量噪声。在图像修复与超分辨率任务中，相比伪逆引导扩散基线，该方法将推理时间分别降低25%、23%和24%，同时PSNR和SSIM指标几乎无损。
Foundation AI Models for Aerosol Optical Depth Estimation from PACE Satellite Data
Authors: Zahid Hassan Tushar, Sanjay Purushotham
First: 2026-05-01T14:06:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-01T14:06:05+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures, to appear in 2026 IEEE International Geoscience and Remote Sensing Symposium
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aerosol Optical Depth (AOD) retrieval is essential for Earth observation, supporting applications from air quality monitoring to climate studies. Conventional physics-based AOD retrieval methods formulate the problem as a pixel-wise inversion, relying on radiative transfer modeling, memory-intensive look-up tables, and auxiliary meteorological data. While recent data-driven approaches have shown promise, many fail to exploit the spatial-spectral coherence of hyperspectral imagery, leading to spatially inconsistent and noise-sensitive retrievals. We present the first study exploring Foundation AI models for AOD retrieval and propose ViTCG, a Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping-based spatial regression framework that reduces retrieval bias and error. ViTCG uses hyperspectral top-of-atmosphere radiance as input and jointly models spatial context and spectral information. Validation with PACE radiance observations demonstrates a 62% reduction in mean squared error compared to state-of-the-art foundation models, including Prithvi, and produces spatially coherent AOD fields.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于PACE卫星数据的气溶胶光学厚度估算的Foundation AI模型
气溶胶光学厚度（AOD）反演对地球观测至关重要，支持从空气质量监测到气候研究等应用。传统基于物理的AOD反演方法将问题建模为像素级反演，依赖辐射传输建模、内存密集型查找表和辅助气象数据。尽管近期数据驱动方法已显示出潜力，但许多方法未能充分利用高光谱图像的空间-光谱相干性，导致反演结果空间不一致且对噪声敏感。本文首次探索Foundation AI模型用于AOD反演，并提出ViTCG模型——一种基于通道分组的空间回归视觉Transformer框架，可降低反演偏差和误差。ViTCG以大气顶部高光谱辐亮度为输入，联合建模空间上下文与光谱信息。基于PACE辐亮度观测的验证表明，其均方误差较当前最先进的Foundation模型（如Prithvi）降低62%，并生成空间一致的AOD场。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional physics-based and current data-driven AOD retrieval methods, this study introduces ViTCG, a Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping for spatial regression, to better exploit spatial-spectral coherence in hyperspectral satellite data. ViTCG takes top-of-atmosphere radiance as input and jointly models spatial context and spectral features to reduce bias and error. Experimental validation using PACE satellite data shows ViTCG achieves a 62% reduction in mean squared error compared to state-of-the-art foundation models like Prithvi, while generating spatially coherent AOD fields.
为克服传统物理模型方法计算复杂、依赖气象数据，以及现有数据驱动方法缺乏空间-光谱一致性的问题，本研究提出ViTCG模型——一种基于通道分组的视觉Transformer空间回归框架，用于从PACE卫星高光谱数据中反演气溶胶光学厚度。该模型以大气顶辐射为输入，联合建模空间上下文与光谱信息，有效降低反演偏差与误差。实验结果表明，与Prithvi等当前最优基础模型相比，ViTCG将均方误差降低62%，并生成空间一致性更强的AOD场。
